function ellipse(){const scale=data.size/BASE_BOSS;return {x:titan.x,y:titan.y+data.oy,rx:data.rx*scale,ry:data.ry*scale}}
function paws(){const k=data.size/BASE_BOSS;return [{id:'L',x:titan.x-58*titan.face*k,y:titan.y+10*k,r:data.limbR*k,c:'#f28cff'}, {id:'R',x:titan.x+67*titan.face*k,y:titan.y+12*k,r:data.limbR*k,c:'#ffe17d'}]}
function rebuildNav(){navBlocked=new Uint8Array(NAV_X*NAV_Y);const e=ellipse(),pad=10;for(let gy=0;gy<NAV_Y;gy++)for(let gx=0;gx<NAV_X;gx++){let x=(gx+.5)*NAV_CELL,y=(gy+.5)*NAV_CELL;for(const r of obstacles){if(x>r.x-e.rx-pad&&x<r.x+r.w+e.rx+pad&&y>r.y-e.ry-pad&&y<r.y+r.h+e.ry+pad){navBlocked[gy*NAV_X+gx]=1;break}}}navRevision++}
function segmentPassable(ax,ay,bx,by,extra=8,raw=false){const e=ellipse(),rx=raw?2:e.rx+extra,ry=raw?2:e.ry+extra;for(const r of obstacles){const minX=r.x-rx,maxX=r.x+r.w+rx,minY=r.y-ry,maxY=r.y+r.h+ry;
 let dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,lo=0,hi=1;
 for(const [p,q] of [[-dx,ax-minX],[dx,maxX-ax],[-dy,ay-minY],[dy,maxY-ay]]){
 if(Math.abs(p)<1e-9){if(q<0){lo=2;break}}else{let v=q/p;if(p<0)lo=Math.max(lo,v);else hi=Math.min(hi,v)}}
 if(lo<=hi)return false;
}return true}
function findNearestFree(cellX,cellY){let ox=clamp(Math.floor(cellX),0,NAV_X-1),oy=clamp(Math.floor(cellY),0,NAV_Y-1),idx=oy*NAV_X+ox;if(!navBlocked[idx])return idx;
 for(let radius=1;radius<12;radius++){let best=-1,score=1e12;for(let yy=Math.max(0,oy-radius);yy<=Math.min(NAV_Y-1,oy+radius);yy++)for(let xx=Math.max(0,ox-radius);xx<=Math.min(NAV_X-1,ox+radius);xx++){if(Math.max(Math.abs(xx-ox),Math.abs(yy-oy))!==radius)continue;let v=yy*NAV_X+xx;if(navBlocked[v])continue;let s=(xx-cellX)**2+(yy-cellY)**2;if(s<score){best=v;score=s}}if(best>=0)return best}return -1}
function astar(startX,startY,goalX,goalY){let st=findNearestFree(startX/NAV_CELL,startY/NAV_CELL),go=findNearestFree(goalX/NAV_CELL,goalY/NAV_CELL);if(st<0||go<0)return null;
 const n=NAV_X*NAV_Y,cost=new Float32Array(n),prev=new Int32Array(n),closed=new Uint8Array(n);cost.fill(Infinity);prev.fill(-1);const queue=[];
 const point=id=>[id%NAV_X,Math.floor(id/NAV_X)]; const heuristic=(a,b)=>{const [ax,ay]=point(a),[bx,by]=point(b);let dx=Math.abs(ax-bx),dy=Math.abs(ay-by);return 10*(dx+dy)-6*Math.min(dx,dy)};
 const push=(node,priority)=>{queue.push([node,priority]);let i=queue.length-1;while(i>0){let p=(i-1)>>1;if(queue[p][1]<=priority)break;queue[i]=queue[p];i=p}queue[i]=[node,priority]};
 const pop=()=>{const top=queue[0],last=queue.pop();if(queue.length){let i=0;while(true){let left=2*i+1;if(left>=queue.length)break;let right=left+1,child=right<queue.length&&queue[right][1]<queue[left][1]?right:left;if(queue[child][1]>=last[1])break;queue[i]=queue[child];i=child}queue[i]=last}return top[0]};
 cost[st]=0;push(st,heuristic(st,go));let iter=0;
 const dirs=[[-1,0,10],[1,0,10],[0,-1,10],[0,1,10],[-1,-1,14],[1,-1,14],[-1,1,14],[1,1,14]];
 while(queue.length&&iter++<n*4){let id=pop();if(closed[id])continue;closed[id]=1;if(id===go)break;let [gx,gy]=point(id);for(let [dx,dy,c] of dirs){let nx=gx+dx,ny=gy+dy;if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=NAV_X||ny>=NAV_Y)continue;let next=ny*NAV_X+nx;if(navBlocked[next]||closed[next])continue;if(dx&&dy&&(navBlocked[gy*NAV_X+nx]||navBlocked[ny*NAV_X+gx]))continue;let newCost=cost[id]+c;if(newCost>=cost[next])continue;cost[next]=newCost;prev[next]=id;push(next,newCost+heuristic(next,go))}}
 if(!closed[go])return null;
 const cells=[];let cur=go;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){if(cur===st)break;let cx=(cur%NAV_X+.5)*NAV_CELL,cy=(Math.floor(cur/NAV_X)+.5)*NAV_CELL;cells.push({x:cx,y:cy});cur=prev[cur];if(cur<0)return null}
 cells.reverse();let out=[],cx=startX,cy=startY;for(let i=0;i<cells.length;){let furthest=i;for(let j=i+1;j<cells.length;j++){if(!segmentPassable(cx,cy,cells[j].x,cells[j].y))break;furthest=j}out.push(cells[furthest]);cx=cells[furthest].x;cy=cells[furthest].y;i=furthest+1}if(segmentPassable(cx,cy,goalX,goalY))out.push({x:goalX,y:goalY});return out;
}
function clearWallCollision(){let changed=false;const e=ellipse();for(let pass=0;pass<4;pass++){let touch=false;for(const r of obstacles){let cx=clamp(e.x,r.x,r.x+r.w),cy=clamp(e.y,r.y,r.y+r.h),dx=e.x-cx,dy=e.y-cy,n=Math.hypot(dx/e.rx,dy/e.ry);if(n>=1)continue;
 let nx,ny,pushX=0,pushY=0;if(n<.000001){const opts=[{d:e.x-(r.x-e.rx),nx:-1,ny:0},{d:(r.x+r.w+e.rx)-e.x,nx:1,ny:0},{d:e.y-(r.y-e.ry),nx:0,ny:-1},{d:(r.y+r.h+e.ry)-e.y,nx:0,ny:1}];let v=opts.reduce((a,b)=>a.d<b.d?a:b);pushX=v.nx*(Math.max(.001,v.d)+.35);pushY=v.ny*(Math.max(.001,v.d)+.35);nx=v.nx;ny=v.ny}else{let normalizedX=dx/(e.rx*e.rx),normalizedY=dy/(e.ry*e.ry),len=Math.hypot(normalizedX,normalizedY);nx=normalizedX/len;ny=normalizedY/len;let required=(1-n)/(Math.sqrt((nx/e.rx)**2+(ny/e.ry)**2));pushX=nx*(required+.3);pushY=ny*(required+.3)}titan.x+=pushX;titan.y+=pushY;e.x=titan.x;e.y=titan.y+data.oy;let vn=titan.vx*nx+titan.vy*ny;if(vn<0){titan.vx-=vn*nx;titan.vy-=vn*ny}touch=true;changed=true}if(!touch)break}const arena=host.arena();if(arena?.active){const px=titan.x,py=titan.y;titan.x=clamp(titan.x,arena.l+arena.half+e.rx+1,arena.r-arena.half-e.rx-1);titan.y=clamp(titan.y,arena.t+arena.half+e.ry-data.oy+1,arena.b-arena.half-e.ry-data.oy-1);if(titan.x!==px||titan.y!==py){changed=true;titan.vx=titan.vy=0;}}if(changed)titan.contacts++;return changed}
function solvePlayerWalls(){for(let k=0;k<3;k++)for(const r of obstacles){let px=clamp(player.x,r.x,r.x+r.w),py=clamp(player.y,r.y,r.y+r.h),dx=player.x-px,dy=player.y-py,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(d>=PLAYER_R)continue;
 if(d>0.0001){player.x+=dx/d*(PLAYER_R-d+.2);player.y+=dy/d*(PLAYER_R-d+.2)}else{let distances=[{v:Math.abs(player.x-r.x),x:r.x-PLAYER_R,y:player.y},{v:Math.abs(r.x+r.w-player.x),x:r.x+r.w+PLAYER_R,y:player.y},{v:Math.abs(player.y-r.y),x:player.x,y:r.y-PLAYER_R},{v:Math.abs(r.y+r.h-player.y),x:player.x,y:r.y+r.h+PLAYER_R}];let best=distances.reduce((a,b)=>a.v<b.v?a:b);player.x=best.x;player.y=best.y}}}

function resolveBody(){
 const e=ellipse();let xx=player.x-e.x,yy=player.y-e.y,rx=e.rx+PLAYER_R,ry=e.ry+PLAYER_R,n=Math.hypot(xx/rx,yy/ry);
 if(n>1.006)return false;
 markRushContactV534(combat.current);
 if(owner&&!owner.dead&&!combat.dead&&!combat.paused&&!host.ended()&&combat.clock+1e-7>=combat.nextBodyV530){
  combat.nextBodyV530=combat.clock+.20;combat.stats.bodyContactChecks++;
  const prior=`body:${combat.bodySerialV530}`;combat.hitRecords.delete(prior);
  resolveHit(`body:${++combat.bodySerialV530}`,24*.38,'보스 몸통 접촉');
 }

}
function rotateToward(target,dt){const diff=angleDelta(titan.heading,target),maxTurn=dt*(phaseProfileV528().turn*Math.PI/180);titan.heading+=clamp(diff,-maxTurn,maxTurn);const horizontal=Math.cos(titan.heading),desired=horizontal>=0?1:-1;

 else {titan.turnTimer=Math.max(0,titan.turnTimer-dt*2);titan.pivotTimer=Math.max(0,titan.pivotTimer-dt*.7)}return Math.abs(diff)}
function selectPath(dt,d){titan.repathTimer-=dt;titan.lockTimer-=dt;const targetChange=Math.hypot(titan.targetX-player.x,titan.targetY-player.y);const next=titan.path[titan.routeIndex];let nextBlocked=next&&!segmentPassable(titan.x,titan.y,next.x,next.y,1);if(nextBlocked)titan.repathTimer=0;
 if(titan.repathTimer>0&&targetChange<76&&titan.path.length)return;
 if(segmentPassable(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y)) {titan.path=[{x:player.x,y:player.y}];titan.routeIndex=0;titan.repathTimer=.42;titan.lockTimer=0;blockedReason='직접 접근';}
 else if(titan.lockTimer>0&&titan.path.length&&!nextBlocked){titan.repathTimer=.22;blockedReason='우회 방향 유지';}
 else {let path=astar(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y);if(path&&path.length){titan.path=path;titan.routeIndex=0;titan.repaths++;titan.repathTimer=.75;titan.lockTimer=1.1;blockedReason='실제 장애물 경계 고려';}else{titan.path=[];titan.routeIndex=0;titan.repathTimer=.55;blockedReason='통과 가능한 길 없음';}}
 titan.targetX=player.x;titan.targetY=player.y;
}

function moveAI(dt){
 const dx=player.x-titan.x,dy=player.y-titan.y,d=hypot(dx,dy),angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx),e=ellipse();
 const ux=Math.cos(angle),uy=Math.sin(angle),rx=e.rx+PLAYER_R,ry=e.ry+PLAYER_R;
 const aa=ux*ux/(rx*rx)+uy*uy/(ry*ry),bb=-2*uy*data.oy/(ry*ry),cc=data.oy*data.oy/(ry*ry)-1;
 const safeStop=Math.max(8,(-bb+Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,bb*bb-4*aa*cc)))/(2*aa)-1);
 const approach=d>safeStop;titan.approaching=approach;
 const lineOpen=segmentPassable(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y,0,true);
 let tx=player.x,ty=player.y,remaining=d,route=false;
 if(data.ai&&approach){selectPath(dt,d);let wp=titan.path[titan.routeIndex];
  while(wp&&titan.routeIndex<titan.path.length-1&&hypot(wp.x-titan.x,wp.y-titan.y)<30){titan.routeIndex++;wp=titan.path[titan.routeIndex];}
  if(wp){tx=wp.x;ty=wp.y;remaining=hypot(tx-titan.x,ty-titan.y);route=titan.routeIndex<titan.path.length-1;}
 }
 const aim=Math.atan2(ty-titan.y,tx-titan.x),err=rotateToward(aim,dt);
 let targetSpeed=data.ai&&approach&&remaining>8&&titan.path.length?speedFor():0;
 if(lineOpen&&!route&&d<safeStop+65)targetSpeed*=clamp((d-safeStop)/65,.35,1);
 if(err>1.65)targetSpeed*=.18;else if(err>.60)targetSpeed*=.66;
 const wantX=targetSpeed*Math.cos(titan.heading),wantY=targetSpeed*Math.sin(titan.heading),dvx=wantX-titan.vx,dvy=wantY-titan.vy;
 const delta=hypot(dvx,dvy),limit=(targetSpeed>hypot(titan.vx,titan.vy)?230:310)*dt,blend=delta>limit?limit/delta:1;
 titan.vx+=dvx*blend;titan.vy+=dvy*blend;const sp=hypot(titan.vx,titan.vy),oldX=titan.x,oldY=titan.y;
 for(let i=0,n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(sp*dt/8));i<n;i++){titan.x=clamp(titan.x+titan.vx*dt/n,data.rx+3,WORLD_W-data.rx-3);titan.y=clamp(titan.y+titan.vy*dt/n,data.ry+12,WORLD_H-data.ry-12);clearWallCollision();}
 if(targetSpeed>5&&hypot(titan.x-oldX,titan.y-oldY)<dt*3){titan.stuckT+=dt;if(titan.stuckT>.55){titan.repathTimer=0;titan.lockTimer=0;}}else titan.stuckT=Math.max(0,titan.stuckT-2*dt);
 titan.move=clamp(sp/Math.max(1,speedFor()),0,1);titan.anim+=dt*(sp>8?6+4*titan.move:6);
 if(sp>8){titan.footT+=dt*sp/25;if(titan.footT>1){titan.footT%=1;titan.footsteps++;titan.footSide=1-titan.footSide;}}
 setStatus(sp>8?(route?'BYPASS':'APPROACH'):'TURN',route?'장애물을 우회해 직접 추격':'먹잇감 직접 추격');
}

// STEP10: purely VISUAL pose scheduler. All source image base64, hitboxes, timings and AI decisions stay intact.
const motion={enabled:true,intensity:1,time:0,mode:'IDLE',clip:'idle',baseClip:'idle',poseKey:null,poseOpacity:0,posePrev:null,blendTimer:0,
  hitTime:0,hitTotal:.43,deathTime:0,phasePulse:0,impact:0,impactSide:1,

function resetMotion(){motion.time=0;motion.mode='IDLE';motion.clip='idle';motion.baseClip='idle';motion.poseKey=null;
 motion.poseOpacity=0;motion.posePrev=null;motion.blendTimer=0;motion.hitTime=0;motion.deathTime=0;
 motion.phasePulse=0;motion.impact=0;motion.dust.length=0;motion.trails.length=0;motion.chargeTimer=0;
 motion.footstepCursor=titan.footsteps;motion.frameIndex=0;motion.source='idle_01';

function motionHit(visualOnly=false){if(combat.dead)return;motion.hitTime=motion.hitTotal;
 motion.impact=Math.max(motion.impact,.32);motion.impactSide=-motion.impactSide;motion.stats.hitReacts++;

function motionImpact(power=.5){if(combat.dead)return;motion.impact=Math.max(motion.impact,power);motion.stats.impacts++;}
function motionDust(x,y,force=1){motion.dust.push({x,y,t:.65,max:.65,force,seed:motion.time+motion.stats.footDust*1.37});
 if(motion.dust.length>22)motion.dust.shift();motion.stats.footDust++;}
function motionSnapshot(){motion.trails.push({x:titan.x,y:titan.y,face:titan.face,t:.30,alpha:.30,code:motion.source});
 if(motion.trails.length>8)motion.trails.shift();}
function motionAttackVisual(c){if(!c)return null;
 const a=ATTACKS[c.key],wind=c.stage==='WINDUP',active=c.stage==='ACTIVE';
 const fraction=wind?clamp(c.t/Math.max(.01,a.windup),0,1):active?clamp(c.t/Math.max(.01,a.active),0,1):0;
 let pose=null,weight=0,lean=0,drop=0,stretch=1;
 switch(c.key){
 case 'claw':case 'twin':case 'rear':
  pose=wind?'melee_02':active?(c.key==='twin'&&c.t<a.interval?'melee_02':'melee_06'):'melee_02';
  weight=wind?.12+fraction*.68:active?.90:.34;lean=wind?-.055*fraction:active?.075:-.035;drop=wind?3*fraction:active?-3:0;break;
 case 'charge':
  pose=wind?'melee_02':active?'move_06':'melee_02';weight=wind?.15+.56*fraction:active?.84:.25;
  lean=wind?-.08*fraction:active?.075:-.04;stretch=wind?1-.035*fraction:active?1.045:1;drop=wind?5*fraction:0;break;
 case 'feast':
  pose=wind?'melee_02':active?(c.t<ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd?'move_08':c.t<ATTACKS.feast.waveAt?'special_06':'melee_06'):'melee_02';
  weight=wind?.12+.57*fraction:active?.88:.27;lean=wind?-.06*fraction:active&&c.t<ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd?.075:0;drop=wind?6*fraction:0;break;
 case 'crush':case 'crush2':case 'pulse':
  pose=wind?'melee_02':active?'special_06':'melee_02';weight=wind?.15+.60*fraction:active?.94:.28;
  drop=wind?6*fraction:active?-3:0;stretch=wind?1-.045*fraction:active?1.035:1;break;
 case 'spit':case 'mortar':case 'backfire':
  pose=wind?'idle_08':active?'cast_06':'idle_08';
  // Emission pose is brief for individual volleys; don't imply an always-damaging beam.
  const firePulse=['spit','mortar','backfire'].includes(c.key)?Math.min(...Array.from({length:a.count||a.siteCount||2},(_,i)=>Math.abs(c.t-i*a.interval))):0;
  weight=wind?.08+.28*fraction:active?(.14+.81*(1-clamp(firePulse/.19,0,1))):.20;
  lean=wind?-.028*fraction:active?.035:0;break;
 case 'sacs':case 'reclaim':
  pose=wind?'idle_09':active?(c.key==='sacs'?'special_06':'melee_02'):'idle_09';
  weight=wind?.08+.36*fraction:active?.76:.19;drop=wind?4*fraction:0;break;
 default:break;}
 if(c.stage==='RECOVERY'){const fade=1-clamp(c.t/Math.max(.01,Math.min(.6,a.recovery)),0,1);weight*=fade;lean*=fade;drop*=fade;stretch=1+(stretch-1)*fade;}
 return {pose,weight,lean,drop,stretch};
}
function advanceMotion(dt){
 // Even DEAD is paused by the combat state machine: death animation advances independently.
 motion.time+=dt;const before=motion.mode;
 let next='IDLE';
 if(combat.dead){next='DEATH';motion.deathTime=Math.min(9,motion.deathTime+dt);motion.hitTime=0;motion.impact=0;}
 else if(combat.crashV529)next='GROGGY';
 else if(combat.recoilV529>0)next='CRASH_RECOIL';
 else if(combat.phaseTransition>0){next=combat.phaseTarget===3?'MUTATION_III':'MUTATION_II';motion.phasePulse+=dt;}
 else if(combat.ventTime>0)next='VENT';
 else if(combat.current)next=MOTION_SKILLS[combat.current.key]+'_'+combat.current.stage;
 else if(titan.move>.14)next='LOCOMOTION';
 else if(titan.pivotTimer>.10)next='PIVOT';
 if(motion.hitTime>0&&!combat.dead)motion.hitTime=Math.max(0,motion.hitTime-dt);
 motion.impact=Math.max(0,motion.impact-dt*1.65);
 if(next!==before){motion.stats.visualTransitions++;motion.mode=next;}
 const visual=motionAttackVisual(combat.current);
 // Poses are chosen by actual WINDUP/ACTIVE/RECOVERY progression (no gameplay-driven timers changed).
 let pose=visual?.pose||null,opacity=visual?.weight||0;
 if(combat.phaseTransition>0){pose=combat.phaseTarget===3?'special_06':'melee_02';opacity=.10+.55*Math.max(0,Math.sin(motion.phasePulse*6));}
 else if(combat.ventTime>0){pose='idle_09';opacity=.18+.22*Math.abs(Math.sin(motion.time*8));}
 if(motion.hitTime>0&&!combat.dead){pose='melee_02';opacity=Math.max(opacity,.28*motion.hitTime/motion.hitTotal);}
 if(pose && !graphics[pose]?.naturalWidth){pose=null;opacity=0;motion.stats.sourceFailures++;}
 if(motion.poseKey!==pose){motion.posePrev=motion.poseKey;motion.blendTimer=.13;motion.poseKey=pose;}
 motion.blendTimer=Math.max(0,motion.blendTimer-dt);
 motion.poseOpacity+=(opacity-motion.poseOpacity)*Math.min(1,dt*12);
 motion.clip=pose||((titan.move>.14)?'move':'idle');
 for(let i=motion.dust.length-1;i>=0;i--){motion.dust[i].t-=dt;if(motion.dust[i].t<=0)motion.dust.splice(i,1)}
 for(let i=motion.trails.length-1;i>=0;i--){motion.trails[i].t-=dt;if(motion.trails[i].t<=0)motion.trails.splice(i,1)}
 if(!motion.enabled)return;
 if(!combat.dead&&!combat.current&&titan.footsteps>motion.footstepCursor){
  const count=Math.min(3,titan.footsteps-motion.footstepCursor);
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++)motionDust(titan.x-36*titan.face+(i%2)*68*titan.face,titan.y+data.size*.23,.75);
 }
 motion.footstepCursor=titan.footsteps;
 if(combat.current&&['charge','feast'].includes(combat.current.key)&&combat.current.stage==='ACTIVE'&&titan.move>.1){
  motion.chargeTimer+=dt;if(motion.chargeTimer>=.092){motion.chargeTimer%=.092;motionSnapshot();}
 }else motion.chargeTimer=0;
}
function motionSprite(code,x,y,face=1,angle=0,sx=1,sy=1,opacity=1){
 const def=BIO_FRAMES[code],im=graphics[code];if(!def||!im?.naturalWidth)return false;
 const sc=data.size/1200;
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=opacity;ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(angle);ctx.scale(face*sx,sy);
 ctx.drawImage(im,-def.ax*sc,-def.ay*sc,def.w*sc,def.h*sc);ctx.restore();return true;
}
function renderMotionGround(){if(!motion.enabled)return;

  ctx.globalAlpha=.25*k;ctx.fillStyle='#b4a08b';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x+12*(1-k),p.y-13*(1-k),r,r*.24,0,0,6.283);ctx.fill();}
 ctx.restore();
}
function renderMotionTrails(){if(!motion.enabled)return;
 ctx.save();for(const h of motion.trails){const p=scPos(h.x,h.y+data.size*.22),f=h.t/.30;

  motionSprite(h.code in BIO_FRAMES?h.code:'move_06',p.x,p.y,h.face,-.02,1,1,.13*f*motion.intensity);
 }ctx.restore();
}
function renderMotionOrgan(){if(!motion.enabled||combat.dead&&motion.deathTime>3)return;

 const k=(.5+.5*Math.sin(motion.time*(combat.bossPhase===3?10:5.2)))*pumping*intensity;
 ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.16+.34*k;let rad=data.size*(.09+.037*k);
 let g=ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x,p.y,2,p.x,p.y,rad*2.2);
 g.addColorStop(0,'#fff8c6e8');g.addColorStop(.29,'#ff8e5388');g.addColorStop(1,'#b7183400');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,rad*2.2,0,6.283);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
}

function recordEvent(message){combat.events.unshift(`${combat.clock.toFixed(1)}s  ${message}`);combat.events.length=Math.min(9,combat.events.length);combat.lastReason=message;}
function resetCombat(full=true){combat.nextBodyV530=0;combat.bodySerialV530=0;combat.crashV529=null;combat.recoilV529=0;combat.crashSerialV529=0;combat.current=null;combat.projectiles.length=0;combat.tissues.length=0;combat.sacs.length=0;combat.sacSerial=0;combat.pressure=0;combat.ventTime=0;combat.ventCount=0;combat.bossPhase=1;combat.phaseTransition=0;combat.phasePending=false;combat.pendingPhase=0;combat.phaseTarget=0;combat.dead=false;combat.phaseChanges=0;combat.ventDuration=2.2;data.ratio=58;combat.bossHP=4200;combat.bossDamageEvents.length=0;combat.hitRecords.clear();combat.hitEffects.length=0;combat.phase='IDLE';combat.lastKey=null;combat.nextDecision=1.2;combat.invuln=0;combat.damageFlash=0;

 applyPhaseProfileV528(1);resourcePrevV528=null;resetAIBrain();recordEvent('모든 공격·잔류 지형 초기화');}
function playerCorridorClear(x1,y1,x2,y2){if(x2<PLAYER_R||x2>WORLD_W-PLAYER_R||y2<PLAYER_R||y2>WORLD_H-PLAYER_R)return false;
 for(const w of obstacles){const dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1;let lo=0,hi=1;
  for(const [p,q] of [[-dx,x1-(w.x-PLAYER_R)],[dx,w.x+w.w+PLAYER_R-x1],[-dy,y1-(w.y-PLAYER_R)],[dy,w.y+w.h+PLAYER_R-y1]]){
   if(Math.abs(p)<1e-9){if(q<0){lo=2;break}}else{let v=q/p;if(p<0)lo=Math.max(lo,v);else hi=Math.min(hi,v)}
  }if(lo<=hi)return false;
 }return true;
}
// STEP12 fair-path check: do not count a dodge through the boss's solid body.
// Existing playerCorridorClear remains unchanged for sight, routing and obstacle avoidance.
function playerDodgeCorridorClear(x1,y1,x2,y2){
 if(!playerCorridorClear(x1,y1,x2,y2))return false;
 const e=ellipse(),rx=e.rx+PLAYER_R+2,ry=e.ry+PLAYER_R+2,dx=x2-x1,dy=y2-y1;
 const startNorm=((x1-e.x)/rx)**2+((y1-e.y)/ry)**2;let previousNorm=startNorm;
 for(let i=1;i<=24;i++){
  const t=i/24,px=x1+dx*t,py=y1+dy*t;
  const norm=((px-e.x)/rx)**2+((py-e.y)/ry)**2;
  if(norm<1&&(startNorm>=1||norm<previousNorm-1e-7))return false;previousNorm=norm;
 }
 return true;
}
// Auto AI suppresses aimed blast when all simple exits are blocked by geometry.
function canEscapeCircle(x,y,r,notice,other=null){if(hypot(player.x-x,player.y-y)>r+PLAYER_R)return true;
 const travel=Math.max(0,escapeSpeed()*notice*tissueMultiplier()-8),required=r+PLAYER_R+5;
 if(travel<required-hypot(player.x-x,player.y-y))return false;
 for(let k=0;k<24;k++){const a=Math.PI*2*k/24,tx=player.x+Math.cos(a)*travel,ty=player.y+Math.sin(a)*travel;
  if(hypot(tx-x,ty-y)<=required)continue;
  if(other&&other.some(o=>hypot(tx-o.x,ty-o.y)<=o.r+PLAYER_R+3))continue;
  if(playerDodgeCorridorClear(player.x,player.y,tx,ty))return true;
 }return false;
}
// STEP12: auto-AI avoids using short-range swipes if the player has no obstacle-free escape.
// Include the 0.82 movement multiplier caused by existing digestive zones.
function canEscapeMelee(aims,range,halfAngle,notice){
 const danger=(x,y)=>aims.some(angle=>inSector(x,y,angle,range,halfAngle));if(!danger(player.x,player.y))return true;
 const travel=Math.max(0,escapeSpeed()*notice*tissueMultiplier()-8);
 for(let i=0;i<48;i++){const angle=2*Math.PI*i/48,tx=player.x+Math.cos(angle)*travel,ty=player.y+Math.sin(angle)*travel;if(!danger(tx,ty)&&playerDodgeCorridorClear(player.x,player.y,tx,ty))return true;}return false;
}
const PHASE2_THRESHOLD=2940,PHASE3_THRESHOLD=1470;
const PHASE2_TIME=2.1,PHASE3_TIME=2.65;
function phase2MoveRatio(){applyPhaseProfileV528(2);}
function phase3MoveRatio(){applyPhaseProfileV528(3);}
function updatePhaseTarget(){
 if(owner?.encounterPhaseV526)return;
 if(combat.dead||combat.bossHP<=0)return;
 const next=combat.bossHP<=PHASE3_THRESHOLD?3:combat.bossHP<=PHASE2_THRESHOLD?2:1;
 // A single overwhelming hit can skip an intermediate phase. While transforming,
 // upgrade the same animation instead of playing phase II and III back-to-back.
 if(combat.phaseTransition>0&&next>combat.phaseTarget){
  combat.phaseTarget=next;combat.pendingPhase=0;combat.phasePending=false;
  combat.phaseTransition=Math.max(combat.phaseTransition,1.65);
  recordEvent(`치명적인 변이 가속 · 중간 연출 없이 ${next}페이즈로 직접 전환`);
  return;
 }
 if(next>combat.bossPhase&&next>combat.pendingPhase&&next>combat.phaseTarget){
  combat.pendingPhase=next;combat.phasePending=true;
  recordEvent(`HP 임계값 진입 · 현재 공격 종료 후 ${next}페이즈 전환 대기`);
 }
}
function requestPhase2(){
 if(combat.dead||combat.bossPhase>=2||combat.phaseTarget>=2){showNotice('이미 2페이즈 이상 진행 중',1.5);return false;}
 combat.bossHP=Math.min(combat.bossHP,PHASE2_THRESHOLD);updatePhaseTarget();
 if(!combat.current&&combat.ventTime<=0)beginPhaseTransition();renderCombatUI();return true;
}
function requestPhase3(){
 if(combat.dead||combat.bossPhase>=3||combat.phaseTarget>=3){showNotice('이미 3페이즈 진행 중',1.5);return false;}
 combat.bossHP=Math.min(combat.bossHP,PHASE3_THRESHOLD);updatePhaseTarget();
 if(!combat.current&&combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)beginPhaseTransition();renderCombatUI();return true;
}
function beginPhaseTransition(){
 const next=combat.pendingPhase;
 if(combat.dead||combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0||next<=combat.bossPhase||combat.current||combat.ventTime>0||combat.phaseTransition>0)return false;
 combat.pendingPhase=0;combat.phasePending=false;combat.phaseTarget=next;
 combat.phaseTransition=next===3?PHASE3_TIME:PHASE2_TIME;
 // STEP12: preserve already-launched projectiles and digestive puddles across mutation.
 // Their strike phase is captured at launch; no new attack is initiated during the transformation.
 combat.phase='TRANSITION';
 titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;
 recordEvent(next===3?'노심 통제 붕괴 · 3페이즈 최종 변이':'갑각 파손 · 2페이즈 전환');
 return true;
}
function beginPhase2(){return combat.pendingPhase===2?beginPhaseTransition():false;}
function beginPhase3(){return combat.pendingPhase===3?beginPhaseTransition():false;}
function advancePhase2(dt){
 if(combat.phaseTransition<=0||combat.dead)return;
 combat.phaseTransition=Math.max(0,combat.phaseTransition-dt);
 if(combat.phaseTransition>0)return;
 const next=combat.phaseTarget;combat.phaseTarget=0;
 if(next<=combat.bossPhase)return;
 combat.bossPhase=next;combat.phaseChanges++;combat.stats.phaseChanges++;
 if(next===3){
  combat.stats.phase3Changes++;phase3MoveRatio();combat.ventDuration=phaseProfileV528().vent;
  recordEvent('PHASE III · 붕괴하는 포식자! 연속 돌진 / 최종 포식 · 진액 회수로 재생');
  showNotice('PHASE Ⅲ · COLLAPSING DEVOURER · 12 SKILLS',3.3);
 }else{
  phase2MoveRatio();combat.ventDuration=phaseProfileV528().vent;
  recordEvent('2페이즈 · 깨진 갑각! 거체 압살 / 지반 맥동 · 기동성 증가');
  showNotice('PHASE Ⅱ · 깨진 갑각 · 거체 압살 / 지반 맥동 해금',3);
 }
 combat.phase='IDLE';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;updatePhaseTarget();
 if(combat.pressure>=100)startVent();
 else if(combat.phasePending)beginPhaseTransition();
}
function finishBossDeath(){combat.crashV529=null;combat.recoilV529=0;
 if(combat.dead)return;
 combat.dead=true;combat.pendingPhase=0;combat.phasePending=false;combat.phaseTarget=0;combat.phaseTransition=0;
 combat.current=null;combat.projectiles.length=0;combat.tissues.length=0;combat.sacs.length=0;
 combat.pressure=0;combat.ventTime=0;combat.auto=false;combat.paused=true;
 combat.phase='DEAD';combat.stats.deaths++;titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;
 recordEvent('BIO TITAN 사망 · HP 0 · 모든 페이즈 전환 취소');
 showNotice('BIO TITAN DEFEATED · ↻ 초기화로 재전투',4);
}
function pulseSafe(angle,aim,a){
 return Math.abs(angleDelta(angle,aim+a.gapOffset))<=a.gapHalf || Math.abs(angleDelta(angle,aim-a.gapOffset))<=a.gapHalf;
}
function pulseDangerAt(px,py,c){const a=ATTACKS.pulse,dx=px-titan.x,dy=py-titan.y;
 return !pulseSafe(Math.atan2(dy,dx),c.aim,a);
}
function pulseAutofair(){
 const a=ATTACKS.pulse,d=hypot(player.x-titan.x,player.y-titan.y),bearing=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x);
 if(d<a.ringStart-PLAYER_R-a.ringWidth||d>a.ringEnd+PLAYER_R+a.ringWidth)return true;
 const arrival=a.lockBefore+Math.max(0,(d-a.ringStart-PLAYER_R-a.ringWidth*.5)/(a.ringEnd-a.ringStart))*a.active;
 const reach=escapeSpeed()*tissueMultiplier()*Math.max(0,arrival-.10);
 // Check both real gap centers plus a radial exit using player-sized wall clearance.
 for(const sign of [-1,1]){
  const angle=bearing+sign*a.gapOffset,x=titan.x+Math.cos(angle)*d,y=titan.y+Math.sin(angle)*d;
  if(hypot(x-player.x,y-player.y)<=reach&&playerDodgeCorridorClear(player.x,player.y,x,y))return true;
 }
 const distance=a.ringEnd+PLAYER_R+a.ringWidth*.5+5,x=titan.x+Math.cos(bearing)*distance,y=titan.y+Math.sin(bearing)*distance;
 return distance-d<=reach&&playerDodgeCorridorClear(player.x,player.y,x,y);
}
function tickPulse(c,oldT,newT){
 const a=ATTACKS.pulse;const r0=a.ringStart+(a.ringEnd-a.ringStart)*oldT/a.active;
 const r1=a.ringStart+(a.ringEnd-a.ringStart)*newT/a.active;
 const d=hypot(player.x-titan.x,player.y-titan.y);
 if(d+PLAYER_R+a.ringWidth*.5<r0||d-PLAYER_R-a.ringWidth*.5>r1)return;
 if(!pulseDangerAt(player.x,player.y,c)){if(!c.safeRecorded){c.safeRecorded=true;combat.stats.avoids++;recordEvent('지반 맥동 · 측면 균열 통로 회피')}return}
 if(!c.pulseHit){c.pulseHit=true;combat.stats.pulseHits++;resolveHit(`${c.id}:pulse`,a.damage,a.name)}
}
function backfireSites(c){
 const a=ATTACKS.backfire, pad=a.radius+PLAYER_R+3;
 const x=clamp(c.targetX,pad,WORLD_W-pad),y=clamp(c.targetY,pad,WORLD_H-pad);
 const v=1.65*UNIT,px=-Math.sin(c.aim),py=Math.cos(c.aim);
 // 3 fixed explosions separated laterally; all remain in actual world bounds.
 return [
  {x,y,r:a.radius,impacted:false},
  {x:clamp(x+px*v,pad,WORLD_W-pad),y:clamp(y+py*v,pad,WORLD_H-pad),r:a.radius,impacted:false},
  {x:clamp(x-px*v,pad,WORLD_W-pad),y:clamp(y-py*v,pad,WORLD_H-pad),r:a.radius,impacted:false}
 ];
}
function backfireFair(){
 const a=ATTACKS.backfire,dir=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x);
 const mock={aim:dir,targetX:player.x,targetY:player.y};
 const sites=backfireSites(mock);
 return canEscapeCircle(sites[0].x,sites[0].y,a.radius,a.lockBefore,sites.slice(1));
}
function impactBackfire(c,index){
 const a=ATTACKS.backfire,site=c.sites[index];
 if(!site||site.impacted)return;
 site.impacted=true;combat.stats.backfireImpacts++;c.impactCount++;
 combat.hitEffects.push({x:site.x,y:site.y,t:.44,damage:'✹'});
 const hit=hypot(player.x-site.x,player.y-site.y)<=a.radius+PLAYER_R;
 if(hit)resolveHit(`${c.id}:backfire`,a.damage,`배압 포격 ${index+1}타`);
 else combat.stats.avoids++;
 recordEvent(`배압 포격 ${index+1}/3 · 고정 지점 압력 배출`);
}
function hitFeast(c,section){
 const id=section==='돌진'?`${c.id}:feast:rush:${c.rushIndex||0}`:`${c.id}:feast:finish`;
 if(combat.hitRecords.has(id))return false;
 const ok=resolveHit(id,ATTACKS.feast.damage,`최종 포식 · ${section}`);
 if(ok&&section==='돌진')combat.stats.feastChargeHits++;
 if(ok&&section==='압살')combat.stats.feastSlamHits++;
 if(ok&&section==='맥동')combat.stats.feastWaveHits++;
 return ok;
}
function tickFeastCharge(c,dt){advanceRushV535(c,dt,true);}
function feastSlam(c){
 if(c.slamDone)return;
 c.slamDone=true;
 const a=ATTACKS.feast,d=hypot(player.x-c.finalX,player.y-c.finalY);
 combat.hitEffects.push({x:c.finalX,y:c.finalY,t:.44,damage:'✹'});
 if(d<=a.slamRadius+PLAYER_R)hitFeast(c,'압살');else combat.stats.avoids++;
 recordEvent('최종 포식 2/3 · 목표 고정된 분쇄 타격');
}
function feastSafe(angle,c,a){return Math.abs(angleDelta(angle,c.aim+a.pulseOffset))<=a.pulseHalf||Math.abs(angleDelta(angle,c.aim-a.pulseOffset))<=a.pulseHalf;}
function tickFeastWave(c,oldT,newT){
 const a=ATTACKS.feast;if(newT<=a.waveAt||oldT>=a.active)return;
 const lo=Math.max(a.waveAt,oldT),hi=Math.min(a.active,newT),r0=a.pulseStart+(a.pulseEnd-a.pulseStart)*(lo-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,r1=a.pulseStart+(a.pulseEnd-a.pulseStart)*(hi-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration;
 const d=hypot(player.x-c.finalX,player.y-c.finalY);if(d+PLAYER_R+a.pulseWidth/2<r0||d-PLAYER_R-a.pulseWidth/2>r1)return;
 if(feastSafe(Math.atan2(player.y-c.finalY,player.x-c.finalX),c,a)){if(!c.safeRecorded){c.safeRecorded=true;combat.stats.avoids++;}return;}
 if(!combat.hitRecords.has(`${c.id}:feast:finish`))hitFeast(c,'맥동');
}
function tickFeast(c,oldT,span){
 const a=ATTACKS.feast,to=oldT+span;
 if(oldT<a.chargeEnd)tickFeastCharge(c,Math.max(0,Math.min(a.chargeEnd,to)-oldT));
 if(c.chargeFinished&&oldT<a.chargeEnd){
  if((c.rushIndex||0)+1<(a.rushes||1)&&!combat.paused){nextRushV528(c);return;}
  if(c.rushWallV529&&!c.rushContactV529){beginCrashV529(c);return;}
  // The follow-up lands at the actual stopping point, not a projected old target.
  if(!c.feastLandedV533){clearWallCollision();c.finalX=titan.x;c.finalY=titan.y;c.feastLandedV533=true;}
  titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;
 }
 if(oldT<a.slamAt&&to>=a.slamAt)feastSlam(c);
 if(oldT<a.waveAt&&to>=a.waveAt)recordEvent('최종 포식 · 지반 맥동 확산');
 tickFeastWave(c,oldT,to);
}
const aiBrain={advanced:true,mode:'OBSERVE',intent:'먹이를 관찰',reason:'',chosen:null,lastX:0,lastY:0,ready:false,vx:0,vy:0,speed:0,still:0,orbit:0,wallProximity:999,lineClear:true,sequence:[],lastScores:[],lastChoiceAt:-100,decisions:0,skips:0,prevented:0,alternations:0,pressureChoices:0,lastPhase:1,lastRushAtV528:-100,lastSalvoAtV528:-100,lastSignatureAtV528:-100,signatureIndexV528:0,lastBarrageAtV530:-6};
function resetAIBrain(){
 Object.assign(aiBrain,{mode:'OBSERVE',intent:'먹이를 관찰',reason:'',chosen:null,lastX:player.x,lastY:player.y,
  ready:false,vx:0,vy:0,speed:0,still:0,orbit:0,wallProximity:999,lineClear:true,
  sequence:[],lastScores:[],lastChoiceAt:-100,decisions:0,skips:0,prevented:0,alternations:0,pressureChoices:0,lastPhase:1,lastRushAtV528:-100,lastSalvoAtV528:-100,lastSignatureAtV528:-100,signatureIndexV528:0,lastBarrageAtV530:-6});
}
function observePlayerMotion(dt){
 if(dt<=0)return;
 if(!aiBrain.ready){aiBrain.lastX=player.x;aiBrain.lastY=player.y;aiBrain.ready=true;return}
 const dx=player.x-aiBrain.lastX,dy=player.y-aiBrain.lastY;
 aiBrain.lastX=player.x;aiBrain.lastY=player.y;
 if(hypot(dx,dy)>UNIT*dt*2.4+2){aiBrain.vx=aiBrain.vy=0;return} // QA teleport: never learn phantom speed
 const alpha=1-Math.exp(-dt*8);
 aiBrain.vx+=(dx/dt-aiBrain.vx)*alpha;aiBrain.vy+=(dy/dt-aiBrain.vy)*alpha;
 aiBrain.speed=hypot(aiBrain.vx,aiBrain.vy);
 aiBrain.still=aiBrain.speed<21?Math.min(10,aiBrain.still+dt):Math.max(0,aiBrain.still-dt*3);
 const rx=player.x-titan.x,ry=player.y-titan.y,rd=Math.max(1,hypot(rx,ry));
 aiBrain.orbit=(rx*aiBrain.vy-ry*aiBrain.vx)/rd;
 let edge=Math.min(player.x-PLAYER_R,WORLD_W-player.x-PLAYER_R,player.y-PLAYER_R,WORLD_H-player.y-PLAYER_R);
 for(const o of obstacles){const px=clamp(player.x,o.x,o.x+o.w),py=clamp(player.y,o.y,o.y+o.h);edge=Math.min(edge,Math.max(0,hypot(player.x-px,player.y-py)-PLAYER_R))}
 aiBrain.wallProximity=edge;
 aiBrain.lineClear=playerCorridorClear(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y);
}
function assessTactic(){
 aiBrain.lineClear=playerCorridorClear(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y);
 const d=hypot(player.x-titan.x,player.y-titan.y),readySac=[...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].some(p=>p.ready&&!p.returning);
 const recover=combat.pressure>=55||combat.bossHP<combat.bossMaxHP;
 let mode='HUNT',intent='먹이를 압박하며 간격 조절';
 if(readySac&&recover){mode='HARVEST';intent='저장낭 회수 · 압력과 체력 관리'}
 else if(combat.pressure>=85){mode='VENT_SETUP';intent='노심 과압 · 배기 직전의 공격 선택'}
 else if(!aiBrain.lineClear){mode='BYPASS';intent='잔해 너머 추적 · 직선 공격 억제'}
 else if(d<215){mode='GRAPPLE';intent='근접 제압 · 앞발과 반격'}
 else if(d>420){mode='SIEGE';intent='먼 거리 포위 · 포격과 추격'}
 else if(aiBrain.speed>75){mode='INTERCEPT';intent='움직이는 먹잇감의 동선 차단'}
 else {mode='ZONE';intent='도주 경로 압박 · 구역 봉쇄'}
 aiBrain.mode=mode;aiBrain.intent=intent;
 return {d,mode,readySac,recover,fast:aiBrain.speed>75,still:aiBrain.still>.55,
         wall:aiBrain.wallProximity<105,blocked:!aiBrain.lineClear,orbital:Math.abs(aiBrain.orbit)>55,
         phase:combat.bossPhase,pressure:combat.pressure};
}
function tacticalScore(key,s){
 // Meaningful but not omniscient. This scores only moves already allowed by canStart().
 const d=s.d,p=s.pressure,phase=s.phase,close=d<220,far=d>360;
 const base={claw:9,twin:9,rear:10,spit:9,mortar:9,charge:9,sacs:8,reclaim:0,crush2:9,pulse:9,backfire:14,feast:12};
 let v=base[key]||1;const why=[];
 function add(value,reason){v+=value;if(value>=3)why.push(reason)}
 if(key==='claw'){
  if(close)add(10,'근접 노출');if(s.fast)add(3,'움직임 견제');
 }else if(key==='twin'){
  if(close)add(12,'붙잡기 기회');if(s.still)add(5,'정지 목표');
 }else if(key==='rear'){
  add(14,'후방 위협');if(s.orbital)add(5,'측면 회피 읽기');
 }else if(key==='spit'){
  if(far)add(9,'거리 벌린 먹이');if(s.fast)add(5,'이동 견제');
  if(s.blocked)v-=50;
 }else if(key==='mortar'){
  if(far)add(9,'원거리 압박');if(s.still)add(9,'머무른 구역');
  if(s.wall)add(4,'퇴로 제한');
 }else if(key==='charge'){
  if(far)add(10,'접근 필요');if(s.still)add(3,'직선 통로');
  if(s.blocked)v-=60;
 }else if(key==='sacs'){
  if(combat.sacs.length===0)add(10,'새로운 포식 구역');
  if(combat.sacs.length===2)add(2,'영역 유지');
  if(s.fast)add(6,'동선 봉쇄');
  if(combat.sacs.length>=4)v-=30;
 }else if(key==='reclaim'){
  if(s.readySac&&s.recover)add(37,'저장낭 회수 시점');
  if(p>=75)add(20,'대사압 해소');
  if(phase<3&&combat.bossHP<3000)add(14,'파손 조직 재생');
  if(!s.readySac)v-=80;
 }else if(key==='crush2'){
  if(!far)add(10,'접근한 먹이 압살');if(s.still)add(9,'멈춰 선 먹이');
 }else if(key==='pulse'){
  if(s.fast)add(7,'지속 이동 견제');
  if(s.orbital)add(6,'회전 회피 대응');
 }else if(key==='backfire'){
  if(d>285)add(10,'중장거리 압력 포격');if(s.still)add(6,'위치 집착');
  if(s.wall)add(3,'경계 구역');
 }else if(key==='feast'){
  if(far)add(8,'결정적 돌파');if(phase===3)add(7,'노심 붕괴');
  if(p<48&&d>230)add(9,'안정된 노심 · 강력한 연계');
  if(p>=55)v-=8; // don't spam long sequences when a vent is imminent
 }
 if(p>=83&&s.readySac&&key!=='reclaim')v-=5;
 // Finite memory reduces perceptible rote cycles, while retaining a valid fallback.
 if(combat.bossPhase===1&&['spit','mortar'].includes(key))v+=12;
 const last=aiBrain.sequence;
 if(last.at(-1)===key)v-=23;
 else if(last.at(-2)===key)v-=10;
 else if(last.slice(-4).includes(key))v-=4;
 // Very small texture noise only, not the old blind weighted roulette.
 v+=(Math.random()-.5)*1.2;
 return {key,score:v,reason:why.slice(0,2).join(' · ')||'지형과 사거리 적합'};
}
function tacticalChoose(keys){
 const st=assessTactic();
 const scores=keys.map(k=>tacticalScore(k,st)).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
 aiBrain.lastScores=scores.map(v=>({key:v.key,score:+v.score.toFixed(2)}));
 // When safety gating leaves no legal attack, pursue/reposition instead of forcing hits.
 if(!scores.length){aiBrain.chosen=null;aiBrain.reason='공격 불가 · 이동/방향/장애물 또는 회피 경로 확인 중';aiBrain.skips++;return null}
 const pick=scores[0];
 aiBrain.chosen=pick.key;aiBrain.reason=`${st.mode} · ${pick.reason}`;
 return pick.key;
}
function registerAIChoice(key){
 if(!key)return;
 const last=aiBrain.sequence;
 if(last.length&&last.at(-1)!==key)aiBrain.alternations++;
 last.push(key);while(last.length>7)last.shift();
 aiBrain.lastChoiceAt=combat.clock;aiBrain.decisions++;if(key==='sacs')aiBrain.lastSalvoAtV528=combat.clock;if(key==='charge'||key==='feast')aiBrain.lastRushAtV528=combat.clock;if(['crush2','pulse','feast'].includes(key)){aiBrain.lastSignatureAtV528=combat.clock;aiBrain.signatureIndexV528++;}
 if(key==='barrage')aiBrain.lastBarrageAtV530=combat.clock;
 if(key==='reclaim')aiBrain.pressureChoices++;
}
function classicChoose(keys,d){
 const ranked=keys.map(k=>({k,w:({claw:d<190?9:2,twin:d<210?8:1,rear:12,spit:d>250?7:3,charge:d>250?6:2,
 mortar:d>230?5:3,sacs:combat.sacs.length===0?7:2,reclaim:combat.bossPhase===3?(combat.pressure>=60?7:2):combat.bossHP<3900?12:combat.pressure>=70?10:2,
 crush2:d<260?7:5,pulse:d>210?6:4,backfire:d>220?8:4,feast:d>240&&combat.pressure<65?7:2})[k]||1}));
 let roll=Math.random()*ranked.reduce((acc,v)=>acc+v.w,0);for(const row of ranked){roll-=row.w;if(roll<=0)return row.k}return ranked.at(-1)?.k||null;
}
function decideCombatAI(){
 const st=assessTactic();
 const eligible=MAIN_ATTACKS.filter(k=>canStart(k,false) && !(st.blocked && (k==='spit'||k==='charge')));
 // Never force an unsafe skill. A low-frequency retry lets mass and steering settle.
 if(!eligible.length){assessTactic();aiBrain.chosen=null;aiBrain.skips++;aiBrain.reason='안전한 사거리·조준 조건 미충족 · 재접근';return null}
 let picked;
 if(eligible.includes('barrage')&&combat.clock-aiBrain.lastBarrageAtV530>=[14,12,10][combat.bossPhase-1]){aiBrain.mode='BARRAGE';aiBrain.reason='전방위 원형 파동 탄막';return 'barrage';}
 const profile=phaseProfileV528(),signatures=combat.bossPhase===3?['feast']:combat.bossPhase===2?['crush2','pulse']:[];
 if(signatures.length&&combat.clock-aiBrain.lastSignatureAtV528>=profile.signatureEvery){
  const key=signatures[aiBrain.signatureIndexV528%signatures.length];
  if(eligible.includes(key)){aiBrain.mode=combat.bossPhase===3?'FRENZY COMBO':'SIEGE';aiBrain.reason='페이즈 고유 공격';return key;}
 }
 if(eligible.includes('charge')&&combat.clock-aiBrain.lastRushAtV528>=profile.rushEvery){aiBrain.mode='RUSH';aiBrain.reason='돌진으로 간격 붕괴';return 'charge';}
 
 if(aiBrain.advanced){picked=tacticalChoose(eligible)}
 else {const filtered=eligible.filter(k=>k!==combat.lastKey);picked=classicChoose(filtered.length?filtered:eligible,hypot(player.x-titan.x,player.y-titan.y));aiBrain.mode='CLASSIC';aiBrain.intent='기존 8단계 가중 추첨';aiBrain.reason='이전 방식 비교';aiBrain.chosen=picked;}
 return picked;
}
function renderAIThought(){
 const text=!combat.auto?'수동 스킬 시험 · ①~⑫ 버튼으로 직접 선택':
   !aiBrain.advanced?'기존 가중 추첨 모드 · 설정에서 고급 AI 전환 가능':
   `${aiBrain.intent} · ${aiBrain.chosen?ATTACKS[aiBrain.chosen].name:'공격 후보 탐색'}`;
 const status=$('aiTactic');status.textContent='◈ '+text;status.classList.toggle('manual',!combat.auto);
 const detail=$('aiThinking');if(!detail)return;
 const top=aiBrain.lastScores.slice(0,3).map(x=>`${ATTACKS[x.key].name} ${x.score.toFixed(1)}`).join(' / ');
 detail.textContent=`전술: ${aiBrain.mode} · ${aiBrain.reason||'관찰 중'}\n`+
  `움직임 ${aiBrain.speed.toFixed(0)}px/s · 사각 ${aiBrain.lineClear?'열림':'차폐'} · 벽 여유 ${aiBrain.wallProximity.toFixed(0)}px\n`+
  `후보 ${top||'없음'} · 선택 ${aiBrain.decisions} · 판단 대기 ${aiBrain.skips} · 연속기 변화 ${aiBrain.alternations}`;
 detail.style.whiteSpace='pre-line';
}
function canStart(k,force=false){
 if(combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0||k==='sacs'||k==='reclaim')return false;
 if(combat.dead||combat.paused||combat.ventTime>0||combat.phaseTransition>0||combat.phasePending||combat.playerHP<=0||combat.current||!ATTACKS[k]||combat.cooldowns[k]>0||((ATTACKS[k].phase||1)>combat.bossPhase))return false;
 if(k==='barrage'&&!barragePlanV530(combat.serial+1))return false;
 if(force)return true;
 if(!data.ai||ATTACKS[k].regression)return false;
 const d=hypot(player.x-titan.x,player.y-titan.y);
 const aim=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x),da=Math.abs(angleDelta(titan.heading,aim));
 if(k==='barrage')return data.ai&&combat.clock-aiBrain.lastBarrageAtV530>=[14,12,10][combat.bossPhase-1]&&!combat.projectiles.length;
 if(k==='backfire')return combat.bossPhase===3&&d>200&&d<620&&backfireFair();
 if(k==='feast')return combat.bossPhase===3&&d>195&&d<530&&segmentPassable(titan.x,titan.y,player.x,player.y,6)&&canEscapeCircle(player.x,player.y,ATTACKS.feast.slamRadius,ATTACKS.feast.lockBefore);
 if(k==='rear')return d<190&&da>2.0&&canEscapeMelee([titan.heading+clamp(angleDelta(titan.heading,aim),-.65,.65)+Math.PI],ATTACKS.rear.maxRange,ATTACKS.rear.halfAngle,ATTACKS.rear.lockBefore);
 if(k==='crush2')return combat.bossPhase>=2&&d<480&&d>120&&canEscapeCircle(player.x,player.y,ATTACKS.crush2.radius,ATTACKS.crush2.lockBefore);
 if(k==='pulse')return combat.bossPhase>=2&&d>160&&d<380&&pulseAutofair();
 if(k==='reclaim')return [...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].some(p=>p.ready&&!p.returning)&&(combat.bossHP<4050||combat.pressure>=65);
 if(k==='sacs')return d<650&&combat.sacs.length+ATTACKS.sacs.siteCount<=36;
 if(k==='charge')return d>55&&d<760&&measureChargeReach(titan.x,titan.y,aim,ATTACKS.charge.maxRange)>=16;
 if(da>.85)return false;
 if(k==='claw')return d<225&&canEscapeMelee([aim+((combat.serial+1)%2?1:-1)*.09],ATTACKS.claw.maxRange,ATTACKS.claw.halfAngle,ATTACKS.claw.lockBefore);
 if(k==='twin')return d>60&&d<205&&canEscapeMelee([aim-.30,aim+.30],ATTACKS.twin.maxRange,ATTACKS.twin.halfAngle,ATTACKS.twin.lockBefore);
 if(k==='spit')return d>40&&d<660&&canEscapeVolleyV530(aim);
 
 if(k==='mortar')return d<650&&d>110&&canEscapeCircle(player.x,player.y,ATTACKS.mortar.radius,ATTACKS.mortar.lockBefore);
 return false;
}
function startAttack(key,force=false){if(!canStart(key,force))return false;const a=ATTACKS[key];const dir=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x);
 combat.current={key,id:++combat.serial,stage:'WINDUP',t:0,total:0,locked:false,aim:titan.heading,
 targetX:player.x,targetY:player.y,forced:force,fired:0,side:(combat.serial%2?1:-1),travel:0,impactCount:0,sites:[],pulseHit:false,safeRecorded:false,slamDone:false,chargeFinished:false,originX:titan.x,originY:titan.y,secondDone:false,pressureApplied:false,recoverList:[],rushIndex:0,rushContactV529:false,rushWallV529:false};
 if(key==='barrage'){combat.current.barrageV530=barragePlanV530(combat.current.id);combat.stats.barrageBursts++;}
 titan.vx=titan.vy=0;titan.move=0;titan.path=[];titan.repathTimer=0;titan.lockTimer=0;titan.pivotTimer=0;
 combat.current.aim=['charge','feast'].includes(key)?dir:titan.heading+clamp(angleDelta(titan.heading,dir),-.65,.65);
 combat.phase='WINDUP';combat.stats.started++;recordEvent(`${a.name} 준비 (#${combat.serial})`);return true;
}
function cancelAttack(reason='취소',clearAll=false){if(combat.current){combat.stats.cancelled++;recordEvent(`${ATTACKS[combat.current.key].name} ${reason}`)}combat.current=null;combat.phase='IDLE';
 if(clearAll){combat.projectiles.length=0;combat.tissues.length=0;combat.hitEffects.length=0;combat.hitRecords.clear()} // persistent storage sacs survive manual attack switches
 titan.vx=titan.vy=0;titan.move=0;titan.path=[];titan.repathTimer=0;combat.nextDecision=.7;
 if(combat.phasePending&&combat.ventTime<=0)beginPhaseTransition();
}
function inSector(x,y,aim,range,halfAngle){const dx=x-titan.x,dy=y-titan.y,d=hypot(dx,dy);
 return d<=range+PLAYER_R&&Math.abs(angleDelta(aim,Math.atan2(dy,dx)))<=halfAngle+Math.asin(Math.min(.99,PLAYER_R/Math.max(PLAYER_R+.1,d)));
}
function resolveHit(hitId,damage,kind,strikePhase=combat.bossPhase){
 if(combat.hitRecords.has(hitId))return false;combat.hitRecords.add(hitId);
 if(!combat.damage){recordEvent(kind+' 판정 적중 · 피해 OFF');return true;}
 if(combat.invuln>0||host.player.inv>0||host.player.hp<=0||host.ended()){
  combat.stats.guardBlocks++;return false;
 }
 const phaseAmount=!kind.startsWith('STEP4')?(strikePhase===3?Math.ceil(damage*1.22):strikePhase===2?Math.ceil(damage*1.12):damage):damage;
 const amount=phaseAmount*Math.max(0,owner.dmg/24);
 const before=host.player.hp,beforeShield=host.player.shieldHP||0;
 host.damage(amount);combat.playerHP=host.player.hp;
 const applied=Math.max(0,before-host.player.hp)+Math.max(0,beforeShield-(host.player.shieldHP||0));
 if(applied>0){
  combat.invuln=.75;host.player.inv=Math.max(host.player.inv||0,.75);combat.damageFlash=.35;combat.stats.hits++;
  combat.hitEffects.push({x:player.x,y:player.y,t:.45,damage:Math.round(applied)});
  recordEvent(kind+' 적중 · 실제 적용 '+applied.toFixed(1));
 }
 if(host.player.hp<=0||host.ended()){cancelAttack('플레이어 전투 종료',true);combat.paused=true;}
 return applied>0;
}
// Capture real contact even when the locked rush has no remaining movement at a wall.
// This is the SAME 1.006 ellipse tolerance used by existing body-contact damage.
function markRushContactV534(c){
 if(!c||c.stage!=='ACTIVE'||!['charge','feast'].includes(c.key)||!owner||owner.dead||combat.dead||combat.paused||host.ended())return false;
 if(c.key==='feast'&&(c.t>=ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd||c.feastLandedV533))return false;
 const e=ellipse(),touch=hypot((player.x-e.x)/(e.rx+PLAYER_R),(player.y-e.y)/(e.ry+PLAYER_R))<=1.006;
 if(touch)c.rushContactV529=true;
 return touch;
}
function sweptBodyV528(ax,ay,bx,by){
 const e=ellipse(),rx=e.rx+PLAYER_R,ry=e.ry+PLAYER_R;
 return segCircle(ax/rx,(ay+data.oy)/ry,bx/rx,(by+data.oy)/ry,player.x/rx,player.y/ry,1);
}
function chargeWarningWidthV528(aim,r){const e=ellipse();return .05+Math.max(r+PLAYER_R,Math.hypot(e.rx*Math.sin(aim),e.ry*Math.cos(aim))+PLAYER_R+Math.abs(data.oy*Math.cos(aim)));}
function chargeWarningFrontV528(aim){const e=ellipse();return .05+Math.hypot(e.rx*Math.cos(aim),e.ry*Math.sin(aim))+PLAYER_R+Math.abs(data.oy*Math.sin(aim));}
function inRushWarningV528(c,px,py,a){return rushContainsV535(c,px,py);}
// Lock one hull-aware polyline. Wall slides are part of the warning, never live homing.
function rushSpaceV535(){
 const e=ellipse(),a=host.arena(),skin=.65;
 const bounds={l:e.rx+3,r:WORLD_W-e.rx-3,t:e.ry+12,b:WORLD_H-e.ry-12};
 if(a?.active){bounds.l=Math.max(bounds.l,a.l+a.half+e.rx+1.25);bounds.r=Math.min(bounds.r,a.r-a.half-e.rx-1.25);
  bounds.t=Math.max(bounds.t,a.t+a.half+e.ry-data.oy+1.25);bounds.b=Math.min(bounds.b,a.b-a.half-e.ry-data.oy-1.25);}
 const blocks=[];
 for(const r of obstacles){
  // The arena boundary is already represented exactly by the four hull-center limits.
  if(a?.active&&(r.x+r.w<=a.l+a.half+.01||r.x>=a.r-a.half-.01||r.y+r.h<=a.t+a.half+.01||r.y>=a.b-a.half-.01))continue;
  blocks.push({l:r.x-e.rx-skin,r:r.x+r.w+e.rx+skin,t:r.y-e.ry-data.oy-skin,b:r.y+r.h+e.ry-data.oy+skin});
 }
 return {bounds,blocks};
}
function castRushV535(x,y,ux,uy,distance,space){
 const b=space.bounds;let best={d:distance,wall:false,nx:0,ny:0,block:null};
 function keep(d,nx,ny,block=null){if(d>=-1e-6&&d<=best.d+1e-7){best={d:Math.max(0,d),wall:true,nx,ny,block};}}
 if(ux>1e-8)keep((b.r-x)/ux,-1,0);else if(ux<-1e-8)keep((b.l-x)/ux,1,0);
 if(uy>1e-8)keep((b.b-y)/uy,0,-1);else if(uy<-1e-8)keep((b.t-y)/uy,0,1);
 for(const r of space.blocks){
  let lo=-Infinity,hi=Infinity,nx=0,ny=0,valid=true;
  for(const [p,v,min,max,axis] of [[x,ux,r.l,r.r,0],[y,uy,r.t,r.b,1]]){
   if(Math.abs(v)<1e-8){if(p<min||p>max){valid=false;break;}continue;}
   const t1=(min-p)/v,t2=(max-p)/v,near=Math.min(t1,t2),far=Math.max(t1,t2);
   if(near>lo){lo=near;nx=axis===0?(v>0?-1:1):0;ny=axis===1?(v>0?-1:1):0;}hi=Math.min(hi,far);
  }
  if(valid&&hi>=Math.max(0,lo)&&lo>=-1e-6)keep(lo,nx,ny,r);
 }
 return best;
}
function buildRushRouteV535(c,budget){
 const space=rushSpaceV535(),b=space.bounds;
 let x=clamp(c.originX??titan.x,b.l,b.r),y=clamp(c.originY??titan.y,b.t,b.b);
 const points=[{x,y}],target={x:c.targetX??player.x,y:c.targetY??player.y};
 let ux=Math.cos(c.aim),uy=Math.sin(c.aim),left=budget,total=0,slides=0,wall=false;
 function add(nx,ny){const d=hypot(nx-x,ny-y);if(d<1e-5)return;points.push({x:nx,y:ny});total+=d;left-=d;x=nx;y=ny;}
 for(let bend=0;bend<7&&left>1e-4;bend++){
  const hit=castRushV535(x,y,ux,uy,left,space),travel=Math.max(0,Math.min(left,hit.d-(hit.wall?.12:0)));
  add(x+ux*travel,y+uy*travel);
  if(!hit.wall){wall=false;break;}
  wall=true;
  // Continue along the surface instead of classifying this first touch as a crash.
  let tx=hit.nx?0:ux,ty=hit.ny?0:uy;
  if(hypot(tx,ty)<1e-6){
   tx=hit.nx?0:Math.sign(target.x-x);ty=hit.ny?0:Math.sign(target.y-y);
  }
  if(hypot(tx,ty)<1e-6&&hit.block){
   const r=hit.block;
   if(hit.nx){const up=y-r.t+hypot(target.x-x,target.y-r.t),down=r.b-y+hypot(target.x-x,target.y-r.b);ty=up<=down?-1:1;}
   else{const leftCost=x-r.l+hypot(target.x-r.l,target.y-y),rightCost=r.r-x+hypot(target.x-r.r,target.y-y);tx=leftCost<=rightCost?-1:1;}
  }
  const tangent=hypot(tx,ty);if(tangent<1e-6)break;tx/=tangent;ty/=tangent;
  if(slides>0&&!hit.block)break; // A second arena face is the terminal corner, not a bounce.
  slides++;
  if(hit.block){
   // Slide past a static structure corner, then resume toward the locked target.
   const r=hit.block,edge=tx?(tx>0?r.r+.4:r.l-.4):(ty>0?r.b+.4:r.t-.4);
   const toCorner=Math.max(0,tx?(edge-x)/tx:(edge-y)/ty),span=Math.min(left,toCorner);
   const sideHit=castRushV535(x,y,tx,ty,span,space),run=Math.max(0,Math.min(span,sideHit.d-(sideHit.wall?.12:0)));
   add(x+tx*run,y+ty*run);if(sideHit.wall||left<=1e-4){wall=true;break;}
   const dx=target.x-x,dy=target.y-y,dist=hypot(dx,dy);if(dist<1){ux=tx;uy=ty;}else{ux=dx/dist;uy=dy/dist;}
   continue;
  }
  // The full remaining budget follows this arena side. Direction remains fixed after lock.
  const end=castRushV535(x,y,tx,ty,left,space),run=Math.max(0,Math.min(left,end.d-(end.wall?.12:0)));
  add(x+tx*run,y+ty*run);wall=true;break;
 }
 return {points,total,wall,slides,bounds:b,index:1,travel:0};
}
function rushRouteV535(c,preview=false){
 return c.routeV535||buildRushRouteV535(c,ATTACKS[c.key].maxRange);
}
function rushContainsV535(c,px,py){
 const r=rushRouteV535(c),e=ellipse(),rx=e.rx+PLAYER_R,ry=e.ry+PLAYER_R;
 if(r.points.length===1)return hypot((px-r.points[0].x)/rx,(py-r.points[0].y-data.oy)/ry)<=1.006;
 return r.points.some((p,i)=>i>0&&segCircle(r.points[i-1].x/rx,(r.points[i-1].y+data.oy)/ry,p.x/rx,(p.y+data.oy)/ry,px/rx,py/ry,1.006));
}
function rushRampIntegralV535(t){t=Math.max(0,t);return t<=.4?.32*t+.85*t*t:.264+t-.4;}
function advanceRushV535(c,dt,feast=false){
 markRushContactV534(c);if(c.chargeFinished)return;
 const a=ATTACKS[c.key],r=c.routeV535||(c.routeV535=buildRushRouteV535(c,a.maxRange));
 let distance=Math.max(0,a.chargeSpeed*(rushRampIntegralV535(c.t+dt)-rushRampIntegralV535(c.t)));
 const beforeX=titan.x,beforeY=titan.y;
 for(let guard=0;distance>1e-7&&r.index<r.points.length&&guard<256;guard++){
  const p=r.points[r.index],dx=p.x-titan.x,dy=p.y-titan.y,d=hypot(dx,dy);
  if(d<1e-6){r.index++;continue;}
  const step=Math.min(8,d,distance),nx=titan.x+dx/d*step,ny=titan.y+dy/d*step,ox=titan.x,oy=titan.y;
  titan.x=nx;titan.y=ny;c.travel+=step;r.travel=c.travel;distance-=step;
  c.rushHeadingV535=Math.atan2(dy,dx);titan.heading=c.rushHeadingV535;
  if(sweptBodyV528(ox,oy,nx,ny)){
   c.rushContactV529=true;if(feast)hitFeast(c,'돌진');else resolveHit(`${c.id}:charge:${c.rushIndex||0}`,a.damage,'공성 돌진');
  }
  if(d-step<1e-6)r.index++;
  if(combat.paused)break;
 }
 const finished=r.index>=r.points.length||c.travel>=r.total-1e-5;
 if(finished){c.chargeFinished=true;c.rushWallV529=r.wall;if(r.wall){combat.stats.chargeWalls++;recordEvent('돌진 경로 끝 · 마지막 접촉 판정');}}
 const moved=hypot(titan.x-beforeX,titan.y-beforeY),v=dt>0?moved/dt:0,heading=c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim;
 titan.vx=finished?0:Math.cos(heading)*v;titan.vy=finished?0:Math.sin(heading)*v;titan.move=finished?0:feast?.9:.95;titan.anim+=dt*(feast?9:10);
}
function renderRushWarningV535(c,outlineOnly=false){
 if(c.stage==='RECOVERY')return;
 const r=rushRouteV535(c),feast=c.key==='feast',progress=c.stage==='WINDUP'?clamp(c.t/ATTACKS[c.key].windup,0,1):1,a=host.arena();
 ctx.save();ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
 if(a?.active){const p=scPos(a.l+a.half,a.t+a.half);ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(p.x,p.y,a.r-a.l-a.half*2,a.b-a.t-a.half*2);ctx.clip();}
 for(let i=1;i<r.points.length;i++){
  const p=scPos(r.points[i-1].x,r.points[i-1].y+data.oy),q=scPos(r.points[i].x,r.points[i].y+data.oy),dx=q.x-p.x,dy=q.y-p.y,len=hypot(dx,dy),ux=dx/len,uy=dy/len;
  const e=ellipse(),width=Math.hypot(e.rx*uy,e.ry*ux)+PLAYER_R;
  if(!outlineOnly){ctx.strokeStyle=feast?'rgba(112,30,136,.16)':'rgba(192,48,18,.13)';ctx.lineWidth=width*2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y);ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.globalAlpha=outlineOnly?.72:.80;ctx.strokeStyle=feast?'#adf460':'#ff9867';ctx.lineWidth=c.locked?1.8:1.3;ctx.setLineDash(c.locked?[]:feast?[4,5]:[10,6]);
  for(const sign of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x-uy*width*sign,p.y+ux*width*sign);ctx.lineTo(q.x-uy*width*sign,q.y+ux*width*sign);ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.globalAlpha=.50+progress*.30;ctx.strokeStyle=feast?'#bc81ea':'#ffe5b3';ctx.lineWidth=feast?2.4:1.25;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y);ctx.stroke();
  // Ivory teeth for feast, short paired claw scores for siege: different silhouettes, not just arrows.
  for(let d=30;d<len-14;d+=feast?62:48){const x=p.x+ux*d,y=p.y+uy*d;ctx.strokeStyle=feast?'#d2ffae':'#ffbe81';ctx.lineWidth=feast?2.5:1.5;
   for(const sign of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();if(feast){ctx.moveTo(x-uy*24*sign-ux*11,y+ux*24*sign-uy*11);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x-uy*14*sign,y+ux*14*sign,x+ux*8-uy*5*sign,y+uy*8+ux*5*sign);}else{ctx.moveTo(x-uy*19*sign-ux*7,y+ux*19*sign-uy*7);ctx.lineTo(x-uy*19*sign+ux*7,y+ux*19*sign+uy*7);}ctx.stroke();}
  }
 }
 if(r.points.length>2){const p=scPos(r.points[1].x,r.points[1].y);ctx.globalAlpha=.85;ctx.strokeStyle=feast?'#d6ff9f':'#ffd6a0';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,7,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.restore();
}

function measureChargeReach(x,y,aim,max){return buildRushRouteV535({originX:x,originY:y,aim,targetX:player.x,targetY:player.y},max).total;}
function lockAttack(c){if(c.locked)return;c.locked=true;combat.stats.locks++;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];
 if(a.kind==='charge'||a.kind==='feast'){c.originX=titan.x;c.originY=titan.y;c.routeV535=buildRushRouteV535(c,a.maxRange);c.chargeReach=c.routeV535.total;c.rushHeadingV535=c.aim;}
 if(a.kind==='feast'){const p=c.routeV535.points[c.routeV535.points.length-1];c.finalX=p.x;c.finalY=p.y;}
 if(a.kind==='backfire')c.sites=backfireSites(c);
 if(a.kind==='mortar'||a.kind==='sacs'){
  const x=clamp(c.targetX,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_W-a.radius-PLAYER_R),y=clamp(c.targetY,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_H-a.radius-PLAYER_R);
  let ox=-Math.sin(c.aim)*1.75*UNIT*c.side,oy=Math.cos(c.aim)*1.75*UNIT*c.side;
  let x2=clamp(x+ox,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_W-a.radius-PLAYER_R),y2=clamp(y+oy,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_H-a.radius-PLAYER_R);
  // Flip the displacement near arena edges, preserving separation of the sites.
  if(hypot(x2-x,y2-y)<2*a.radius+2*PLAYER_R+8){x2=clamp(x-ox,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_W-a.radius-PLAYER_R);y2=clamp(y-oy,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_H-a.radius-PLAYER_R)}
  c.sites=[{x,y,r:a.radius,impacted:false},{x:x2,y:y2,r:a.radius,impacted:false}];
  if(a.kind==='mortar'&&a.siteCount===3)c.sites.push({x:clamp(x-ox,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_W-a.radius-PLAYER_R),y:clamp(y-oy,a.radius+PLAYER_R,WORLD_H-a.radius-PLAYER_R),r:a.radius,impacted:false});
 }
 if(a.kind==='sacs')c.sites=resourceSitesV528(c,a.siteCount);
 if(a.kind==='reclaim')c.recoverList=[...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].filter(p=>p.ready&&!p.returning).map(p=>({id:p.id}));
 recordEvent(`${a.name} 목표 고정 · 마지막 추적 종료`);
}
function launchSpit(c,idx){
 const a=ATTACKS.spit,angle=c.aim+a.spread[idx%a.spread.length],muzzle=c.muzzleV530??(c.muzzleV530=Math.min(60,Math.max(20,hypot(c.targetX-titan.x,c.targetY-titan.y)-PLAYER_R-16))),ox=titan.x+Math.cos(angle)*muzzle,oy=titan.y+Math.sin(angle)*muzzle;
 combat.projectiles.push({id:`${c.id}:spit:${idx}`,x:ox,y:oy,px:ox,py:oy,vx:Math.cos(angle)*a.projSpeed,vy:Math.sin(angle)*a.projSpeed,dist:0,r:11,damage:a.damage,strikePhase:combat.bossPhase,life:a.maxRange/a.projSpeed});
 combat.stats.projectiles++;c.fired++;recordEvent(`소화액 ${idx+1}/${a.count} 발사`);
}

function canEscapeVolleyV530(aim){
 const a=ATTACKS.spit,travel=Math.max(0,escapeSpeed()*a.lockBefore-8);
 for(let i=0;i<24;i++){
  const turn=2*Math.PI*i/24,x=player.x+Math.cos(turn)*travel,y=player.y+Math.sin(turn)*travel;
  if(!playerDodgeCorridorClear(player.x,player.y,x,y))continue;
  if(a.spread.every(offset=>{const ux=Math.cos(aim+offset),uy=Math.sin(aim+offset);return !segCircle(titan.x,titan.y,titan.x+ux*(a.maxRange+60),titan.y+uy*(a.maxRange+60),x,y,11+PLAYER_R+3);}))return true;
 }
 return false;
}
function barragePlanV530(serial){
 const a=ATTACKS.barrage,ar=host.arena(),origin={x:titan.x,y:titan.y};
 const d=hypot(player.x-origin.x,player.y-origin.y);if(d<112+PLAYER_R)return null;
 const aim=Math.atan2(player.y-origin.y,player.x-origin.x),sign=serial%2?1:-1;
 const corners=ar?.active?[[ar.l,ar.t],[ar.r,ar.t],[ar.l,ar.b],[ar.r,ar.b]]:[[view.camX,view.camY],[view.camX+view.W,view.camY],[view.camX,view.camY+view.H],[view.camX+view.W,view.camY+view.H]];
 const range=Math.max(500,...corners.map(p=>hypot(p[0]-origin.x,p[1]-origin.y)))+70;
 return {originX:origin.x,originY:origin.y,range,rotation:sign*.075,base:aim+.07*sign,spokes:a.spokes,speed:a.projSpeed};
}
function barrageAnglesV530(plan,wave){
 const result=[];
 for(let i=0;i<plan.spokes;i++)result.push(plan.base+i*Math.PI*2/plan.spokes+wave*plan.rotation);
 return result;
}
function launchBarrageV530(c,wave){
 const a=ATTACKS.barrage,p=c.barrageV530;if(!p)return;
 for(const angle of barrageAnglesV530(p,wave)){
  const ux=Math.cos(angle),uy=Math.sin(angle),x=p.originX+ux*58,y=p.originY+uy*58;
  combat.projectiles.push({id:`${c.id}:barrage:${wave}:${combat.stats.barrageProjectiles}`,barrageIdV530:c.id,waveV530:wave,x,y,px:x,py:y,vx:ux*p.speed,vy:uy*p.speed,dist:0,r:a.radius,damage:a.damage,strikePhase:combat.bossPhase,life:p.range/p.speed});
  combat.stats.barrageProjectiles++;combat.stats.projectiles++;
 }
 c.fired=wave+1;combat.stats.barrageWaves++;
 fx11Wave(p.originX,p.originY,66,'#fb7d91',.32,2);fx11Sound('spit',.5);
 recordEvent(`생체 폭풍 ${wave+1}/${a.waveCount}파 · 전방위 발사`);
}

function renderBarrageV530(c,outlineOnly){
 if(c.stage!=='WINDUP')return;
 const p=c.barrageV530;if(!p)return;const o=scPos(p.originX,p.originY);
 ctx.globalAlpha=.44;ctx.setLineDash([6,8]);ctx.lineWidth=1;ctx.strokeStyle='#ed7c9170';
 for(const angle of barrageAnglesV530(p,0)){
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(o.x+Math.cos(angle)*60,o.y+Math.sin(angle)*60);
  ctx.lineTo(o.x+Math.cos(angle)*p.range,o.y+Math.sin(angle)*p.range);ctx.stroke();
 }
 ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.strokeStyle='#f3a1ac';ctx.lineWidth=2;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(o.x,o.y,64+8*c.t/ATTACKS.barrage.windup,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
}
function renderProjectilesV530(){
 // Two flat fill passes for dense barrages; avoid a blur and gradient per bullet.
 const colors=['#ed728b','#f4b078','#cc89e8'];
 for(let k=0;k<3;k++){
  ctx.fillStyle=colors[k];ctx.beginPath();
  for(const p of combat.projectiles){if(p.barrageIdV530===undefined||p.waveV530%3!==k)continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);if(q.x<-20||q.y<-20||q.x>view.W+20||q.y>view.H+20)continue;const angle=Math.atan2(p.vy,p.vx);ctx.moveTo(q.x+Math.cos(angle)*p.r*1.55,q.y+Math.sin(angle)*p.r*1.55);ctx.ellipse(q.x,q.y,p.r*1.55,p.r*.82,angle,0,Math.PI*2);}
  ctx.fill();
 }
 ctx.fillStyle='#fff2d7';ctx.beginPath();
 for(const p of combat.projectiles){if(p.barrageIdV530===undefined)continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);if(q.x<-20||q.y<-20||q.x>view.W+20||q.y>view.H+20)continue;ctx.moveTo(q.x+2.5,q.y);ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,2.2,0,Math.PI*2);}ctx.fill();
 for(const p of combat.projectiles){if(p.barrageIdV530!==undefined)continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);if(q.x<-25||q.y<-25||q.x>view.W+25||q.y>view.H+25)continue;
  ctx.shadowColor='#ff7c3f';ctx.shadowBlur=18;const grad=ctx.createRadialGradient(q.x-3,q.y-3,0,q.x,q.y,15);grad.addColorStop(0,'#fff9c3');grad.addColorStop(.4,'#ffae51');grad.addColorStop(1,'#af3d1b');ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,p.r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 }
}
function segCircle(ax,ay,bx,by,cx,cy,r){const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,len2=dx*dx+dy*dy,t=len2>1e-9?clamp(((cx-ax)*dx+(cy-ay)*dy)/len2,0,1):0;
 return hypot(cx-(ax+dx*t),cy-(ay+dy*t))<=r;
}
function tickProjectiles(dt){for(let i=combat.projectiles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=combat.projectiles[i];p.px=p.x;p.py=p.y;const step=Math.min(p.life,dt);p.x+=p.vx*step;p.y+=p.vy*step;p.dist+=Math.hypot(p.vx,p.vy)*step;p.life-=step;
  let hitWall=!segmentPassable(p.px,p.py,p.x,p.y,0,true);if(!hitWall)for(const w of obstacles){const nearX=clamp(p.x,w.x,w.x+w.w),nearY=clamp(p.y,w.y,w.y+w.h);if(hypot(p.x-nearX,p.y-nearY)<p.r){hitWall=true;break}}
  const touched=segCircle(p.px,p.py,p.x,p.y,player.x,player.y,p.r+PLAYER_R);
  if(touched&&!hitWall){resolveHit(p.id,p.damage,'소화액 탄환',p.strikePhase);combat.projectiles.splice(i,1);continue}
  if(hitWall||p.life<=1e-7||p.x<0||p.x>WORLD_W||p.y<0||p.y>WORLD_H){combat.projectiles.splice(i,1);if(!hitWall)combat.stats.avoids++}
 }}
function makeTissue(c,site,i){} // V529: regeneration fluid comes only from a wall-crash groggy batch.
function mortarImpact(c,i){const a=ATTACKS.mortar,site=c.sites[i];if(!site||site.impacted)return;
 site.impacted=true;c.impactCount++;combat.stats.mortarImpacts++;
 combat.hitEffects.push({x:site.x,y:site.y,t:.40,damage:'✦'});
 if(hypot(player.x-site.x,player.y-site.y)<=site.r+PLAYER_R){resolveHit(`${c.id}:mortar:${i}`,a.damage,`곡사포 ${i+1}착탄`)}else combat.stats.avoids++;
 if(combat.paused)return;makeTissue(c,site,i);recordEvent(`생체낭 ${i+1}/${a.siteCount}착탄`);
}
function tickTissues(dt){tickResourcesV528(combat.tissues,dt,'tissue');}
function stepCharge(c,dt){advanceRushV535(c,dt,false);}
// Pressure exists only for Phase I here. It may trigger ventilation ONLY at attack completion.
function addPressure(c){if(c.pressureApplied)return;c.pressureApplied=true;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];
 if(a.regression)return;
 combat.pressure=Math.min(100,combat.pressure+(a.pressure||0));
 recordEvent(`대사 압력 +${a.pressure||0} → ${combat.pressure}/100`);
}
function damageBoss(amount=400,source='진단 피해'){
 if(!owner||combat.dead)return 0;
 const inflicted=Math.min(owner.hp,Math.max(0,amount)*incomingMultiplier(owner));
 owner.hp-=inflicted;combat.bossHP=owner.hp/owner.maxHp*4200;
 if(inflicted>0){motionHit();host.hitMotion(owner);}
 updatePhaseTarget();combat.bossDamageEvents.push({at:combat.clock,base:amount,applied:inflicted,source});
 if(owner.hp<=0)host.kill(owner);
 else if(combat.phasePending&&!combat.current&&combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)beginPhaseTransition();
 return inflicted;
}
function startVent(){if(combat.ventTime>0)return;
 combat.ventTime=combat.ventDuration;combat.ventCount++;combat.stats.vents++;
 combat.pressure=100;combat.phase='VENT';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;
 recordEvent(`압력 한계 → 강제 배기 ${combat.ventDuration.toFixed(1)}초 · 받는 피해 ×${combat.bossPhase===3?'1.30':combat.bossPhase===2?'1.25':'1.20'}`);
}
function tickVent(dt){if(combat.ventTime<=0)return;
 combat.ventTime=Math.max(0,combat.ventTime-dt);
 if(combat.ventTime===0){combat.pressure=0;combat.phase='IDLE';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;recordEvent('강제 배기 종료 · 압력 0');if(combat.phasePending)beginPhaseTransition();}
}
function newResourceV528(id,x,y,r){
 const p=phaseProfileV528();return {id,x,y,r,resourceV528:true,life:p.reclaimDelay+p.returnTime,age:0,grace:.30,ready:false,returning:false,reclaimDelay:p.reclaimDelay,returnTime:p.returnTime,heal:p.heal,strikePhase:combat.bossPhase};
}
function resourceSitesV528(c,count){
 const a=host.arena(),pad=30,bounds=a?.active?{l:a.l+a.half+pad,r:a.r-a.half-pad,t:a.t+a.half+pad,b:a.b-a.half-pad}:{l:pad,r:WORLD_W-pad,t:pad,b:WORLD_H-pad};
 const sites=[];
 for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const angle=c.aim+i*2.399963229728653+c.side*.40,rad=68+Math.floor(i/3)*42;
  const x=clamp(c.targetX+Math.cos(angle)*rad,bounds.l,bounds.r),y=clamp(c.targetY+Math.sin(angle)*rad,bounds.t,bounds.b);
  if(obstacles.some(o=>x>o.x-20&&x<o.x+o.w+20&&y>o.y-20&&y<o.y+o.h+20))continue;
  sites.push({x,y,r:ATTACKS.sacs.radius,impacted:false});
 }
 return sites;
}
function spawnSac(c,site,index){
 if(combat.sacs.length>=36)return;
 combat.sacs.push(newResourceV528(`sac:${++combat.sacSerial}`,site.x,site.y,ATTACKS.sacs.radius));combat.stats.sacsPlaced++;
 recordEvent(`재생 진액 ${index+1}/${c.sites.length} 살포 · 밟아 파괴 가능`);
}
function beginResourceReturnV528(p){
 if(p.returning)return;p.returning=true;p.returnT=0;p.returnX=p.x;p.returnY=p.y;combat.stats.resourceReturns++;
}
function popResourceV528(p){
 combat.stats.resourceStomps++;combat.hitEffects.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,t:.27,damage:'✦'});
 fx11Burst(p.x,p.y,'sacs',8,.32);recordEvent('진액 파괴 · 보스 회복 차단');
}
function tickResourcesV528(list,dt,kind){
 if(!owner||owner.dead||combat.dead||host.ended()){list.length=0;return;}
 const previous=resourcePrevV528||player;
 for(let i=list.length-1;i>=0;i--){
  const p=list[i];p.age+=dt;p.grace=Math.max(0,p.grace-dt);p.life=Math.max(0,p.reclaimDelay+p.returnTime-p.age);p.ready=!p.crashBatchV529&&p.age>=Math.max(2.8,p.reclaimDelay-.5);
  // Foot contact wins over absorption on the same simulation tick. Dash paths are swept.
  if(p.grace<=0&&segCircle(previous.x,previous.y,player.x,player.y,p.x,p.y,p.r+PLAYER_R)){
   list.splice(i,1);popResourceV528(p);continue;
  }
  if(p.crashBatchV529&&!p.returning)continue;
  if(!p.returning&&p.age>=p.reclaimDelay)beginResourceReturnV528(p);
  if(!p.returning)continue;
  p.returnT+=dt;const progress=clamp(p.returnT/p.returnTime,0,1),smooth=progress*progress*(3-2*progress);
  const ox=p.x,oy=p.y;p.x=p.returnX+(titan.x-p.returnX)*smooth;p.y=p.returnY+(titan.y-p.returnY)*smooth;
  if(segCircle(ox,oy,p.x,p.y,player.x,player.y,p.r+PLAYER_R)){
   list.splice(i,1);popResourceV528(p);continue;
  }
  if(progress<1)continue;
  list.splice(i,1);const before=combat.bossHP;
  combat.bossHP=Math.min(combat.bossMaxHP,Math.max(0,combat.bossHP)+combat.bossMaxHP*p.heal);
  const healed=combat.bossHP-before;combat.stats.resourceHealed+=healed;
  if(p.crashBatchV529&&combat.crashV529?.id===p.crashBatchV529){combat.crashV529.recovered++;combat.stats.crashRecoveries++;}
  if(kind==='tissue')combat.stats.tissuesRecovered++;else combat.stats.sacsRecovered++;
  combat.pressure=Math.max(0,combat.pressure-3);
  fx11Burst(titan.x,titan.y-35,'reclaim',7,.32);
  recordEvent(`진액 회수 · 최대 HP ${(p.heal*100).toFixed(2)}% 재생`);
 }
}
function tickSacs(dt){tickResourcesV528(combat.sacs,dt,'sac');}
function tickReclaim(c,span){
 for(const item of c.recoverList){const p=[...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].find(s=>s.id===item.id);if(p&&!p.returning)beginResourceReturnV528(p);}
}
function drawHitCone(c,offset,damage,index){const a=ATTACKS[c.key],dir=c.aim+offset;
 combat.stats.twinStrikes++;
 if(inSector(player.x,player.y,dir,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle))resolveHit(`${c.id}:twin:${index}`,damage,`${a.name} ${index+1}타`);
 else combat.stats.avoids++;
}
function enterActive(c){const a=ATTACKS[c.key];lockAttack(c);c.stage='ACTIVE';c.t=0;combat.phase='ACTIVE';addPressure(c);recordEvent(`${a.name} 실행`);motionImpact(a.kind==='charge'||a.kind==='feast'?.62:.43);
 if(a.kind==='cone'){if(inSector(player.x,player.y,c.aim+c.side*.09,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle))resolveHit(`${c.id}:claw`,a.damage,a.name);else combat.stats.avoids++}
 else if(a.kind==='circle'){if(hypot(player.x-c.targetX,player.y-c.targetY)<=a.radius+PLAYER_R){if(c.key==='crush2')combat.stats.crush2Hits++;resolveHit(`${c.id}:circle`,a.damage,a.name)}else combat.stats.avoids++}
 else if(a.kind==='pulse'){c.pulseHit=false;recordEvent('지반 맥동 · 두 측면 균열이 안전');}
 else if(a.kind==='twin'){drawHitCone(c,-.30*c.side,a.damage,0)}
 else if(a.kind==='rear'){combat.stats.rearStrikes++;if(inSector(player.x,player.y,c.aim+Math.PI,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle))resolveHit(`${c.id}:rear`,a.damage,a.name);else combat.stats.avoids++}
 else if(a.kind==='barrage'){launchBarrageV530(c,0)}
 else if(a.kind==='volley'){launchSpit(c,0)}
 else if(a.kind==='mortar'){mortarImpact(c,0)}
 else if(a.kind==='backfire'){impactBackfire(c,0)}
 else if(a.kind==='feast'){recordEvent('최종 포식 1/3 · 고정 방향 공성 돌진');}
 else if(a.kind==='sacs'){for(const [i,site] of c.sites.entries())spawnSac(c,site,i)}
 else if(a.kind==='reclaim'){if(!c.recoverList.length)recordEvent('회수 가능한 저장낭 없음 · 빈 회수 동작')}
}
function completeAttack(c){
 if(combat.current!==c)return;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];combat.cooldowns[c.key]=a.cooldown;combat.lastKey=c.key;combat.stats.completed++;
 recordEvent(`${a.name} 완료 · 재사용 ${a.cooldown.toFixed(1)}초`);combat.current=null;combat.phase='IDLE';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;
 if(combat.phasePending){if(combat.ventTime<=0)beginPhaseTransition();}else if(combat.pressure>=100)startVent();
}
function nextRushV528(c){
 c.rushIndex++;c.rushContactV529=false;c.rushWallV529=false;c.stage='WINDUP';c.t=0;c.locked=false;c.travel=0;c.chargeFinished=false;c.warningThreatened=false;c.evasionAwarded=false;
 c.aim=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x);c.originX=titan.x;c.originY=titan.y;c.targetX=player.x;c.targetY=player.y;
 delete c.routeV535;delete c.rushHeadingV535;delete c.feastLandedV533;delete c.chargeReach;delete c.finalX;delete c.finalY;titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;combat.phase='WINDUP';combat.stats.chargeRushes++;
 recordEvent('폭주 연속 돌진 · 새 경고 후 방향 고정');
}
function tickAttack(dt){const c=combat.current;if(!c)return;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];let remaining=dt;
 for(let limit=0;remaining>1e-7&&limit<12&&combat.current===c;limit++){
  if(c.stage==='WINDUP'){
   const untilLock=Math.max(0,a.windup-a.lockBefore),turning=Math.atan2(player.y-titan.y,player.x-titan.x);
   if(!c.locked){if(c.t<untilLock-1e-7){const span=Math.min(remaining,untilLock-c.t);const maxTurn=(95*Math.PI/180)*span;
     if(a.kind!=='rear'){c.aim+=clamp(angleDelta(c.aim,turning),-maxTurn,maxTurn);c.targetX=player.x;c.targetY=player.y;rotateToward(turning,span);}c.t+=span;c.total+=span;remaining-=span;
     if(c.t+1e-7>=untilLock)lockAttack(c);continue;
    }lockAttack(c);}
   const span=Math.min(remaining,a.windup-c.t);c.t+=span;c.total+=span;remaining-=span;
   if(c.t+1e-7>=a.windup)enterActive(c);
  }else if(c.stage==='ACTIVE'){
   const span=Math.min(remaining,a.active-c.t);
   if(a.kind==='charge')stepCharge(c,span);
   if(a.kind==='feast'){tickFeast(c,c.t,span);if(combat.current!==c||c.stage!=='ACTIVE'){remaining-=span;continue;}}
   if(a.kind==='reclaim')tickReclaim(c,span);
   if(a.kind==='pulse')tickPulse(c,c.t,c.t+span);
   const nextT=c.t+span;
   if(a.kind==='barrage'){for(let i=c.fired;i<a.waveCount;i++){if(nextT+1e-7>=i*a.interval)launchBarrageV530(c,i);else break}}
   if(a.kind==='volley'){for(let i=c.fired;i<a.count;i++){if(nextT+1e-7>=i*a.interval)launchSpit(c,i);else break}}
   if(a.kind==='mortar'){for(let i=1;i<a.siteCount;i++)if(nextT+1e-7>=i*a.interval)mortarImpact(c,i);}
   if(a.kind==='backfire'){for(let i=1;i<a.siteCount;i++)if(nextT+1e-7>=i*a.interval)impactBackfire(c,i);}
   if(a.kind==='twin'&&!c.secondDone&&nextT+1e-7>=a.interval){c.secondDone=true;drawHitCone(c,.30*c.side,a.damage,1)}
   c.t=nextT;c.total+=span;remaining-=span;
   if(c.t+1e-7>=a.active||(a.kind==='charge'&&c.chargeFinished)){
    if(a.kind==='charge'&&(c.rushIndex||0)+1<(a.rushes||1)&&!combat.paused){nextRushV528(c);continue;}
    if(a.kind==='charge'&&c.rushWallV529&&!c.rushContactV529){beginCrashV529(c);break;}
    c.stage='RECOVERY';c.t=0;combat.phase='RECOVERY';titan.vx=titan.vy=0;titan.move=0;recordEvent(`${a.name} 타격 종료 · 회복`);
   }
  }else if(c.stage==='RECOVERY'){
   const span=Math.min(remaining,a.recovery-c.t);c.t+=span;c.total+=span;remaining-=span;
   if(c.t+1e-7>=a.recovery)completeAttack(c);
  }else break;
 }
}
// A groggy batch is transactional: six fluid drops, one recall outcome, one possible penalty.
const CRASH_RULE_V529=Object.freeze({stun:5,count:6,heal:.01,penalty:.05,returnTime:.80,hitTime:.52});

function crashSitesV529(){
 const a=host.arena(),pad=36,bounds=a?.active?{l:a.l+a.half+pad,r:a.r-a.half-pad,t:a.t+a.half+pad,b:a.b-a.half-pad}:{l:pad,r:WORLD_W-pad,t:pad,b:WORLD_H-pad};
 const cx=a?.active?(a.l+a.r)/2:WORLD_W/2,cy=a?.active?(a.t+a.b)/2:WORLD_H/2,aim=Math.atan2(cy-titan.y,cx-titan.x),sites=[],e=ellipse();
 const near=clamp(view.W*.35,128,170),far=clamp(view.W*.62,205,270),ux=Math.cos(aim),uy=Math.sin(aim);
 function accept(x,y){
  if(x<bounds.l||x>bounds.r||y<bounds.t||y>bounds.b)return false;
  const d=hypot(x-titan.x,y-titan.y);if(d<near||d>far)return false;
  if(((x-e.x)/(e.rx+PLAYER_R+27))**2+((y-e.y)/(e.ry+PLAYER_R+27))**2<1)return false;
  if(sites.some(s=>hypot(x-s.x,y-s.y)<50))return false;
  if(obstacles.some(o=>x>o.x-22&&x<o.x+o.w+22&&y>o.y-22&&y<o.y+o.h+22))return false;
  sites.push({x,y});return true;
 }
 for(let pass=0;pass<3&&sites.length<6;pass++)for(let i=0;i<320&&sites.length<6;i++){
  // Jitter both axes, rather than placing equidistant points on a circle.
  const forward=(pass===0?.38:-.9)*far+Math.random()*(pass===0?.62:1.8)*far;
  const side=(Math.random()*2-1)*far*.82;
  accept(titan.x+ux*forward-uy*side,titan.y+uy*forward+ux*side);
 }
 // Deterministic spatial fallback for unusually cluttered arenas. Keep original safety bounds.
 if(sites.length<6)for(let y=bounds.t;y<=bounds.b&&sites.length<6;y+=27)for(let x=bounds.l;x<=bounds.r&&sites.length<6;x+=29)accept(x,y);
 return sites;
}
function beginCrashV529(c){
 markRushContactV534(c);
 if(!owner||owner.dead||host.ended()||combat.dead||combat.bossPhase<2||combat.crashV529||!c.rushWallV529||c.rushContactV529)return false;
 const a=ATTACKS[c.key];combat.cooldowns[c.key]=a.cooldown;combat.lastKey=c.key;combat.stats.completed++;
 combat.current=null;combat.ventTime=0;combat.pressure=0;combat.phase='GROGGY';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;
 titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;const id=++combat.crashSerialV529;
 combat.crashV529={id,key:c.key,stage:'STUN',remaining:5,elapsed:0,recallElapsed:0,recovered:0,spawned:0,resolved:false};
 combat.stats.groggyStarts++;
 for(const site of crashSitesV529()){
  const p=newResourceV528(`crash:${id}:${combat.crashV529.spawned}`,site.x,site.y,18);
  p.scatterFromV530={x:titan.x,y:titan.y-24};p.scatterTimeV530=.32;p.grace=.32;
  p.crashBatchV529=id;p.reclaimDelay=5;p.returnTime=CRASH_RULE_V529.returnTime;p.heal=CRASH_RULE_V529.heal;p.ready=false;p.life=5.8;
  combat.sacs.push(p);combat.crashV529.spawned++;combat.stats.crashDrops++;
 }
 fx11Burst(titan.x,titan.y,'charge',18,.65);fx11Sound('charge',.65);host.shake(4);
 recordEvent('돌진 빗나감 · 벽 충돌 · 5초 기절 · 진액 6개');return true;
}
function resolveCrashV529(){
 const g=combat.crashV529;if(!g||g.resolved)return;g.resolved=true;
 const survived=!!owner&&!owner.dead&&!combat.dead&&!host.ended();
 combat.sacs=combat.sacs.filter(p=>p.crashBatchV529!==g.id);combat.tissues=combat.tissues.filter(p=>p.crashBatchV529!==g.id);
 combat.crashV529=null;combat.phase='IDLE';combat.nextDecision=phaseProfileV528().decision;
 if(!survived)return;
 if(g.recovered>0){recordEvent(`정신 회복 · 진액 ${g.recovered}/${g.spawned}개 회수`);return;}
 // Write the actual owner once, then synchronize the host-commit reference to prevent double loss.
 const loss=Math.min(owner.hp,owner.maxHp*CRASH_RULE_V529.penalty);owner.hp=Math.max(0,owner.hp-loss);
 combat.bossHP=owner.hp/owner.maxHp*4200;lastEngineHP=combat.bossHP;combat.stats.crashFailures++;combat.stats.crashSelfDamage+=loss;
 combat.recoilV529=CRASH_RULE_V529.hitTime;motionHit();host.hitMotion(owner);
 recordEvent('진액 회수 0개 · 최대 체력 5% 손실');host.flash('진액 회수 실패 · 체력 5% 손실',1000);
 if(owner.hp<=0)host.kill(owner);
}
function tickCrashV529(dt){
 const g=combat.crashV529;if(!g)return;
 if(!owner||owner.dead||combat.dead||host.ended()){
  combat.sacs=combat.sacs.filter(p=>p.crashBatchV529!==g.id);combat.crashV529=null;return;
 }
 if(g.spawned>0&&!combat.sacs.some(p=>p.crashBatchV529===g.id)&&!combat.tissues.some(p=>p.crashBatchV529===g.id)){if(g.stage==='STUN'&&g.remaining>1e-7){combat.stats.earlyWakes++;recordEvent('진액 전부 파괴 · 즉시 기상');}resolveCrashV529();return;}
 if(g.stage==='STUN'){
  g.elapsed+=dt;g.remaining=Math.max(0,CRASH_RULE_V529.stun-g.elapsed);
  if(g.remaining>1e-7)return;
  g.stage='RECALL';g.recallElapsed=0;combat.phase='CRASH_RECALL';
  for(const p of combat.sacs)if(p.crashBatchV529===g.id)beginResourceReturnV528(p);
  if(!combat.sacs.some(p=>p.crashBatchV529===g.id)){resolveCrashV529();return;}
 }else g.recallElapsed+=dt;
 if(!combat.sacs.some(p=>p.crashBatchV529===g.id)||g.recallElapsed>CRASH_RULE_V529.returnTime+.20)resolveCrashV529();
}
function tickCombat(dt){if(combat.paused)return;combat.clock+=dt;combat.invuln=Math.max(0,combat.invuln-dt);combat.damageFlash=Math.max(0,combat.damageFlash-dt);
 for(const k in combat.cooldowns)combat.cooldowns[k]=Math.max(0,combat.cooldowns[k]-dt);
 for(let i=combat.hitEffects.length-1;i>=0;i--){combat.hitEffects[i].t-=dt;if(combat.hitEffects[i].t<=0)combat.hitEffects.splice(i,1)}
 if(combat.crashV529){tickProjectiles(dt);tickTissues(dt);tickSacs(dt);tickCrashV529(dt);return;}
 if(combat.recoilV529>0){combat.recoilV529=Math.max(0,combat.recoilV529-dt);tickProjectiles(dt);return;}
 tickAttack(dt);if(combat.paused||combat.crashV529)return;advancePhase2(dt);if(combat.paused)return;tickProjectiles(dt);if(combat.paused)return;tickTissues(dt);if(combat.paused)return;tickSacs(dt);if(combat.paused)return;tickVent(dt);
 if(combat.current||combat.phaseTransition>0||combat.phasePending||combat.ventTime>0||!combat.auto)return;
 combat.nextDecision-=dt;if(combat.nextDecision>0)return;
 const picked=decideCombatAI();
 if(!picked){combat.nextDecision=.24;return}
 const ok=startAttack(picked,false);
 if(ok){registerAIChoice(picked);recordEvent(`AI ${aiBrain.mode} · ${ATTACKS[picked].name} / ${aiBrain.reason}`);}
 else {aiBrain.prevented++;combat.nextDecision=.22;}
}
// Manual skill buttons are *test selectors*, not ordinary attack requests.
// Pressing one takes priority over AI, safely cancels the previous test, and bypasses
// only that skill's test cooldown. Auto battle keeps normal cooldowns/interlocks.
function requestAttack(key){
 if(!ATTACKS[key])return false;
 if(combat.dead){showNotice('BIO TITAN 사망 · 초기화를 먼저 눌러 주세요',2,true);return false;}
 combat.auto=false;
 if((ATTACKS[key].phase||1)>combat.bossPhase){showNotice(`${ATTACKS[key].phase}페이즈 잠금 · 위쪽 페이즈 테스트 버튼을 사용`,2,true);return false;}
 if(combat.phaseTransition>0||combat.phasePending){showNotice('갑각 파손 중 · 연출 종료 후 사용',2,true);return false;}
 if(combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0){showNotice('기절과 회수 결과가 끝난 뒤 사용',1.2);return false;}
 if(combat.ventTime>0){showNotice(`강제 배기 ${combat.ventTime.toFixed(1)}초 · 종료 후 선택`,1.3,true);renderCombatUI();return false;}
 if(combat.playerHP<=0){resetCombat(true);recordEvent('수동 시험을 위해 체력 복구');}
 else if(combat.paused){combat.paused=false;recordEvent('수동 시험으로 전투 재개');}
 if(combat.current)cancelAttack('수동 스킬 전환',true);
 else {combat.projectiles.length=0;combat.tissues.length=0;combat.hitEffects.length=0;combat.hitRecords.clear();}
 combat.cooldowns[key]=0; // Only for an explicitly tapped test button.
 const ok=startAttack(key,true);
 if(ok){recordEvent(`직접 선택: ${ATTACKS[key].name} · 자동 공격 OFF`);showNotice(`${ATTACKS[key].name} 수동 테스트 시작`,1.5);}
 else showNotice('선택 스킬 실행 실패 · 다시 눌러 주세요',1.5,true);
 renderCombatUI();return ok;
}
function toggleAttackMode(){
 combat.auto=!combat.auto;
 if(combat.auto){combat.nextDecision=.18;recordEvent('자동 공격 ON · 9단계 전술 의사결정 활성화');}
 else recordEvent('자동 공격 OFF · 버튼으로 원하는 스킬 선택');
 renderCombatUI();
}
function holdBossForAttack(dt){
