 if(combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0){titan.vx=titan.vy=titan.move=0;titan.anim+=dt*3;setStatus('GROGGY',combat.crashV529?.stage==='STUN'?'벽 충돌 · 기절':'회수 결과');return;}
 if(combat.current?.stage==='RECOVERY'&&combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0){moveAI(dt);return;}
 if(!(combat.current?.stage==='ACTIVE'&&['charge','feast'].includes(combat.current.key))){titan.vx=titan.vy=0;titan.move=0;titan.anim+=dt*6;}
 setStatus('ATTACK',combat.phaseTransition>0?'변이 중':combat.current?`${ATTACKS[combat.current.key].name} ${combat.current.stage}`:'짧은 배기');
}
function renderResourceV528(s,tissue=false){
 let rx=s.x,ry=s.y;
 if(s.scatterFromV530&&s.age<s.scatterTimeV530){const u=clamp(s.age/s.scatterTimeV530,0,1);rx=s.scatterFromV530.x+(s.x-s.scatterFromV530.x)*u;ry=s.scatterFromV530.y+(s.y-s.scatterFromV530.y)*u-42*Math.sin(Math.PI*u);}
 const p=scPos(rx,ry);if(p.x<-100||p.y<-100||p.x>view.W+100||p.y>view.H+100)return;
 const pulse=.92+.08*Math.sin(combat.clock*8+s.age),r=s.r*pulse;
 ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle=tissue?'#729d344b':'#63d43c40';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y,r,r*.78,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle=s.returning?'#e8ffbd':'#a9f86f';ctx.lineWidth=1.7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y,r,r*.78,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.shadowColor='#baff68';ctx.shadowBlur=8;const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x-2,p.y-3,1,p.x,p.y,Math.max(2,r*.65));g.addColorStop(0,'#f4ffc0');g.addColorStop(.45,'#9fe15a');g.addColorStop(1,'#225e32');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y,r*.55,r*.43,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 if(s.returning){const b=scPos(titan.x,titan.y);ctx.strokeStyle='#c7ff8b99';ctx.setLineDash([3,7]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.lineTo(p.x+(b.x-p.x)*.22,p.y+(b.y-p.y)*.22);ctx.stroke();ctx.setLineDash([]);}
 else{ctx.strokeStyle='#e8ffc0';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r+3,-Math.PI/2,-Math.PI/2+Math.PI*2*clamp(1-s.age/s.reclaimDelay,0,1));ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.restore();
}
function renderTissues(){for(const p of combat.tissues)renderResourceV528(p,true);}
function renderSacs(){for(const p of combat.sacs)renderResourceV528(p,false);}
// Warning geometry is deliberately simple. The painted animation is a separate pass.
function feastStepV533(c){
 if(c.stage==='WINDUP')return 'rush';if(c.stage!=='ACTIVE')return null;
 const a=ATTACKS.feast;
 if(c.t<a.chargeEnd&&!c.chargeFinished)return 'rush';
 if(c.t<a.slamAt)return 'slam';if(c.t<a.waveAt)return 'waveReady';return 'wave';
}
function feastLabelV533(c){
 const step=feastStepV533(c);
 return '최종 포식 · '+(step==='rush'?((c.rushIndex||0)+1)+'/2 돌진 '+(c.stage==='WINDUP'?'예고':'중'):step==='slam'?'지면 분쇄 준비':step==='waveReady'?'분쇄 · 충격파 준비':step==='wave'?'충격파 확산':'회복');
}
function warningShapesV533(c=combat.current){
 if(!c||c.stage==='RECOVERY')return [];
 const a=ATTACKS[c.key];
 if(a.kind==='pulse')return [{kind:c.stage==='ACTIVE'?'wave':'pulseReady',x:titan.x,y:titan.y,aim:c.aim,
  r:c.stage==='ACTIVE'?a.ringStart+(a.ringEnd-a.ringStart)*clamp(c.t/a.active,0,1):a.ringEnd,
  width:a.ringWidth,offset:a.gapOffset,half:a.gapHalf}];
 if(a.kind!=='feast')return [];
 const step=feastStepV533(c);
 if(step==='rush')return [{kind:'rush',x:c.originX,y:c.originY,aim:c.aim,length:c.locked?c.chargeReach:a.maxRange,
  width:chargeWarningWidthV528(c.aim,a.chargeR),front:chargeWarningFrontV528(c.aim),route:rushRouteV535(c).points}];
 if(step==='slam')return [{kind:'slam',x:c.finalX,y:c.finalY,r:a.slamRadius}];
 if(step==='waveReady'||step==='wave')return [{kind:step==='wave'?'wave':'pulseReady',x:c.finalX,y:c.finalY,aim:c.aim,
  r:step==='wave'?a.pulseStart+(a.pulseEnd-a.pulseStart)*clamp((c.t-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,0,1):a.pulseEnd,
  width:a.pulseWidth,offset:a.pulseOffset,half:a.pulseHalf}];
 return [];
}
function dangerArcsV533(aim,offset,half){return [[-Math.PI,-offset-half],[-offset+half,offset-half],[offset+half,Math.PI]].map(([l,h])=>[aim+l,aim+h]);}
function renderSpecialWarningV533(c,outlineOnly){
 if(c.key==='feast'&&feastStepV533(c)==='rush')return renderRushWarningV535(c,outlineOnly);
 const a=ATTACKS[c.key],shapes=warningShapesV533(c);if(!shapes.length)return;
 const progress=c.stage==='WINDUP'?clamp(c.t/a.windup,0,1):1;
 ctx.save();ctx.setLineDash([]);ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
 for(const s of shapes){const p=scPos(s.x,s.y);
  if(s.kind==='rush'){
   const ux=Math.cos(s.aim),uy=Math.sin(s.aim),nx=-uy,ny=ux;
   const ox=p.x-ux*s.front,oy=p.y-uy*s.front,ex=p.x+ux*(s.length+s.front),ey=p.y+uy*(s.length+s.front);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(ox+nx*s.width,oy+ny*s.width);ctx.lineTo(ex+nx*s.width,ey+ny*s.width);
   ctx.lineTo(ex-nx*s.width,ey-ny*s.width);ctx.lineTo(ox-nx*s.width,oy-ny*s.width);ctx.closePath();
   if(!outlineOnly){ctx.fillStyle='rgba(123,12,21,.13)';ctx.fill();}
   ctx.globalAlpha=(outlineOnly?.52:.65)+progress*.2;ctx.strokeStyle=c.locked?'#ff805f':'#e5a28c';ctx.lineWidth=c.locked?1.7:1.2;ctx.stroke();
   ctx.globalAlpha=.5;ctx.strokeStyle='#ffb58c';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;
   const scroll=c.stage==='ACTIVE'?0:combat.clock*30%55;
   for(let d=38+scroll;d<s.length;d+=55){const x=p.x+ux*d,y=p.y+uy*d;
    ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-ux*10+nx*8,y-uy*10+ny*8);ctx.lineTo(x+ux*3,y+uy*3);ctx.lineTo(x-ux*10-nx*8,y-uy*10-ny*8);ctx.stroke();}
  }else if(s.kind==='slam'){
   const u=clamp((c.t-a.chargeEnd)/Math.max(.01,a.slamAt-a.chargeEnd),0,1);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,s.r,0,Math.PI*2);if(!outlineOnly){ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(145,19,27,${.09+.06*u})`;ctx.fill();}
   ctx.strokeStyle='#fa755c';ctx.globalAlpha=.85;ctx.lineWidth=1.7;ctx.stroke();
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,s.r+4,-Math.PI/2,-Math.PI/2+Math.PI*2*u);ctx.strokeStyle='#ffe1b1';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.stroke();
  }else{
   // Gaps are empty space. No green safe-area fan covers the scene.
   const live=s.kind==='wave',arcs=dangerArcsV533(s.aim,s.offset,s.half);
   ctx.globalAlpha=live?(outlineOnly?.8:.45):.22+progress*.2;
   ctx.strokeStyle=live?'#ffdfa8':c.locked?'#ff7657':'#c59b88';ctx.lineWidth=live?1.5:1.2;
   for(const [lo,hi] of arcs){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,s.r,lo,hi);ctx.stroke();}
   if(!live){
    // Four short boundary marks show the two actual openings without painting a safe zone.
    ctx.globalAlpha=.50;ctx.strokeStyle='#d4c1a1';
    for(const sign of [-1,1])for(const edge of [-1,1]){const angle=s.aim+sign*s.offset+edge*s.half;
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x+Math.cos(angle)*(s.r-9),p.y+Math.sin(angle)*(s.r-9));
     ctx.lineTo(p.x+Math.cos(angle)*(s.r+6),p.y+Math.sin(angle)*(s.r+6));ctx.stroke();}
   }
  }
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

function renderCombatEffects(){ctx.save();
 if(combat.phaseTransition>0){const is3=combat.phaseTarget===3,progress=1-combat.phaseTransition/(is3?PHASE3_TIME:PHASE2_TIME),p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-80);ctx.globalAlpha=.3+.35*Math.sin(combat.clock*(is3?45:30))**2;ctx.strokeStyle=is3?'#ff418a':'#ffa85d';ctx.lineWidth=6+20*progress;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,85+150*progress,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.font='900 12px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillStyle=is3?'#ffb5db':'#ffe0a0';ctx.fillText(is3?'PHASE III · COLLAPSING DEVOURER':'PHASE II · BROKEN CARAPACE',p.x,p.y-105);ctx.textAlign='start'}
 if(combat.ventTime>0){const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-65),r=75+22*Math.sin(combat.clock*16);ctx.shadowColor='#ffb541';ctx.shadowBlur=35;ctx.globalAlpha=.45+.25*Math.sin(combat.clock*18);ctx.strokeStyle='#ffd17b';ctx.lineWidth=8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=.8;ctx.fillStyle='#fff0a6';ctx.font='bold 11px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText(`PRESSURE VENT · CORE EXPOSED ×${combat.bossPhase===3?'1.30':combat.bossPhase===2?'1.25':'1.20'}`,p.x,p.y-r-11);ctx.textAlign='start';ctx.shadowBlur=0;ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
 if(combat.current?.key==='reclaim'&&combat.current.stage==='ACTIVE'){const t=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-24);ctx.lineWidth=3;ctx.strokeStyle='#abff79';for(const h of combat.current.recoverList){const p=[...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].find(s=>s.id===h.id);if(!p)continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(t.x,t.y);ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y);ctx.stroke();}}renderProjectilesV530();
 const c=combat.current;if(c&&c.key==='mortar'&&c.sites.length){for(const [i,h] of c.sites.entries()){
  if(h.impacted)continue;const a=ATTACKS.mortar,eta=a.windup-c.total+i*a.interval,flight=clamp(1-eta/(a.lockBefore+i*a.interval),0,1);
  const src=scPos(titan.x+(i===0?-23:20),titan.y-75),dst=scPos(h.x,h.y),mx=src.x+(dst.x-src.x)*flight,my=src.y+(dst.y-src.y)*flight-115*Math.sin(flight*Math.PI);
  ctx.fillStyle='#ffb75b';ctx.shadowColor='#ff9a36';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(mx,my,7+flight*5,0,6.283);ctx.fill();ctx.shadowBlur=0;
 }}
 if(c&&c.key==='backfire'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'){
  for(const h of c.sites)if(h.impacted){const q=scPos(h.x,h.y);ctx.strokeStyle='#ff8bb0';ctx.shadowColor='#ff4a91';ctx.shadowBlur=18;ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,h.r+8+Math.sin(combat.clock*20)*6,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;}
 }
 if(c&&c.key==='feast'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'){
  if(c.t<ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd){const q=scPos(titan.x,titan.y);ctx.fillStyle='#fa698e';ctx.globalAlpha=.5;for(let i=0;i<4;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x-Math.cos(c.aim)*(20+i*18),q.y-Math.sin(c.aim)*(20+i*18),16-i*3,0,6.283);ctx.fill()}ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
  if(c.t>=ATTACKS.feast.slamAt&&c.t<ATTACKS.feast.waveAt){const q=scPos(c.finalX,c.finalY);ctx.strokeStyle='#ffb8d3';ctx.lineWidth=7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,ATTACKS.feast.slamRadius+(c.t-ATTACKS.feast.slamAt)*42,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();}
 }
 if(c&&c.key==='crush2'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'){const q=scPos(c.targetX,c.targetY),rad=ATTACKS.crush2.radius+16*Math.min(c.t/.28,1);ctx.strokeStyle='#ffad66';ctx.lineWidth=6;ctx.shadowColor='#ff7134';ctx.shadowBlur=23;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,rad,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;}
 if(c&&c.key==='claw'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'){
 const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y),r=110+110*Math.min(c.t/.18,1),offset=c.side*.09;
 ctx.lineWidth=8;ctx.strokeStyle='#ffdc9f';ctx.shadowColor='#ff8b3a';ctx.shadowBlur=17;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,c.aim-ATTACKS.claw.halfAngle+offset,c.aim+ATTACKS.claw.halfAngle+offset);ctx.stroke();ctx.shadowBlur=0;}
 if(c&&c.key==='charge'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'){const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y);ctx.fillStyle='#f2aa55';ctx.globalAlpha=.4;for(let i=0;i<5;i++){let x=p.x-Math.cos(c.aim)*(20+i*16),y=p.y-Math.sin(c.aim)*(20+i*16);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,15-i*2,0,6.283);ctx.fill()}ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
 for(const e of combat.hitEffects){const q=scPos(e.x,e.y);ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,e.t/.35);ctx.fillStyle='#ffdbbd';ctx.font='bold 19px system-ui';ctx.fillText(typeof e.damage==='number'?'-'+e.damage:e.damage,q.x+12,q.y-20-(.45-e.t)*27)}
 if(false&&combat.damageFlash>0){ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,54,38,${.09*combat.damageFlash/.35})`;ctx.fillRect(0,0,view.W,view.H)}ctx.restore();}

function renderCombatUI(){}
const FX11={enabled:true,quality:1,shakeEnabled:true,soundOn:true,volume:.55,
  t:0,seed:0x51a7e95,particles:[],waves:[],slashes:[],bolts:[],flash:0,flashRGB:'255,131,64',shake:0,
  trailClock:0,ambientClock:0,noise:null,audio:null,unlocked:false,voices:0,
  stats:{bursts:0,particles:0,waves:0,sounds:0,voiceSkips:0,peak:0}};

const fx11Rand=()=>{let a=FX11.seed|0;a^=a<<13;a^=a>>>17;a^=a<<5;FX11.seed=a;return (a>>>0)/4294967296;};
const fx11Pick=(a,b)=>a+(b-a)*fx11Rand();
const FX_COLORS={claw:'#ffd09a',twin:'#ffbe8c',rear:'#edcd9b',spit:'#8cf1a2',mortar:'#ff9d51',charge:'#ffc080',sacs:'#bdff8a',reclaim:'#bdf6a9',crush2:'#ff954f',crush:'#ffa35c',pulse:'#ffd06e',backfire:'#ff6998',feast:'#ff4e79',vent:'#ffd38c',phase:'#f78964',death:'#ffcf98'};
function fx11Unlocked(){
 if(!FX11.soundOn||host.feedback().sound===false)return false;
 FX11.audio=host.audio();return !!FX11.audio&&FX11.audio.state==='running';
}
function fx11AudioUserGesture(){
 if(!FX11.soundOn||host.feedback().sound===false)return;
 if(!FX11.audio)fx11Unlocked();else if(FX11.audio.state==='suspended')FX11.audio.resume().catch(()=>{});
}



function fx11Tone(freq=115,slide=.45,duration=.26,wave='sawtooth',vol=.27,noise=false){
 if(!FX11.soundOn||host.feedback().sound===false||!FX11.audio||FX11.audio.state!=='running'||FX11.voices>=16)return;
 const ac=FX11.audio,t=ac.currentTime,g=ac.createGain(),lp=ac.createBiquadFilter();
 lp.type='lowpass';lp.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(300,freq*5),t);lp.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(130,freq*.9),t+duration);
 const amp=Math.min(.22,Math.max(.001,vol*FX11.volume*.27));
 g.gain.setValueAtTime(.0001,t);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(amp,t+.009);g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+duration);
 lp.connect(g);g.connect(ac.destination);FX11.voices++;
 let src;
 if(noise){
  if(!FX11.noise){const b=ac.createBuffer(1,ac.sampleRate,ac.sampleRate),arr=b.getChannelData(0);let rs=91;for(let i=0;i<arr.length;i++){rs=(1664525*rs+1013904223)|0;arr[i]=((rs>>>0)/2147483648)-1;}FX11.noise=b;}
  src=ac.createBufferSource();src.buffer=FX11.noise;src.playbackRate.value=Math.max(.25,Math.min(4,freq/260));
 }else{src=ac.createOscillator();src.type=wave;src.frequency.setValueAtTime(Math.max(32,freq),t);src.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(24,freq*slide),t+duration);}
 src.connect(lp);src.onended=()=>{FX11.voices=Math.max(0,FX11.voices-1);try{src.disconnect();lp.disconnect();g.disconnect()}catch(e){}};
 src.start(t);src.stop(t+duration+.01);FX11.stats.sounds++;
}
function fx11Sound(kind,power=1){if(!FX11.soundOn)return;
 const play=(f,s,d,w,v,n)=>fx11Tone(f,s,d,w,v*power,n);
 switch(kind){
 case 'wind':play(90,.65,.12,'triangle',.16);break;
 case 'lock':play(340,.55,.11,'sine',.12);break;
 case 'claw':case 'rear':play(580,.20,.23,'sawtooth',.30);play(110,.5,.21,'triangle',.23);play(390,.55,.19,'noise',.12,true);break;
 case 'twin':play(150,.4,.28,'triangle',.42);play(85,.50,.34,'sawtooth',.23);break;
 case 'spit':play(490,.25,.15,'triangle',.23);play(800,.45,.10,'sine',.07);play(640,.67,.15,'noise',.11,true);break;
 case 'charge':play(92,.37,.43,'sawtooth',.39);play(180,.19,.40,'triangle',.34);break;
 case 'mortar':play(140,.24,.40,'triangle',.36);play(540,.19,.12,'sawtooth',.11);play(280,.35,.26,'noise',.20,true);break;
 case 'sacs':play(190,.72,.28,'sine',.18);play(315,.52,.25,'triangle',.13);break;
 case 'reclaim':play(178,1.85,.38,'triangle',.30);break;
 case 'crush2':case 'crush':play(100,.22,.57,'sawtooth',.55);play(63,.35,.42,'triangle',.48);break;
 case 'pulse':play(110,.47,.56,'triangle',.40);play(170,.29,.33,'sawtooth',.20);break;
 case 'backfire':play(240,.27,.31,'sawtooth',.40);play(95,.4,.40,'triangle',.24);play(560,.22,.30,'noise',.24,true);break;
 case 'feast':play(78,.24,.75,'sawtooth',.60);play(310,.33,.57,'triangle',.30);break;
 case 'vent':play(310,.29,.60,'triangle',.31);play(480,.22,.52,'sawtooth',.17);play(770,.24,.73,'noise',.23,true);break;
 case 'phase':play(165,.53,.79,'sawtooth',.35);play(68,.51,.81,'triangle',.35);break;
 case 'death':play(80,.22,1.10,'sawtooth',.40);play(130,.22,1.25,'triangle',.32);break;
 case 'hit':play(600,.18,.16,'triangle',.21);break;
 case 'step':play(80,.6,.08,'triangle',.10);break;
 default:play(140,.5,.15,'triangle',.12);
 }}
function fx11Particle(x,y,vx,vy,life,r,color,drag=.94,grav=0,shape='spark'){
 if(!FX11.enabled||FX11.quality===0)return;
 if(FX11.particles.length>=Math.round(230*FX11.quality))FX11.particles.shift();
 FX11.particles.push({x,y,vx,vy,life,max:life,r,color,drag,grav,shape});FX11.stats.particles++;
}
function fx11Burst(x,y,key,n=28,power=1,baseDir=null){
 if(!FX11.enabled||FX11.quality===0)return;
 FX11.stats.bursts++;const col=FX_COLORS[key]||'#ffbf79',count=Math.round(n*FX11.quality);
 for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
  const a=baseDir===null?fx11Pick(0,Math.PI*2):baseDir+fx11Pick(-1.0,1.0);
  const sp=fx11Pick(35,165)*power;
  fx11Particle(x+fx11Pick(-6,6),y+fx11Pick(-4,4),Math.cos(a)*sp,Math.sin(a)*sp-fx11Pick(2,20),fx11Pick(.30,.83),fx11Pick(1.2,4)*power,col,.94,fx11Pick(20,85),i%5===0?'droplet':'spark');
 }}
function fx11Wave(x,y,r,color,seconds=.48,width=5,from=0){
 if(!FX11.enabled||FX11.quality===0)return;
 FX11.waves.push({x,y,r,from,age:0,duration:seconds,width,color});
 if(FX11.waves.length>23)FX11.waves.shift();FX11.stats.waves++;
}
function fx11Slash(x,y,aim,max,half,color){if(!FX11.enabled)return;
 FX11.slashes.push({x,y,aim,max,half,color,age:0,duration:.28});if(FX11.slashes.length>10)FX11.slashes.shift();}
function fx11Flash(strength=.35,rgb='255,127,70',shake=1){if(!FX11.enabled)return;
 FX11.flash=Math.max(FX11.flash,Math.min(.34,strength));FX11.flashRGB=rgb;
 FX11.shake=Math.max(FX11.shake,FX11.shakeEnabled?shake:0);}
function fx11Impact(x,y,key,r=75,power=1){fx11Burst(x,y,key,28,power);fx11Wave(x,y,r,FX_COLORS[key]||'#ffa973',.46,5);
 fx11Flash(.13*power,key==='spit'||key==='sacs'?'106,255,127':'255,142,87',Math.min(5,3*power));fx11Sound(key,power);}
function fx11Reset(){FX11.t=0;FX11.particles.length=0;FX11.waves.length=0;FX11.slashes.length=0;FX11.bolts.length=0;FX11.flash=0;FX11.shake=0;FX11.trailClock=0;FX11.ambientClock=0;
 for(const k in FX11.stats)FX11.stats[k]=0;
 cvs.style.transform='';}
function fx11Tick(dt){if(!FX11.enabled){cvs.style.transform='';return;}
 FX11.t+=dt;FX11.flash=Math.max(0,FX11.flash-dt*.74);FX11.shake=Math.max(0,FX11.shake-dt*13);
 for(let i=FX11.particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=FX11.particles[i];p.life-=dt;if(p.life<=0){FX11.particles.splice(i,1);continue;}
  p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.vx*=Math.pow(p.drag,dt*60);p.vy=p.vy*Math.pow(p.drag,dt*60)+p.grav*dt;}
 for(let i=FX11.waves.length-1;i>=0;i--){FX11.waves[i].age+=dt;if(FX11.waves[i].age>=FX11.waves[i].duration)FX11.waves.splice(i,1);}
 for(let i=FX11.slashes.length-1;i>=0;i--){FX11.slashes[i].age+=dt;if(FX11.slashes[i].age>=FX11.slashes[i].duration)FX11.slashes.splice(i,1);}
 const c=combat.current;
 FX11.trailClock-=dt;
 if(FX11.trailClock<=0){FX11.trailClock=.055/Math.max(.45,FX11.quality);
  const p=combat.projectiles;for(const q of p){fx11Particle(q.x,q.y,fx11Pick(-8,8),fx11Pick(-14,7),fx11Pick(.15,.3),fx11Pick(1.2,2.9),'#9afa99',.89,0,'droplet');}
  if(c&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'&&(c.key==='charge'||c.key==='feast')&&hypot(titan.vx,titan.vy)>30){
   fx11Particle(titan.x+fx11Pick(-35,35),titan.y+fx11Pick(-2,13),fx11Pick(-20,20),fx11Pick(-40,-12),.55,fx11Pick(2,6),'#d2a377',.88,62,'dust');}
  if(combat.ventTime>0){const a=fx11Pick(0,6.28);fx11Particle(titan.x+Math.cos(a)*32,titan.y-75+Math.sin(a)*20,fx11Pick(-35,35),fx11Pick(-100,-48),fx11Pick(.5,.9),fx11Pick(1,3),'#ffe2a2',.98,-14,'smoke');}
  if(combat.phaseTransition>0){fx11Particle(titan.x+fx11Pick(-70,70),titan.y-70,fx11Pick(-42,42),fx11Pick(-82,10),fx11Pick(.3,.7),fx11Pick(1,4),'#fb6587',.96,-40);}
 }
 FX11.ambientClock-=dt;
 if(FX11.ambientClock<=0){FX11.ambientClock=.20;
  if(c?.stage==='WINDUP'){const k=c.key,a=ATTACKS[k];let x=titan.x+fx11Pick(-29,29),y=titan.y-71+fx11Pick(-23,23);
   fx11Particle(x,y,fx11Pick(-17,17),fx11Pick(-17,5),.32,fx11Pick(1,3),FX_COLORS[k]||'#ffc',.90,-18);}
  if(combat.bossPhase===3&&!combat.dead&&FX11.quality>=.7){fx11Particle(titan.x+fx11Pick(-37,37),titan.y-72+fx11Pick(-24,24),fx11Pick(-10,10),-25,.34,2,'#ff83ad',.96,-12);}
 }
 FX11.stats.peak=Math.max(FX11.stats.peak,FX11.particles.length);

 if(FX11.shakeEnabled)host.shake(FX11.shake);

}
function fx11RenderGround(){if(!FX11.enabled)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';
 const c=combat.current;
 if(c&&c.stage==='WINDUP'){
  const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-85),factor=clamp(c.t/Math.max(.01,ATTACKS[c.key].windup),0,1);
  const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(p.x,p.y,1,p.x,p.y,105);g.addColorStop(0,`rgba(255,130,65,${.14+factor*.22})`);g.addColorStop(.55,'rgba(255,70,34,.055)');g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,70,34,0)');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,105,0,6.283);ctx.fill();}
 if(combat.ventTime>0){const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-70),r=62+8*Math.sin(FX11.t*21);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,187,92,.08)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,6.283);ctx.fill();}
 ctx.restore();}
function fx11Render(){if(!FX11.enabled)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';
 for(const w of FX11.waves){const t=w.age/w.duration,p=scPos(w.x,w.y),r=w.from+(w.r-w.from)*t;if(p.x+r<0||p.x-r>view.W||p.y+r<0||p.y-r>view.H)continue;
  ctx.globalAlpha=(1-t)*.72;ctx.strokeStyle=w.color;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.75,w.width*(1-t));ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,0,6.283);ctx.stroke();}
 for(const s of FX11.slashes){const t=s.age/s.duration,p=scPos(s.x,s.y),r=s.max*(.60+.32*t);
  ctx.globalAlpha=Math.pow(1-t,1.7)*.85;ctx.strokeStyle=s.color;ctx.lineWidth=11*(1-t)+1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r,s.aim-s.half,s.aim+s.half);ctx.stroke();
  ctx.lineWidth=2.5*(1-t)+.5;ctx.globalAlpha*=.7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r+11,s.aim-s.half,s.aim+s.half);ctx.stroke();}
 for(const p of FX11.particles){const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);if(q.x<-35||q.x>view.W+35||q.y<-35||q.y>view.H+35)continue;
  const alpha=clamp(p.life/p.max,0,1),r=p.r*(p.shape==='smoke'?1+.65*(1-alpha):1);
  ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*.9;ctx.fillStyle=p.color;ctx.beginPath();
  if(p.shape==='dust'||p.shape==='smoke')ctx.ellipse(q.x,q.y,r*1.9,r*.65,0,0,6.283);
  else ctx.arc(q.x,q.y,r,0,6.283);ctx.fill();}
 if(combat.current?.key==='reclaim'&&combat.current.stage==='ACTIVE'){
  const c=combat.current;const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-38);
  for(const site of c.recoverList){const q=[...combat.sacs,...combat.tissues].find(s=>s.id===site.id);if(!q)continue;
   const g=scPos(q.x,q.y),u=(FX11.t*1.7+q.age*.31)%1;
   ctx.globalAlpha=.8;ctx.fillStyle='#b7ffa4';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x+(g.x-p.x)*u,p.y+(g.y-p.y)*u,3.2,0,6.283);ctx.fill();}}
 ctx.restore();
 if(FX11.flash>.005&&host.feedback().flash>0){ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(host.feedback().flash===1?.10:.22,FX11.flash);ctx.fillStyle=`rgb(${FX11.flashRGB})`;ctx.fillRect(0,0,view.W,view.H);ctx.restore();}
}
// Instrument existing event boundaries, never update actual skill rules.
const fx11Original={resetCombat,update,startAttack,lockAttack,enterActive,launchSpit,mortarImpact,spawnSac,tickReclaim,drawHitCone,impactBackfire,feastSlam,tickFeast,damageBoss,resolveHit,startVent,beginPhaseTransition,finishBossDeath,stepCharge,tickPulse};
resetCombat=function(...args){const out=fx11Original.resetCombat(...args);fx11Reset();return out;};
update=function(dt){fx11Original.update(dt);fx11Tick(dt);};
startAttack=function(...args){const ok=fx11Original.startAttack(...args);if(ok){const key=args[0];fx11Sound('wind',.7);fx11Wave(titan.x,titan.y-73,35,FX_COLORS[key]||'#ffae76',.36,2);}
 return ok;};
lockAttack=function(c){const was=c.locked;const out=fx11Original.lockAttack(c);if(!was&&c.locked){fx11Sound('lock',.45);fx11Wave(titan.x,titan.y-74,53,FX_COLORS[c.key]||'#efb683',.36,2);}
 return out;};
enterActive=function(c){const out=fx11Original.enterActive(c),k=c.key,aim=c.aim;
 if(['claw','twin','rear'].includes(k)){
  const aa=k==='rear'?aim+Math.PI:aim+(k==='claw'?c.side*.09:-c.side*.30);
  fx11Slash(titan.x,titan.y,aa,ATTACKS[k].maxRange,ATTACKS[k].halfAngle,FX_COLORS[k]);
  fx11Burst(titan.x+Math.cos(aa)*128,titan.y+Math.sin(aa)*128,k,15,.65,aa);
  fx11Flash(.075,'255,218,148',2);fx11Sound(k);
 }else if(['crush2','crush'].includes(k))fx11Impact(c.targetX,c.targetY,k,ATTACKS[k].radius+12,1.15);
 else if(k==='pulse'){fx11Sound('pulse');fx11Wave(titan.x,titan.y,90,FX_COLORS.pulse,.48,3);fx11Flash(.085,'255,184,93',2);}
 else if(k==='charge'||k==='feast'){fx11Sound(k,.7);fx11Burst(titan.x,titan.y,k,14,.80,aim+Math.PI);}
 else if(k==='reclaim')fx11Sound('reclaim');
 return out;};
launchSpit=function(c,i){const out=fx11Original.launchSpit(c,i),a=c.aim,x=titan.x+Math.cos(a)*(c.muzzleV530||60),y=titan.y+Math.sin(a)*(c.muzzleV530||60);
 fx11Burst(x,y,'spit',11,.45,a);fx11Sound('spit');return out;};
mortarImpact=function(c,i){const site=c.sites[i],prior=!!site?.impacted;const out=fx11Original.mortarImpact(c,i);
 if(site&&!prior&&site.impacted)fx11Impact(site.x,site.y,'mortar',ATTACKS.mortar.radius+20,.9);return out;};
spawnSac=function(c,site,i){const out=fx11Original.spawnSac(c,site,i);fx11Burst(site.x,site.y,'sacs',8,.40);fx11Wave(site.x,site.y,ATTACKS.sacs.radius+5,'#9add74',.35,2);if(i===0)fx11Sound('sacs');return out;};
const fx11Recovered=()=>combat.sacs.length;
tickReclaim=function(c,span){const prev=fx11Recovered(),out=fx11Original.tickReclaim(c,span);if(combat.sacs.length<prev){fx11Burst(titan.x,titan.y-35,'reclaim',26,.75);fx11Sound('reclaim',.7);}return out;};
drawHitCone=function(c,offset,damage,i){const out=fx11Original.drawHitCone(c,offset,damage,i),aa=c.aim+offset;
 fx11Slash(titan.x,titan.y,aa,ATTACKS.twin.maxRange,ATTACKS.twin.halfAngle,'#ffc08e');fx11Burst(titan.x+Math.cos(aa)*142,titan.y+Math.sin(aa)*142,'twin',15,.66,aa);fx11Sound('twin',.85);return out;};
impactBackfire=function(c,i){const site=c.sites[i],prior=!!site?.impacted;const out=fx11Original.impactBackfire(c,i);
 if(site&&!prior&&site.impacted)fx11Impact(site.x,site.y,'backfire',ATTACKS.backfire.radius+28,1.0);return out;};
feastSlam=function(c){const out=fx11Original.feastSlam(c);fx11Impact(c.finalX,c.finalY,'feast',ATTACKS.feast.slamRadius+20,1.5);return out;};
tickFeast=function(c,oldT,span){const out=fx11Original.tickFeast(c,oldT,span);
 if(oldT<ATTACKS.feast.waveAt&&oldT+span>=ATTACKS.feast.waveAt){fx11Wave(titan.x,titan.y,130,'#ffa2b5',.60,7);fx11Sound('pulse',1.2);}return out;};
startVent=function(){const existing=combat.ventTime;const out=fx11Original.startVent();if(!existing&&combat.ventTime>0){fx11Sound('vent');fx11Burst(titan.x,titan.y-72,'vent',42,.70);fx11Wave(titan.x,titan.y-68,110,'#ffd7a7',.75,7);fx11Flash(.15,'255,192,125',3);}return out;};

 })));
 function frame(key,index,x,y,w,h,alpha=1,anchorX=.5,anchorY=.5){
  const a=art[key],im=a?.im;if(!im||!(im.naturalWidth||im.width)||alpha<=0)return false;
  index=Math.max(0,Math.min(a.count-1,index|0));ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;
  ctx.drawImage(im,(index%a.cols)*a.size,Math.floor(index/a.cols)*a.size,a.size,a.size,x-w*anchorX,y-h*anchorY,w,h);ctx.restore();return true;
 }
 const fx={seed:0x326bf1,t:0,particles:[],slashes:[],impacts:[],trails:[],flares:[],lastBullets:[],seen:new WeakMap(),cache:null,
  trailTimer:0,phaseTimer:0,lastRush:'',stats:{emittedParticles:0,emittedSlashes:0,emittedImpacts:0,emittedBursts:0,projectilePops:0,peakParticles:0,peakEvents:0,cacheBytes:0,draws:0}};
 const enabled=()=>!!options.fx&&FX11.enabled&&FX11.quality>0;
 const reduced=()=>!!host.feedback().reducedMotion;
 const quality=()=>clamp(FX11.quality,0,1);
 const random=()=>{let v=fx.seed|0;v^=v<<13;v^=v>>>17;v^=v<<5;fx.seed=v;return(v>>>0)/4294967296;};
 const pick=(a,b)=>a+(b-a)*random();
 const hash=(v)=>{v=Math.imul((v|0)^0x45d9f3b,0x45d9f3b);v=Math.imul(v^(v>>>16),0x45d9f3b);return((v^(v>>>16))>>>0)/4294967296;};
 const ease=t=>1-Math.pow(1-clamp(t,0,1),3);
 const visible=(x,y,r=80)=>x-view.camX+r>=0&&x-view.camX-r<=view.W&&y-view.camY+r>=0&&y-view.camY-r<=view.H;
 const skin=k=>k==='feast'?'feast':['spit','sacs','reclaim','fluid'].includes(k)?'acid':['backfire','feast','barrage','phase3'].includes(k)?'rage':k==='wall'?'dust':'bone';
 function reset(){
  fx.seed=0x326bf1;fx.t=0;for(const k of ['particles','slashes','impacts','trails','flares','lastBullets'])fx[k].length=0;
  fx.seen=new WeakMap();fx.trailTimer=fx.phaseTimer=0;fx.lastRush='';
  for(const k of Object.keys(fx.stats))if(k!=='cacheBytes')fx.stats[k]=0;
 }
 function surface(w,h){
  let c;if(typeof OffscreenCanvas==='function')c=new OffscreenCanvas(w,h);
  else{c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=w;c.height=h;}
  if(!c||typeof c.getContext!=='function')return null;
  const g=c.getContext('2d');return g?{c,g}:null;
 }
 function rgb(hex){return[parseInt(hex.slice(1,3),16),parseInt(hex.slice(3,5),16),parseInt(hex.slice(5,7),16)];}
 function stampFog(color,seed){
  const s=surface(96,96);if(!s)return null;const im=s.g.createImageData(96,96),col=rgb(color);
  for(let y=0;y<96;y++)for(let x=0;x<96;x++){
   const dx=(x-47.5)/47,dy=(y-47.5)/47,r=Math.hypot(dx,dy),i=(y*96+x)*4;
   let noise=0,amp=.52,frequency=4;
   for(let o=0;o<3;o++){
    const xx=x/96*frequency,yy=y/96*frequency,ix=Math.floor(xx),iy=Math.floor(yy),ux=xx-ix,uy=yy-iy;
    const sx=ux*ux*(3-2*ux),sy=uy*uy*(3-2*uy),a=hash(seed+ix+iy*91+o*923),b=hash(seed+ix+1+iy*91+o*923),c=hash(seed+ix+(iy+1)*91+o*923),d=hash(seed+ix+1+(iy+1)*91+o*923);
    noise+=amp*((a+(b-a)*sx)*(1-sy)+(c+(d-c)*sx)*sy);frequency*=2;amp*=.5;
   }
   const edge=Math.max(0,1-r),density=Math.pow(edge,1.15)*clamp((noise-.12)*2.8,0,1);
   im.data[i]=col[0];im.data[i+1]=col[1];im.data[i+2]=col[2];im.data[i+3]=Math.round(density*225);
  }
  s.g.putImageData(im,0,0);return s.c;
 }
 function stampGlow(p){
  const s=surface(96,96);if(!s)return null;const g=s.g;
  const grad=g.createRadialGradient(48,48,0,48,48,47);grad.addColorStop(0,p.white+'e0');grad.addColorStop(.16,p.hot+'c0');grad.addColorStop(.40,p.mid+'45');grad.addColorStop(1,p.mid+'00');
  g.fillStyle=grad;g.fillRect(0,0,96,96);return s.c;
 }
 

 

 

 function warm(){
  if(fx.cache)return fx.cache;const c={};let bytes=0;
  for(const [key,p] of Object.entries(palettes)){c[key]={glow:stampGlow(p),fog:[stampFog(p.fog,71),stampFog(p.fog,331)]};bytes+=3*96*96*4;}
  fx.cache=c;fx.stats.cacheBytes=bytes;return c;
 }

 function glow(x,y,r,k,alpha=.5,sx=1,sy=1){
  if(alpha<=0||!visible(x,y,r))return;const im=warm()[k]?.glow;if(!im)return;
  const p=scPos(x,y);ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(alpha,0,1);ctx.drawImage(im,p.x-r*sx,p.y-r*sy,2*r*sx,2*r*sy);ctx.restore();
 }
 function particle(x,y,k,kind='chip',power=1,angle=null){
  if(!enabled())return;const cap=Math.round((reduced()?65:210)*quality());if(cap<1)return;
  if(fx.particles.length>=cap)fx.particles.shift();
  const a=angle===null?pick(0,TAU):angle+pick(-.9,.9),speed=pick(25,145)*power;
  const life=kind==='smoke'?pick(.5,1.05):kind==='spark'?pick(.13,.36):pick(.36,.85);
  fx.particles.push({x,y,z:pick(0,7),vx:Math.cos(a)*speed,vy:Math.sin(a)*speed*.70,vz:pick(45,145)*power,
   k,kind,age:0,life,rot:pick(0,TAU),spin:pick(-9,9),size:kind==='smoke'?pick(15,32)*power:kind==='spark'?pick(2,4):pick(2,5)*power,seed:Math.floor(random()*99999),bounce:false});
  fx.stats.emittedParticles++;
 }
 function burst(x,y,key,count=18,power=1,angle=null){
  if(!enabled()||!visible(x,y,300))return;fx.stats.emittedBursts++;const k=skin(key),n=Math.round(count*quality()*(reduced()?.35:1));
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++)particle(x,y,k,i%7===0?'smoke':i%3===0?'spark':i%4===0&&k==='acid'?'drop':'chip',power,angle);
 }
 function slash(x,y,aim,r,half,color){
  if(!enabled())return;fx.slashes.push({x,y,aim,r,half,age:0,duration:reduced()?.19:.34,k:combat.bossPhase===3?'rage':'bone',seed:Math.floor(random()*10000)});
  if(fx.slashes.length>9)fx.slashes.shift();fx.stats.emittedSlashes++;
  const side=combat.current?.side||1;
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=aim-half+(i/7)*half*2;particle(x+Math.cos(a)*r*.83,y+Math.sin(a)*r*.83,combat.bossPhase===3?'rage':'bone',i%3?'spark':'chip',.6,a+side*Math.PI*.5);}
 }
 function impact(x,y,key,r=80,power=1){
  if(!enabled()||!visible(x,y,r+240))return;
  fx.impacts.push({x,y,key,k:skin(key),r,age:0,duration:key==='fluid'?.65:1.3,power,seed:Math.floor(random()*99991)});
  if(fx.impacts.length>18)fx.impacts.shift();fx.stats.emittedImpacts++;
  burst(x,y,key,key==='fluid'?13:key==='wall'?28:32,power);
 }
 function flare(x,y,key,r=35,life=.22){
  if(!enabled())return;fx.flares.push({x,y,k:skin(key),r,age:0,life});if(fx.flares.length>24)fx.flares.shift();
 }
 function tick(dt){
  if(combat.paused&&!combat.dead)return;
  if(!enabled()){fx.particles.length=fx.slashes.length=fx.impacts.length=fx.trails.length=fx.flares.length=0;fx.lastBullets.length=0;return;}
  warm();fx.t+=dt;
  for(const list of [fx.slashes,fx.impacts,fx.trails,fx.flares])for(let i=list.length-1;i>=0;i--){list[i].age+=dt;if(list[i].age>=(list[i].duration||list[i].life))list.splice(i,1);}
  for(let i=fx.particles.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=fx.particles[i];p.age+=dt;if(p.age>=p.life){fx.particles.splice(i,1);continue;}
   p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.z+=p.vz*dt;p.vz-=330*dt;p.vx*=Math.exp(-dt*(p.kind==='smoke'?3:1.7));p.vy*=Math.exp(-dt*2);p.rot+=p.spin*dt;
   if(p.z<0){p.z=0;if(!p.bounce&&p.kind==='chip'){p.vz=Math.abs(p.vz)*.22;p.bounce=true;}else p.vz=0;}
  }
  for(const p of combat.projectiles)fx.seen.set(p,fx.t);
  for(const p of fx.lastBullets)if(fx.seen.get(p)!==fx.t&&!combat.dead){
   if(visible(p.x,p.y,30)){fx.stats.projectilePops++;const k=p.barrageIdV530===undefined?'spit':'barrage';for(let j=0;j<2;j++)particle(p.x,p.y,skin(k),'spark',.32,Math.atan2(p.vy,p.vx)+Math.PI);}
  }
  fx.lastBullets=combat.projectiles.slice();
  const c=combat.current,rushing=c?.stage==='ACTIVE'&&['charge','feast'].includes(c.key)&&titan.move>.2&&(c.key!=='feast'||feastStepV533(c)==='rush');
  fx.trailTimer-=dt;
  if(rushing&&fx.trailTimer<=0){
   fx.trailTimer=reduced()?.10:.035/Math.max(.5,quality());
   const id=c.id+':'+(c.rushIndex||0);if(id!==fx.lastRush){fx.lastRush=id;fx.trails.length=0;}
   fx.trails.push({x:titan.x,y:titan.y,aim:c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim,face:titan.face,age:0,duration:.42,key:c.key,k:skin(c.key),frame:owner?.bioMotionV435?.step13SampleV519?.a||'move_06'});
   if(fx.trails.length>15)fx.trails.shift();
   const heading=c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim,nx=-Math.sin(heading),ny=Math.cos(heading);for(const side of [-1,1])particle(titan.x+nx*40*side,titan.y+ny*40*side,c.key==='feast'?'feast':'dust','smoke',.7,heading+Math.PI);
  }
  fx.phaseTimer-=dt;
  if(fx.phaseTimer<=0){fx.phaseTimer=.11/Math.max(.4,quality());
   if(combat.ventTime>0||combat.phaseTransition>0){particle(titan.x+pick(-35,35),titan.y-40,combat.bossPhase===3?'rage':'bone','smoke',.6);}
  }
  fx.stats.peakParticles=Math.max(fx.stats.peakParticles,fx.particles.length);
  fx.stats.peakEvents=Math.max(fx.stats.peakEvents,fx.impacts.length+fx.slashes.length+fx.flares.length+fx.trails.length);
 }
 function ribbon(s,ground=false){
  const u=s.age/s.duration;if(u>=1||!visible(s.x,s.y,s.r+40))return;
  const p=scPos(s.x,s.y),scale=s.r*.85,idx=Math.min(3,Math.floor(u*5));
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x,p.y-(ground?0:12));ctx.rotate(s.aim+(u-.4)*.16);
  ctx.globalAlpha=ground?.17:Math.min(1,(1-u)*2.5);if(ground)ctx.scale(1,.78);
  // The crescent's empty left-side pivot stays anchored at the boss, never at its image center.
  frame('slash',idx,0,0,scale*1.35,scale*1.6,1,.08,.5);
  ctx.restore();
 }

 function cracks(e){
  if(e.age>.55)return;const p=scPos(e.x,e.y),u=clamp(e.age/.55,0,1);
  frame('eruption',Math.min(3,Math.floor(u*5)),p.x,p.y+e.r*.08,e.r*2.5,e.r*1.8,(1-u)*.27);
 }

 function impactGround(e){
  if(!visible(e.x,e.y,e.r+130))return;if(e.key==='feast'){mawV535(Math.min(3,Math.floor(e.age/.72*4)),e.x,e.y,e.r*2.55,Math.max(0,1-e.age/1.0)*.75,0,.88);return;}cracks(e);
  if(e.age<.18)glow(e.x,e.y,e.r*.8,e.k,(1-e.age/.18)*.24,1,.60);
 }

 function impactFront(e){
  const t=e.age;if(t>.72||!visible(e.x,e.y,e.r+120))return;
  if(e.key==='feast'){mawV535(Math.min(3,Math.floor(t/.72*4)),e.x,e.y,e.r*2.65,Math.min(1,(1-t/.72)*2.4),0,.9);glow(e.x,e.y,e.r,'feast',Math.max(0,.4-t));return;}
  const p=scPos(e.x,e.y),u=t/.72,index=Math.min(3,Math.floor(u*6));
  const grow=.48+.68*ease(Math.min(1,u*2.2)),size=e.r*2.45*grow;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x,p.y-e.r*.09);ctx.rotate((hash(e.seed)-.5)*.20);
  ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,(1-u)*2.4);
  frame('eruption',index,0,0,size,size*.88);
  ctx.restore();
 }

 function trailGround(){
  if(fx.trails.length<2)return;
  if(fx.trails[fx.trails.length-1].key==='feast')return feastTrailV535();
  ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  for(const side of [-1,1])for(let layer=0;layer<2;layer++){
   ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<fx.trails.length;i++){const p=fx.trails[i],q=scPos(p.x-Math.sin(p.aim)*side*42,p.y+Math.cos(p.aim)*side*42);i?ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y):ctx.moveTo(q.x,q.y);}
   ctx.globalAlpha=layer?.40:.55;ctx.strokeStyle=layer?'#dd846c':'#080d13';ctx.lineWidth=layer?1.6:6;ctx.stroke();
  }
  if(!reduced())for(let i=0;i<fx.trails.length;i+=5){
   const s=fx.trails[i],f=host.frames[s.frame],im=f?.im;if(!im?.naturalWidth)continue;const p=scPos(s.x,s.y+data.size*.22),sc=data.size/1200;
   ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-s.age/s.duration)*.11;ctx.translate(p.x,p.y);ctx.scale(s.face,1);ctx.drawImage(im,-f.ax*sc,-f.ay*sc,f.w*sc,f.h*sc);ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
 }
 function ground(){
  if(!enabled())return;warm();fx.stats.draws++;
  trailGround();feastGroundV535();for(const s of fx.slashes)ribbon(s,true);for(const e of fx.impacts)impactGround(e);
  for(const p of fx.particles){if(p.kind!=='chip'||p.z<2||!visible(p.x,p.y,20))continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=(1-p.age/p.life)*.22;ctx.fillStyle='#05090f';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(q.x,q.y,p.size*1.2,p.size*.45,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
 }
 function renderParticles(){
  const cache=warm();
  for(const p of fx.particles){
   if(!visible(p.x,p.y-p.z,90))continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y-p.z),u=p.age/p.life,alpha=Math.pow(1-u,p.kind==='smoke'?.8:1.2),pal=palettes[p.k];
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(q.x,q.y);ctx.rotate(p.rot);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(p.kind==='smoke'?.57:.9);
   if(p.kind==='smoke'){
    const im=cache[p.k].fog[p.seed%2],r=p.size*(.6+1.5*u);if(im)ctx.drawImage(im,-r,-r*.72,r*2,r*1.44);
   }else if(p.kind==='spark'){
    ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.strokeStyle=pal.hot;ctx.lineWidth=1.15;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,0);ctx.lineTo(-p.size*(2.2-u),p.size*.35);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle=pal.white;ctx.fillRect(-.8,-.8,1.6,1.6);
   }else if(p.kind==='drop'){
    ctx.fillStyle=pal.mid;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,p.size,p.size*.62,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=pal.hot;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-p.size*.2,-p.size*.2,p.size*.4,p.size*.2,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();
   }else{
    ctx.fillStyle=pal.mid;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-p.size,-p.size*.38);ctx.lineTo(p.size*.5,-p.size*.65);ctx.lineTo(p.size,p.size*.45);ctx.lineTo(-p.size*.4,p.size*.6);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle=pal.hot;ctx.lineWidth=.7;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-p.size,-p.size*.38);ctx.lineTo(p.size*.5,-p.size*.65);ctx.lineTo(p.size,p.size*.45);ctx.stroke();
   }
   ctx.restore();
  }
 }
 function projectiles(){
  for(const p of combat.projectiles){
   if(!visible(p.x,p.y,50))continue;const q=scPos(p.x,p.y),angle=Math.atan2(p.vy,p.vx),small=p.barrageIdV530!==undefined;
   const seed=hash(String(p.id).length*1009+(p.waveV530||0)*97+Math.floor(p.vx));
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(q.x,q.y);ctx.rotate(angle);const size=small?30:54;
   // The opaque gland is centered on the unchanged collision point; tendrils trail behind it.
   frame('projectile',Math.floor(fx.t*14+seed*4)%4,0,0,size,size,1,.64,.5);ctx.restore();
  }
 }

 function lobPosition(c,site,i){
  const a=ATTACKS[c.key],eta=a.windup-c.total+i*a.interval,total=a.lockBefore+i*a.interval;
  const u=clamp(1-eta/Math.max(.01,total),0,1),sx=titan.x+(i%2?-23:20),sy=titan.y-70;
  return {x:sx+(site.x-sx)*u,y:sy+(site.y-sy)*u-115*Math.sin(u*Math.PI),u,sx,sy};
 }
 function lobs(){
  const c=combat.current;if(!c||!['mortar','backfire'].includes(c.key))return;
  for(const [i,s] of c.sites.entries()){
   if(s.impacted)continue;const p=lobPosition(c,s,i);if(p.u<=0||!visible(p.x,p.y,65))continue;
   const q=scPos(p.x,p.y);ctx.save();ctx.translate(q.x,q.y);ctx.rotate(p.u*2+i*.7);
   frame('heart',Math.floor(fx.t*12+i)%4,0,0,40,40);ctx.restore();
  }
 }

 function windup(){
  const c=combat.current;if(!c||c.stage!=='WINDUP')return;if(c.key==='charge'||c.key==='feast')return rushWindupV535(c);const a=ATTACKS[c.key],u=clamp(c.t/a.windup,0,1),k=skin(c.key),pal=palettes[k];
  const cx=titan.x-titan.face*8,cy=titan.y-data.size*.39;
  glow(cx,cy,48+u*16,k,.18+u*.30,1,.65);
  const organs=['claw','twin','rear','charge','feast'].includes(c.key)?[[titan.x+titan.face*52,titan.y-18],[titan.x-titan.face*39,titan.y-5]]:[[titan.x+titan.face*65,titan.y-65]];
  ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.lineCap='round';
  for(const [ox,oy] of organs){
   const p=scPos(cx,cy),q=scPos(ox,oy);ctx.globalAlpha=.14+u*.28;ctx.strokeStyle=pal.mid;ctx.lineWidth=1.3;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.bezierCurveTo(p.x+(q.x-p.x)*.3,p.y+12,q.x-10*titan.face,q.y-20,q.x,q.y);ctx.stroke();
   for(let n=0;n<3;n++){const v=(fx.t*1.9+n/3)%1,x=cx+(ox-cx)*v,y=cy+(oy-cy)*v-7*Math.sin(v*Math.PI);glow(x,y,7+u*4,k,(.25+u*.3)*(1-v*.4));}
   glow(ox,oy,18+u*20,k,.16+u*.45,1,.6);
  }
  ctx.restore();
 }
 function pulse(c,feast=false){
  if(feast)return feastWaveV535(c);
  if(c.stage!=='ACTIVE')return;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];if(feast&&c.t<a.waveAt)return;
  const u=feast?clamp((c.t-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,0,1):clamp(c.t/a.active,0,1);
  const r=feast?a.pulseStart+(a.pulseEnd-a.pulseStart)*u:a.ringStart+(a.ringEnd-a.ringStart)*u;
  const p=scPos(feast?c.finalX:titan.x,feast?c.finalY:titan.y);
  const offset=feast?a.pulseOffset:a.gapOffset,half=feast?a.pulseHalf:a.gapHalf;
  const arcs=dangerArcsV533(c.aim,offset,half),count=Math.min(80,Math.ceil(TAU*r/42)),step=TAU/count;
  ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();
  for(const [lo,hi] of arcs){ctx.moveTo(p.x+Math.cos(lo)*(r+66),p.y+Math.sin(lo)*(r+66));ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,r+66,lo,hi);ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,Math.max(1,r-66),hi,lo,true);ctx.closePath();}ctx.clip();
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
   const angle=i*step;if(feast?feastSafe(angle,c,a):pulseSafe(angle,c.aim,a))continue;
   const x=p.x+Math.cos(angle)*r,y=p.y+Math.sin(angle)*r;if(x<-55||x>view.W+55||y<-75||y>view.H+75)continue;
   const phase=(fx.t*8+hash(i*71+c.id)*3)%3,index=Math.floor(phase);
   // Upright ivory teeth give the moving ground front real volume. The fine rim marks its hit line.
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate((hash(i+91)-.5)*.22);
   frame('spike',index,0,0,58,72,.95,.5,.76);ctx.restore();
  }
  ctx.restore();
 }

 // Uses the resource simulation clock, so pause, recall and early stomps stay synchronized.
 function countdown(s){
  const duration=Math.max(.001,Number(s.reclaimDelay)||5);
  const remaining=clamp(duration-(Number(s.age)||0),0,duration);
  return {remaining,ratio:remaining/duration,returning:!!s.returning,
   label:s.returning?'회수':(Math.ceil(Math.max(0,remaining-1e-8)*10)/10).toFixed(1)+'s'};
 }
 function resourceTimer(s,x,y){
  const state=countdown(s),r=s.r+7,urgent=state.remaining<=1.5;
  ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.setLineDash([]);
  if(!state.returning){
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,TAU);ctx.strokeStyle='#050b12d9';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.stroke();
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,-Math.PI/2,-Math.PI/2+TAU*state.ratio);
   ctx.strokeStyle=urgent?'#ff795c':'#ffe2ae';ctx.lineWidth=2.1;ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.font='700 11px system-ui';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  ctx.lineWidth=3.4;ctx.strokeStyle='#05090ff2';ctx.strokeText(state.label,x,y+r+10);
  ctx.fillStyle=state.returning?'#ffbfa1':urgent?'#ff967b':'#fff0d3';ctx.fillText(state.label,x,y+r+10);
  ctx.restore();
 }

 function resource(s,tissue=false){
  let x=s.x,y=s.y,air=0;
  if(s.scatterFromV530&&s.age<s.scatterTimeV530){const u=clamp(s.age/s.scatterTimeV530,0,1);x=s.scatterFromV530.x+(s.x-s.scatterFromV530.x)*u;y=s.scatterFromV530.y+(s.y-s.scatterFromV530.y)*u;air=42*Math.sin(u*Math.PI);}
  if(!visible(x,y-air,70))return;const p=scPos(x,y),seed=hash(Math.floor(s.x*5+s.y*13)),period=.72+seed*.22;
  const phase=(fx.t/period+seed)%1,sequence=phase*4,index=Math.floor(sequence),mix=(sequence-index)*.68;
  const beat=Math.exp(-Math.pow((phase-.30)/.09,2))*.07+Math.exp(-Math.pow((phase-.52)/.05,2))*.025;
  const twitch=reduced()?0:Math.sin(fx.t*27+seed*19)*Math.max(0,1-Math.abs(phase-.48)*13);
  ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#10060b';ctx.globalAlpha=.45;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(p.x,p.y+5,s.r*1.04,s.r*.45,0,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x+twitch*.9,p.y-air);ctx.rotate((seed-.5)*.65+(reduced()?0:twitch*.025));ctx.scale(1-beat,1+beat*.8);
  const size=s.r*2.65;frame('heart',index,0,0,size,size,1);frame('heart',(index+1)%4,0,0,size,size,mix);ctx.restore();
  resourceTimer(s,p.x,p.y-air);
  // Fine twitching tendon streaks are secondary; the actual silhouette is the painted flesh.
  if(s.returning){const b=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-28);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.38;ctx.strokeStyle='#b94947';ctx.lineWidth=.9;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x,p.y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo((p.x+b.x)/2,(p.y+b.y)/2-13,p.x+(b.x-p.x)*.28,p.y+(b.y-p.y)*.28);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
 }

 function states(){
  if(combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)return;const k=combat.bossPhase===3||combat.phaseTarget===3?'rage':'bone';
  glow(titan.x,titan.y-68,85,k,.32+Math.sin(fx.t*8)*.10,1,.7);
  const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y-65);ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.strokeStyle=palettes[k].hot;ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.globalAlpha=.55;
  for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=i/6*TAU+fx.t*.8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x+Math.cos(a)*23,p.y+Math.sin(a)*18);ctx.bezierCurveTo(p.x+Math.cos(a+.4)*40,p.y+Math.sin(a+.4)*20-12,p.x+Math.cos(a-.2)*55,p.y+Math.sin(a-.2)*25-25,p.x+Math.cos(a)*67,p.y+Math.sin(a)*35-30);ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.restore();
 }
 function rushFront(){
  const c=combat.current;if(!c||c.stage!=='ACTIVE'||!['charge','feast'].includes(c.key)||titan.move<.25)return;
  if(c.key==='feast'&&c.t>=ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd)return;
  if(c.key==='feast')return feastRushV535(c);
  const a=c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim,ux=Math.cos(a),uy=Math.sin(a),nx=-uy,ny=ux,k='bone',pal=palettes[k];
  ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='screen';ctx.lineCap='round';
  for(const sign of [-1,1])for(let j=0;j<5;j++){
   const phase=(fx.t*2.8+j*.19)%1,span=30+phase*88,side=sign*(38+j*4),p=scPos(titan.x+nx*side,titan.y+ny*side-7);
   ctx.globalAlpha=(1-phase)*(.24+j*.025);ctx.strokeStyle=j%2?pal.mid:pal.hot;ctx.lineWidth=j===0?2:1;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x+ux*20,p.y+uy*20);ctx.bezierCurveTo(p.x-ux*12+nx*sign*9,p.y-uy*12+ny*sign*9,p.x-ux*span*.6+nx*sign*14,p.y-uy*span*.6+ny*sign*14,p.x-ux*span+nx*sign*(20+phase*13),p.y-uy*span+ny*sign*(20+phase*13));ctx.stroke();
  }
  ctx.restore();
 }
 function mawV535(index,x,y,size,alpha=1,rotation=0,stretch=1){
  const p=scPos(x,y);ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x,p.y);ctx.rotate(rotation);frame('maw',index,0,0,size,size*stretch,alpha);ctx.restore();
 }
 function rushWindupV535(c){
  const a=ATTACKS[c.key],u=clamp(c.t/a.windup,0,1),feast=c.key==='feast',dir=c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim,ux=Math.cos(dir),uy=Math.sin(dir);
  if(feast){
   mawV535(u<.66?0:1,titan.x+ux*52,titan.y+uy*52,112+u*38,.34+u*.42,fx.t*.16,.86);
   glow(titan.x+ux*52,titan.y+uy*52,34+u*20,'feast',.23+u*.28,1,.72);
   for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const f=(fx.t*1.5+i/5)%1,angle=i/5*TAU+fx.t*.3,dist=(1-f)*83;
    glow(titan.x+ux*52+Math.cos(angle)*dist,titan.y+uy*52+Math.sin(angle)*dist,5+f*7,'feast',.35*f);}
  }else{
   const p=scPos(titan.x+ux*47,titan.y+uy*47);ctx.save();ctx.translate(p.x,p.y);ctx.rotate(dir);
   for(const sign of [-1,1]){ctx.save();ctx.scale(1,sign);frame('slash',Math.min(3,Math.floor(u*4)),0,sign*16,100+u*25,82,.22+u*.32,.08,.5);ctx.restore();}
   ctx.restore();glow(titan.x,titan.y-54,30+u*17,'bone',.16+u*.30,1,.65);
  }
 }
 function feastTrailV535(){
  if(!fx.trails.length)return;ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  for(const sign of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<fx.trails.length;i++){const p=fx.trails[i],q=scPos(p.x-Math.sin(p.aim)*sign*27,p.y+Math.cos(p.aim)*sign*27);i?ctx.lineTo(q.x,q.y):ctx.moveTo(q.x,q.y);}
   ctx.globalAlpha=.32;ctx.lineWidth=12;ctx.strokeStyle='#3c153e';ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=.68;ctx.lineWidth=2.4;ctx.strokeStyle='#a0e64c';ctx.stroke();}
  ctx.restore();for(let i=0;i<fx.trails.length;i+=3){const p=fx.trails[i];mawV535(0,p.x,p.y,46,Math.max(0,1-p.age/p.duration)*.46,p.aim,.72);}
 }
 function feastRushV535(c){
  const dir=c.rushHeadingV535??c.aim,ux=Math.cos(dir),uy=Math.sin(dir),nx=-uy,ny=ux;
  mawV535(Math.floor(fx.t*10)%2+1,titan.x+ux*69,titan.y+uy*69,150,.94,dir+Math.PI/2,.86);
  glow(titan.x+ux*69,titan.y+uy*69,48,'feast',.31,1,.7);
  const p=scPos(titan.x,titan.y);ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
  for(const sign of [-1,1]){const wobble=reduced()?0:Math.sin(fx.t*21+sign)*10;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(p.x+ux*72+nx*sign*23,p.y+uy*72+ny*sign*23);
   ctx.bezierCurveTo(p.x+nx*sign*(61+wobble),p.y+ny*sign*(61+wobble),p.x-ux*59+nx*sign*51,p.y-uy*59+ny*sign*51,p.x-ux*126+nx*sign*32,p.y-uy*126+ny*sign*32);
   ctx.globalAlpha=.9;ctx.strokeStyle='#2b102d';ctx.lineWidth=8;ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#b377c3';ctx.lineWidth=2.6;ctx.stroke();
  }ctx.restore();
 }
 function feastGroundV535(){
  const c=combat.current;if(c?.key!=='feast'||c.stage!=='ACTIVE')return;
  const step=feastStepV533(c);if(step==='rush')return;
  const a=ATTACKS.feast,u=step==='slam'?clamp((c.t-a.chargeEnd)/(a.slamAt-a.chargeEnd),0,1):step==='waveReady'?1:clamp(1-(c.t-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,0,1);
  mawV535(step==='wave'?3:step==='waveReady'?2:u>.45?1:0,c.finalX,c.finalY,a.slamRadius*2.75,.52+u*.30,0,.9);
 }
 function feastWaveV535(c){
  if(c.stage!=='ACTIVE'||c.t<ATTACKS.feast.waveAt)return;
  const a=ATTACKS.feast,u=clamp((c.t-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,0,1),r=a.pulseStart+(a.pulseEnd-a.pulseStart)*u,p=scPos(c.finalX,c.finalY),arcs=dangerArcsV533(c.aim,a.pulseOffset,a.pulseHalf);
  ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
  for(const [lo,hi] of arcs){
   ctx.beginPath();for(let v=lo;v<=hi+.001;v+=.035){const w=Math.min(v,hi),rr=r+Math.sin(w*19+fx.t*12)*3,x=p.x+Math.cos(w)*rr,y=p.y+Math.sin(w)*rr;v===lo?ctx.moveTo(x,y):ctx.lineTo(x,y);}
   ctx.globalAlpha=.65;ctx.strokeStyle='#452047';ctx.lineWidth=18;ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=.95;ctx.strokeStyle='#91d840';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.stroke();ctx.globalAlpha=.75;ctx.strokeStyle='#e2ffc0';ctx.lineWidth=1.3;ctx.stroke();
   for(let w=lo+.10;w<hi-.08;w+=.23){const x=p.x+Math.cos(w)*r,y=p.y+Math.sin(w)*r;if(x<-50||x>view.W+50||y<-50||y>view.H+50)continue;ctx.globalAlpha=.7;ctx.fillStyle='#b8f571';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(x,y,4,7,w,0,TAU);ctx.fill();}
  }ctx.restore();
 }

 function front(){
  if(!enabled())return;fx.stats.draws++;warm();
  for(const e of fx.impacts)impactFront(e);
  for(const s of fx.slashes)ribbon(s,false);
  renderParticles();for(const e of fx.flares){const u=e.age/e.life;glow(e.x,e.y,e.r*(.6+.6*u),e.k,Math.pow(1-u,1.4)*.65);}
  windup();states();rushFront();lobs();projectiles();
  const c=combat.current;if(c?.key==='pulse')pulse(c,false);if(c?.key==='feast')pulse(c,true);
  ctx.save();for(const e of combat.hitEffects){const p=scPos(e.x,e.y);ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,e.t/.35);ctx.fillStyle='#ffdbbd';ctx.font='bold 19px system-ui';ctx.fillText(typeof e.damage==='number'?'-'+e.damage:e.damage,p.x+12,p.y-20-(.45-e.t)*27);}ctx.restore();
 }
 const base={resetCombat,update,fx11Slash,fx11Impact,fx11Particle,launchSpit,launchBarrageV530,popResourceV528,beginCrashV529,resolveCrashV529,renderResourceV528,renderCombatEffects,fx11RenderGround,fx11Render};
 resetCombat=function(...args){const out=base.resetCombat(...args);reset();return out;};
 update=function(dt){base.update(dt);tick(dt);};
 fx11Particle=function(...args){if(!enabled())return base.fx11Particle(...args);};
 fx11Slash=function(...args){const out=base.fx11Slash(...args);slash(...args);return out;};
 fx11Impact=function(...args){const out=base.fx11Impact(...args);impact(...args);return out;};
 launchSpit=function(c,i){const out=base.launchSpit(c,i),a=c.aim,m=c.muzzleV530||60;flare(titan.x+Math.cos(a)*m,titan.y+Math.sin(a)*m,'spit',29,.16);return out;};
 launchBarrageV530=function(c,i){const out=base.launchBarrageV530(c,i);flare(titan.x,titan.y-43,'barrage',45,.24);burst(titan.x,titan.y-18,'barrage',6,.45);return out;};
 popResourceV528=function(p){const out=base.popResourceV528(p);impact(p.x,p.y,'fluid',27,.55);return out;};
 beginCrashV529=function(c){const ok=base.beginCrashV529(c);if(ok)impact(titan.x,titan.y,'wall',83,.95);return ok;};
 resolveCrashV529=function(){const failures=combat.stats.crashFailures,out=base.resolveCrashV529();if(combat.stats.crashFailures>failures)burst(titan.x,titan.y-40,'bone',17,.70);return out;};
 renderResourceV528=function(...args){return resource(...args);};
 renderCombatEffects=function(){if(enabled())return front();projectiles();ctx.save();for(const e of combat.hitEffects){const p=scPos(e.x,e.y);ctx.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,e.t/.35);ctx.fillStyle='#ffdbbd';ctx.font='bold 19px system-ui';ctx.fillText(typeof e.damage==='number'?'-'+e.damage:e.damage,p.x+12,p.y-20-(.45-e.t)*27);}ctx.restore();};
 fx11RenderGround=function(){if(enabled())return ground();return base.fx11RenderGround();};
 fx11Render=function(){if(!enabled())return;};
 // Warm the reusable textures during initial loading, instead of on the first skill impact.
 setTimeout(()=>{if(options.fx)warm();},1000);
 return {version:535,ready:artReady,enabled,warm,reset,snapshot:()=>({enabled:enabled(),artLoaded:Object.values(art).filter(a=>a.im&&(a.im.naturalWidth||a.im.width)).length,artFailed:Object.values(art).filter(a=>a.failed).length,artRGBABytes:Object.values(art).reduce((n,a)=>n+a.size*a.size*a.cols*a.rows*4,0),particles:fx.particles.length,slashes:fx.slashes.length,impacts:fx.impacts.length,trails:fx.trails.length,flares:fx.flares.length,...fx.stats}),diagnostic:qaMode?{fx,art,frame,impact,slash,burst,front,ground,projectiles,resource,countdown,resourceTimer,tick}:null};
})();

// Draw ground refraction and particles in their own Canvas pass. World-space warning overlays stay intact.

 function warningContains(c,px=host.player.x,py=host.player.y){
  if(!c)return false;const a=ATTACKS[c.key];
  if(a.kind==='cone')return inSector(px,py,c.aim+c.side*.09,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle);
  if(a.kind==='twin')return [-.30*c.side,.30*c.side].some(o=>inSector(px,py,c.aim+o,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle));
  if(a.kind==='rear')return inSector(px,py,c.aim+Math.PI,a.maxRange,a.halfAngle);
  if(a.kind==='circle')return hypot(px-c.targetX,py-c.targetY)<=a.radius+PLAYER_R;
  if(a.kind==='mortar'||a.kind==='sacs'||a.kind==='backfire'){
   const sites=c.sites.length?c.sites:[{x:c.targetX,y:c.targetY,r:a.radius}];
   return sites.some(s=>!s.impacted&&hypot(px-s.x,py-s.y)<=(s.r||a.radius)+PLAYER_R);
  }
  if(a.kind==='pulse'){
   const d=hypot(px-titan.x,py-titan.y);
   return d>=a.ringStart-PLAYER_R-a.ringWidth*.5&&d<=a.ringEnd+PLAYER_R+a.ringWidth*.5&&pulseDangerAt(px,py,c);
  }
  if(a.kind==='barrage'){const p=c.barrageV530;return !!p&&hypot(px-p.originX,py-p.originY)<=p.range+58;}
  if(a.kind==='volley')return a.spread.some(offset=>{const angle=c.aim+offset,ux=Math.cos(angle),uy=Math.sin(angle);return segCircle(titan.x+ux*60,titan.y+uy*60,titan.x+ux*(a.maxRange+60),titan.y+uy*(a.maxRange+60),px,py,11+PLAYER_R);});
  if(a.kind==='charge')return inRushWarningV528(c,px,py,a);
  if(a.kind==='feast'){
   const step=feastStepV533(c),d=hypot(px-c.finalX,py-c.finalY);
   if(step==='rush')return inRushWarningV528(c,px,py,a);
   if(step==='slam')return d<=a.slamRadius+PLAYER_R;
   return d>=a.pulseStart-PLAYER_R-a.pulseWidth*.5&&d<=a.pulseEnd+PLAYER_R+a.pulseWidth*.5&&!feastSafe(Math.atan2(py-c.finalY,px-c.finalX),c,a);
  }
  return false;
 }
 const combatStartHostV519=startAttack,combatActiveHostV519=enterActive;
 startAttack=function(...args){
  const ok=combatStartHostV519(...args);
  if(ok){combat.current.warningThreatened=warningContains(combat.current);host.notePattern(ATTACKS[args[0]].name);}
  return ok;
 };
 enterActive=function(c){
  const escaped=c.warningThreatened&&!warningContains(c)&&!c.evasionAwarded;
  const out=combatActiveHostV519(c);
  if(escaped&&owner&&!owner.dead&&!host.ended()){c.evasionAwarded=true;host.awardEvasion(owner);}
  return out;
 };
 function syncDimensions(){
  const d=host.dims();
  if(WORLD_W!==d.WORLD_W||WORLD_H!==d.WORLD_H){
   WORLD_W=d.WORLD_W;WORLD_H=d.WORLD_H;NAV_X=Math.ceil(WORLD_W/NAV_CELL);NAV_Y=Math.ceil(WORLD_H/NAV_CELL);navDirty=true;
  }
  PLAYER_R=host.player.r||10;player.r=PLAYER_R;player.speed=host.player.speed||132;
  view.W=d.W;view.H=d.H;view.DPR=d.DPR;
  const c=host.camera();view.camX=c.x;view.camY=c.y;
 }
 function syncNavigation(){
  if(!navDirty)return;syncDimensions();
  obstacles=host.terrain().filter(n=>!n.arenaHidden).map(n=>{
   const b=host.box(n);return {x:b.l,y:b.t,w:b.r-b.l,h:b.b-b.t};
  }).filter(b=>b.w>0&&b.h>0).concat(host.extraObstacles());
  rebuildNav();navDirty=false;titan.path=[];titan.repathTimer=0;
 }
 function syncPlayer(){
  player.x=host.player.x;player.y=host.player.y;
  combat.playerHP=host.player.hp;combat.invuln=Math.max(combat.invuln,host.player.inv||0);
 }
 function bind(e){
  if(owner===e)return;
  owner=e;syncDimensions();syncPlayer();resetCombat(true);resetMotion();
  Object.assign(titan,{x:e.x,y:e.y,face:host.player.x<e.x?-1:1,
   heading:Math.atan2(host.player.y-e.y,host.player.x-e.x),vx:0,vy:0,move:0,anim:0,
   path:[],routeIndex:0,repathTimer:0,lockTimer:0,turnTimer:0,pivotTimer:0,stuckT:0,footsteps:0,
   targetX:host.player.x,targetY:host.player.y,approaching:false,turns:0,repaths:0,contacts:0});
  combat.bossHP=e.hp/e.maxHp*4200;combat.bossMaxHP=4200;combat.playerHP=host.player.hp;
  if(e.encounterPhaseV526){combat.bossPhase=e.encounterPhaseV526;applyPhaseProfileV528(combat.bossPhase);e.titanPhase=combat.bossPhase;}
  combat.auto=options.auto;combat.paused=false;combat.nextDecision=.28;
  e.bioStep13V519={version:VERSION,active:true};e.burst=0;e.telegraphing=null;e.patternLock=0;
  readyWait=0;navDirty=true;syncNavigation();host.clearLegacy(e);
  recordEvent('BIO TITAN 13단계 본편 연결 · '+(combat.auto?'자동 전투':'수동 진단'));
 }
 function commitPosition(dt,previousX,previousY){
  if(!owner||owner.dead)return;
  owner.x=titan.x;owner.y=titan.y;
  owner.vx=dt>0?(titan.x-previousX)/dt:titan.vx;owner.vy=dt>0?(titan.y-previousY)/dt:titan.vy;
  owner.kx=owner.ky=0;owner.burst=0;owner.telegraphing=null;owner.patternLock=0;
  owner.titanPhase=combat.bossPhase;
  host.player.x=player.x;host.player.y=player.y;
  if(combat.bossHP!==lastEngineHP&&owner.hp>0){
   const change=(combat.bossHP-lastEngineHP)*owner.maxHp/4200;
   owner.hp=Math.max(0,Math.min(owner.maxHp,owner.hp+change));
  }
 }
 function tick(e,dt){
  if(!host.isBio(e)||e.dead)return false;
  bind(e);syncDimensions();syncNavigation();syncPlayer();
  titan.x=e.x;titan.y=e.y;lastEngineHP=combat.bossHP=e.hp/e.maxHp*4200;
  if(e.hp<=0){host.kill(e);return true;}
  updatePhaseTarget();
  if(combat.phasePending&&!combat.current&&combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)beginPhaseTransition();
  const loading=host.loadState();
  if(!loading.fullReady&&!loading.failed&&readyWait<12&&!e.encounterPhaseV526){
   host.warm();readyWait+=dt;e.vx=e.vy=e.kx=e.ky=0;combat.lastReason='BIO TITAN 모션 준비 중';
   syncMotion(e,dt);return true;
  }
  const beforeX=titan.x,beforeY=titan.y;
  const cfg=host.feedback();
  motion.enabled=options.motion&&!cfg.reducedMotion;
  FX11.enabled=options.fx;FX11.quality=options.quality*(cfg.graphics==='eco'?.55:1);
  FX11.volume=options.volume;FX11.shakeEnabled=cfg.shake>0&&!cfg.reducedMotion;
  if(FX11.soundOn&&cfg.sound!==false)fx11AudioUserGesture();
  update(dt);commitPosition(dt,beforeX,beforeY);
  if(owner&&!owner.dead)syncMotion(owner,dt);
  updateQAPanel();
  return true;
 }
 function postUpdate(){
  if(owner?.dead&&!combat.dead){reset();return;}
  if(!owner||owner.dead||host.ended())return;
  if(!host.enemies().includes(owner)){reset();return;}
  syncPlayer();syncDimensions();
  // Crowd separation must not move a locked attack origin after the telegraph has been drawn.
  if(combat.current||combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0||combat.phaseTransition>0||combat.ventTime>0){owner.x=titan.x;owner.y=titan.y;}
  else {titan.x=owner.x;titan.y=owner.y;}
  clearWallCollision();owner.x=titan.x;owner.y=titan.y;
  resolveBody();host.player.x=player.x;host.player.y=player.y;host.resolvePlayerWalls();
 }
 function incomingMultiplier(e){
  if(e!==owner||combat.dead||combat.ventTime<=0)return 1;
  return combat.bossPhase===3?1.30:combat.bossPhase===2?1.25:1.20;
 }
 function onDamage(e,beforeHP){
  if(!host.isBio(e)||e.dead||e.hp<=0)return;
  bind(e);combat.bossHP=e.hp/e.maxHp*4200;
  if(beforeHP>e.hp){motionHit();recordEvent('본편 무기 피해 '+(beforeHP-e.hp).toFixed(1));}
  updatePhaseTarget();
  if(combat.phasePending&&!combat.current&&combat.ventTime<=0&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)beginPhaseTransition();
 }
 function onDeath(e){
  if(!host.isBio(e))return;
  if(owner!==e)bind(e);
  titan.x=e.x;titan.y=e.y;combat.bossHP=0;finishBossDeath();
  if(e.bioMotionV435)delete e.bioMotionV435.step13SampleV519;
  if(e.bioStep13V519)e.bioStep13V519.active=false;
  updateQAPanel(true);
 }
 function reset(){
  owner=null;resetCombat(true);resetMotion();navDirty=true;readyWait=0;lastUIAt=-100;
  if(qaPanel)qaPanel.hidden=true;
 }
 function resolveBossTerrain(e){
  if(!host.isBio(e)||e.dead)return false;
  syncDimensions();syncNavigation();
  const oldX=titan.x,oldY=titan.y,oldVx=titan.vx,oldVy=titan.vy;
  titan.x=e.x;titan.y=e.y;clearWallCollision();
  e.x=clamp(titan.x,data.rx+3,WORLD_W-data.rx-3);
  e.y=clamp(titan.y,data.ry+12,WORLD_H-data.ry-12);
  if(owner!==e){titan.x=oldX;titan.y=oldY;titan.vx=oldVx;titan.vy=oldVy;}
  return true;
 }
 function syncMotion(e,dt){
  if(e!==owner||e.dead)return false;
  const m=host.motion(e);m.face=titan.face;m.phase=combat.bossPhase;
  m.speed=hypot(e.vx||titan.vx,e.vy||titan.vy);m.moving=titan.move>.14;
  let group='idle',index=1,state='idle',progress=0;
  const c=combat.current;
  if(combat.crashV529?.stage==='STUN'){
   group='death';state='groggy';progress=clamp(combat.crashV529.elapsed/.70,0,1);index=1+Math.min(7,Math.floor(progress*8));
  }else if(combat.crashV529?.stage==='RECALL'){
   group='special';state='crashRecall';index=5+Math.min(2,Math.floor(combat.crashV529.recallElapsed/.80*3));
  }else if(combat.recoilV529>0){
   group='hit';state='crashRecoil';progress=clamp(1-combat.recoilV529/.52,0,.99999);index=1+Math.floor(progress*10);
  }else if(combat.phaseTransition>0){
   group='special';state='phase';progress=clamp(1-combat.phaseTransition/(combat.phaseTarget===3?PHASE3_TIME:PHASE2_TIME),0,.99999);index=1+Math.floor(progress*10);
  }else if(combat.ventTime>0){group='special';state='guardCast';index=1+(Math.floor(combat.clock*8)%10);}
  else if(c){
   const a=ATTACKS[c.key];progress=clamp(c.t/Math.max(.001,c.stage==='WINDUP'?a.windup:c.stage==='ACTIVE'?a.active:a.recovery),0,.99999);
   if(['claw','twin','rear'].includes(c.key)){group='melee';state=c.stage==='WINDUP'?'meleeReady':'attack';}
   else if(['spit','barrage','mortar','backfire'].includes(c.key)){group='cast';state=c.stage==='WINDUP'?'acidReady':'acidCast';}
   else if(c.key==='charge'||(c.key==='feast'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'&&c.t<ATTACKS.feast.chargeEnd&&!c.chargeFinished)){
    if(c.stage==='ACTIVE'){group='move';state='charge';index=1+(Math.floor(titan.anim)%10);}
    else {group='melee';state=c.stage==='WINDUP'?'windupCharge':'attack';}
   }else {group='special';state=c.stage==='WINDUP'?'spiralReady':'spiralCast';}
   if(group!=='move'){
    // Preserve the native V501 ready/impact split: melee/special 1..4 -> 5..7; cast 1..5 -> 6..7.
    const readyCount=group==='cast'?5:4,activeCount=group==='cast'?2:3;
    index=c.stage==='WINDUP'?1+Math.floor(progress*readyCount):c.stage==='ACTIVE'?readyCount+1+Math.floor(progress*activeCount):8+Math.floor(progress*3);
   }
   if(c.key==='feast'&&c.stage==='ACTIVE'&&feastStepV533(c)!=='rush'){
    const step=feastStepV533(c),a=ATTACKS.feast;group='special';state='feast_'+step;
    const u=step==='slam'?clamp((c.t-a.chargeEnd)/(a.slamAt-a.chargeEnd),0,.999):step==='waveReady'?clamp((c.t-a.slamAt)/(a.waveAt-a.slamAt),0,.999):clamp((c.t-a.waveAt)/a.waveDuration,0,.999);
    index=step==='slam'?1+Math.floor(u*4):step==='waveReady'?5+Math.floor(u*3):8+Math.floor(u*3);
   }
  }else if(t-(m.hitAt??-99)<.52){
   group='hit';state='hit';index=1+Math.min(9,Math.floor((t-m.hitAt)/.52*10));
  }else if(titan.move>.14){group='move';state='move';index=1+(Math.floor(titan.anim)%10);}
  else {group='idle';state='idle';index=1+(Math.floor(combat.clock*8)%10);}
  const key=group+'_'+String(clamp(index,1,10)).padStart(2,'0');
  const available=host.frames[key]?.im?.naturalWidth?key:'idle_01';
  if(m.state!==state){m.previousState=m.state;m.at=t;}
  m.state=state;m.progress=progress;m.clock=titan.anim;
  if(c){m.releaseAt=t-c.t;m.duration=c.stage==='ACTIVE'?ATTACKS[c.key].active:ATTACKS[c.key].recovery;m.releaseKind=c.key;}
  m.px=e.x;m.py=e.y;m.step13SampleV519={a:available,index:index-1,b:null,mix:0};
  return true;
 }
 function inViewport(fn){
  syncDimensions();ctx.save();ctx.translate(view.camX,view.camY);
  try{fn();}finally{ctx.restore();}
 }
 function drawFloor(){
  if(!owner||owner.dead)return;
  inViewport(()=>{renderTissues();renderSacs();fx11RenderGround();renderMotionGround();});
 }
 function drawEffects(){
  if(!owner)return;
  inViewport(()=>{renderCombatEffects();fx11Render();});
 }
 function drawWarningOverlay(){
  if(!owner||owner.dead||!combat.current||combat.current.stage!=='WINDUP')return;
  syncDimensions();const c=combat.current;
  drawBioAttackWarningV582({c,a:ATTACKS[c.key],titan,route:()=>rushRouteV535(c),body:ellipse,
   bodyOffset:data.oy,playerRadius:PLAYER_R,arena:host.arena(),special:()=>warningShapesV533(c),
   arcs:dangerArcsV533,feastStep:feastStepV533(c),windup:c.stage==='WINDUP',barrage:barrageAnglesV530});
 }
 function imminent(){
  if(!owner||owner.dead)return null;
  const p=host.player,c=combat.current;let best=null;
  if(c&&c.stage==='WINDUP'&&warningContains(c)){
   best={level:Math.max(.32,c.t/ATTACKS[c.key].windup),ang:c.aim,color:'#ff765f'};
  }
  for(const q of combat.projectiles){
   const distance=hypot(p.x-q.x,p.y-q.y);
   if(distance<62){const d={level:clamp(1-distance/62,.25,.85),ang:Math.atan2(q.y-p.y,q.x-p.x),color:'#8cf1a2'};if(!best||d.level>best.level)best=d;}
  }
  return best;
 }
 function drawBodyOverlay(e,size,alpha){
  if(combat.crashV529?.stage==='STUN')return;
  if(e!==owner||e.dead||!motion.enabled||combat.bossPhase<2&&combat.phaseTransition<=0)return;
  const previous=data.size;data.size=size;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(e.x,e.y+size*.22);ctx.scale(titan.face,1);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;
  // STEP12's provisional annotation coordinates extend above the V501 idle silhouette.
  // Keep the supplied crack/core effects inside the actual torso, without changing any sprite or its ground anchor.
  ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,-size*.42,size*.30,size*.22,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.clip();ctx.translate(0,size*.18);
  try{
   if(combat.bossPhase>=2||combat.phaseTransition>0)drawPhase2Armor(size/1200);
   if(combat.bossPhase===3||combat.phaseTarget===3)drawPhase3Core(size/1200);
  }finally{ctx.restore();data.size=previous;}
 }
 function drawIntent(e){
  if(!e||e!==owner||e.dead)return false;
  const el=host.intentBadge(),c=combat.current;
  if(!el)return false;
  const label=combat.crashV529?(combat.crashV529.stage==='STUN'?'기절 '+combat.crashV529.remaining.toFixed(1)+'초':'진액 회수 '+combat.crashV529.recovered+'/6'):combat.recoilV529>0?'회수 실패 · 체력 5% 손실':combat.phaseTransition>0?'PHASE '+combat.phaseTarget+' · 변이':
   combat.ventTime>0?'강제 배기 · 피해 '+incomingMultiplier(e).toFixed(2)+'배':
   c?(c.key==='feast'?feastLabelV533(c):ATTACKS[c.key].name+' · '+(c.stage==='WINDUP'?(c.locked?'표적 고정':'공격 예고'):c.stage==='ACTIVE'?'실행':'회복')):
   !host.loadState().fullReady&&readyWait<12?'모션 준비 중':combat.auto?'추격 중':'수동 진단';
  el.style.display='block';el.textContent='P'+combat.bossPhase+' '+phaseProfileV528().label+' · '+label+' · 진액 '+(combat.sacs.length+combat.tissues.length);
  el.classList.toggle('danger',!!c&&c.stage!=='RECOVERY');
  return true;
 }
 function snapshot(){
  return {version:VERSION,qaMode,attached:!!owner&&!owner.dead,ownerName:owner?.bossName||null,
   bossHP:owner?.hp??null,maxHP:owner?.maxHp??null,balance:owner?.bioBalanceV520?{...owner.bioBalanceV520}:null,bossPhase:combat.bossPhase,phase:combat.phase,
   phasePending:combat.phasePending,pendingPhase:combat.pendingPhase,phaseTarget:combat.phaseTarget,phaseTransition:combat.phaseTransition,
   pressure:combat.pressure,ventTime:combat.ventTime,ventDuration:combat.ventDuration,dead:combat.dead,auto:combat.auto,
   current:combat.current?JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(combat.current)):null,crash:combat.crashV529?{...combat.crashV529}:null,recoil:combat.recoilV529,
   projectiles:combat.projectiles.map(p=>({...p})),tissues:combat.tissues.map(p=>({...p})),sacs:combat.sacs.map(p=>({...p})),
   cooldowns:{...combat.cooldowns},stats:{...combat.stats},events:[...combat.events],
   ai:{mode:aiBrain.mode,intent:aiBrain.intent,reason:aiBrain.reason,decisions:aiBrain.decisions,ranked:aiBrain.lastScores.map(v=>({...v}))},

 }
 function manual(key){
  if(!qaMode||!owner||owner.dead||host.ended())return false;
  syncPlayer();titan.x=owner.x;titan.y=owner.y;
  options.auto=false;const out=requestAttack(key);updateQAPanel(true);return out;
 }
 function forcePhase(phase){
  if(!qaMode||!owner||owner.dead||host.ended())return false;
  syncPlayer();
  const out=phase===3?requestPhase3():phase===2?requestPhase2():false;
  if(out)owner.hp=Math.min(owner.hp,combat.bossHP/4200*owner.maxHp);
  updateQAPanel(true);return out;
 }
 function setAuto(on){
  if(!qaMode)return false;options.auto=!!on;combat.auto=!!on;combat.nextDecision=.18;
  if(on){fx11AudioUserGesture();recordEvent('자동 전투 ON');}else recordEvent('자동 전투 OFF');
  updateQAPanel(true);return true;
 }
 let qaPanel=null;
 function updateQAPanel(force=false){
  if(!qaPanel)return;
  qaPanel.hidden=!owner||owner.dead||!host.running();
  if(!force&&combat.clock-lastUIAt<.15)return;lastUIAt=combat.clock;
  const p=qaPanel.querySelector('[data-status]');
  if(p)p.textContent='P'+combat.bossPhase+' · '+combat.phase+' · 압력 '+Math.round(combat.pressure)+' · '+(combat.auto?'자동 ON':'수동');
  for(const b of qaPanel.querySelectorAll('[data-skill]')){
   const a=ATTACKS[b.dataset.skill];b.disabled=(a.phase||1)>combat.bossPhase||!!combat.crashV529||combat.recoilV529>0||combat.phaseTransition>0||combat.ventTime>0;
  }
 }

const BIO_HOST_BASE_V519={reset,returnToHome,hurtEnemy,killEnemy,resolveTerrainBody,rebuildTerrainGridV424,updateBossIntent,updateBioMotionV435,bioSampleV435,drawBioMotionV435,imminentDanger};

 if(!isBioTitanV435(e))return BIO_HOST_BASE_V519.hurtEnemy(e,dmg,kind);
 const before=e.hp;const out=BIO_HOST_BASE_V519.hurtEnemy(e,dmg*BIO_STEP13_V519.incomingMultiplier(e),kind);

function fenceStripV526(side){
 const scale=Math.max(1,Math.min(DPR,3)),asset=BIO_BARRIER_V535,key=side.toFixed(2)+':'+scale+':'+asset.loaded;
 if(fenceStripCacheV526?.key===key)return fenceStripCacheV526;

 g.fillStyle='#14070b55';g.fillRect(0,23,side,21);
 const count=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(side/182)),tile=side/count;
 if(asset.loaded){
  // Turn the bone spikes inward on every rotated arena side. Draw once into the DPR cache.
  g.save();g.translate(0,height);g.scale(1,-1);
  for(let i=0;i<count;i++)g.drawImage(asset.im,0,0,768,256,i*tile-2,0,tile+4,height);
  g.restore();
 }else{
  g.strokeStyle='#793036';g.lineWidth=12;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,24);g.lineTo(side,24);g.stroke();
  for(let x=12;x<side;x+=28){g.fillStyle='#c1b09b';g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-7,23);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+3,33,x+9,58);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+15,33,x+6,21);g.closePath();g.fill();}
 }

}
function drawStageArenaV526(){

  {x:a.r,y:a.b,rot:Math.PI,l:a.l-40,r:a.r+40,t:a.b-40,b:a.b+40},
  {x:a.r,y:a.t,rot:Math.PI/2,l:a.r-40,r:a.r+40,t:a.t-40,b:a.b+40},
  {x:a.l,y:a.b,rot:-Math.PI/2,l:a.l-40,r:a.l+40,t:a.t-40,b:a.b+40}];
 for(const edge of edges){if(edge.r<v.l||edge.l>v.r||edge.b<v.t||edge.t>v.b)continue;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(edge.x,edge.y);ctx.rotate(edge.rot);ctx.drawImage(strip.canvas,0,-strip.height/2,a.side,strip.height);

}

 // The existing damage function still owns armor, shields, test protection and defeat handling.

}
