// V539: separately drawn articulated poses, phase-timed animation and queued decoding.
// Visual state reads the dedicated VENOM engine. It does not schedule attacks or move bodies.
const VENOM_RENDER_V539=(()=>{
 'use strict';
 const spitAsset={"src":"assets/ea4701a1ab4ec002685d1159781400447bd2e70ead26bd2cd338ce277d2a86f6.png","width":1254,"cells":[{"y":0,"h":330,"tipY":193},{"y":330,"h":315,"tipY":171},{"y":645,"h":307,"tipY":162},{"y":952,"h":302,"tipY":138}],"tipX":1198};let spitAtlas=null,carryFrame="",carryAt=-99,lastCycle="";
 const art=VENOM_GRAPHICS_V539,cache=new Map(),pending=new Map(),shadows=new Map(),fxImages={},motes=[],bursts=[],deaths=[];
 const queue=[],queued=new Set(),pinned=new Set(['idle_00']),maxDecoded=22,TAU=Math.PI*2,U=56;
 let lastStage='',lastAttack=null,lastShots=0,lastPhase=1,face=1,turnAt=-99,phaseAt=-99,emitAt=0,lastSpriteKey='',clockKey='',visualTime=0,lastInfectionStep='';
 let decodedBytes=0,peakBytes=0,failed=[],lastS=null,serial=0,enabled=true,wantedClip='',selectedFrame='',fallbackDraws=0,draws=0,lastHp=null,hitAt=-99;
 const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x)),ease=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x);};
 function bitmap(url){return fetch(url).then(r=>r.blob()).then(b=>createImageBitmap(b));}
 function keep(key,im){
  cache.set(key,im);decodedBytes+=im.width*im.height*4;
  while(cache.size>maxDecoded){const k=[...cache.keys()].find(k=>!pinned.has(k));if(!k)break;const v=cache.get(k);cache.delete(k);decodedBytes-=v.width*v.height*4;v.close?.();}
  peakBytes=Math.max(peakBytes,decodedBytes);return im;
 }
 function pump(){
  while(pending.size<3&&queue.length){
   const key=queue.shift();queued.delete(key);if(cache.has(key)||pending.has(key))continue;
   const p=bitmap(art.motion.frames[key].src).then(im=>keep(key,im)).catch(e=>{failed.push(key+':'+e.message);}).finally(()=>{pending.delete(key);pump();});pending.set(key,p);
  }
 }
 function request(key,urgent=false){
  if(!key||!art.motion.frames[key]||cache.has(key)||pending.has(key))return;
  if(queued.has(key)){if(urgent){queue.splice(queue.indexOf(key),1);queue.unshift(key);}return;}
  queued.add(key);urgent?queue.unshift(key):queue.push(key);pump();
 }
 function frame(key){let im=cache.get(key);if(im){cache.delete(key);cache.set(key,im);return im;}request(key);return null;}
 function warm(clip){
  if(wantedClip!==clip){wantedClip=clip;pinned.clear();pinned.add('idle_00');for(const k of art.motion.clips[clip]||[])pinned.add(k);
   for(let i=queue.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(!pinned.has(queue[i])){queued.delete(queue[i]);queue.splice(i,1);}}
  for(const k of art.motion.clips[clip]||[])request(k);
 }
 function getState(){return VENOM_V537.visualStateV538();}
 function quality(){return feedbackCfg().reducedMotion||perfMode>0?1:2;}
 function decorative(){return enabled&&feedbackCfg().flash!==0;}
 function stamp(id,x,y,w,h=w,a=0,alpha=1,blend='source-over'){
  const im=fxImages[id];if(!decorative()||!im||alpha<=0||w<=0||h<=0)return;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(a);ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(alpha);ctx.globalCompositeOperation=blend;ctx.drawImage(im,-w/2,-h/2,w,h);ctx.restore();
 }
 function animated(id,x,y,w,h,a,phase,alpha=1){const q=((phase%1)+1)%1*4,i=Math.floor(q),m=q-i;stamp(id+'_'+i,x,y,w,h,a,alpha*(1-m));stamp(id+'_'+((i+1)%4),x,y,w,h,a,alpha*m);}
 function glow(x,y,r,color,alpha){
  if(!decorative())return;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,color);g.addColorStop(.35,color+'77');g.addColorStop(1,color+'00');ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(x-r,y-r,r*2,r*2);ctx.restore();
 }
 function burst(x,y,key,n=9,force=55){
  if(!decorative())return;const colors={tentacle:'#b9ee70',infection:'#bbff61',spore:'#b5ef72',storm:'#88e686',breath:'#9aea71',acid:'#b7d94f'};
  bursts.push({x,y,key,at:visualTime,life:.42});if(bursts.length>10)bursts.shift();
  for(let i=0;i<n&&motes.length<72;i++){
   const a=(i/n)*TAU+serial*.71,v=force*(.35+(i*37%13)/13),life=.35+(i%4)*.12;
   motes.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v-12,age:0,life,r:2+(i%3),a,spin:(i%2?1:-1)*2.3,id:i%3?'drop':'shard',color:colors[key]||'#caff59'});
  }serial++;
 }
 function onTick(s,dt){
  visualTime=t;lastS=s;
  for(let i=motes.length-1;i>=0;i--){const p=motes[i];p.age+=dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.vy+=35*dt;p.vx*=Math.exp(-dt*1.6);p.a+=p.spin*dt;if(p.age>=p.life)motes.splice(i,1);}
  for(let i=bursts.length-1;i>=0;i--)if(visualTime-bursts[i].at>bursts[i].life)bursts.splice(i,1);
  if(!s.boss||!s.alive)return;
  const e=s.boss,a=s.attack;if(s.cycle.mode!==lastCycle){if(['retreat','cloudWindup','opening'].includes(s.cycle.mode)){carryFrame=lastSpriteKey||'idle_00';carryAt=t;}lastCycle=s.cycle.mode;}
  if(s.phase!==lastPhase){lastPhase=s.phase;phaseAt=visualTime;burst(e.x,e.y-30,'infection',16,80);}
  lastHp=e.hp; // Receiving normal player fire does not restart an unrelated whole-body pose.
  const infectionWalk=a?.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'&&a.time<s.infectionRhythm.move&&Math.abs(e.vx||0)>5;
  const nextFace=infectionWalk?(e.vx<0?-1:1):a?.locked?(Math.cos(a.a)<0?-1:1):player.x<e.x?-1:1;
  if(a?.locked)face=nextFace;
  if(nextFace!==face&&(!a||!a.locked)&&visualTime-turnAt>.5&&Math.abs(player.x-e.x)>26){face=nextFace;turnAt=visualTime;}
  if(a&&a!==lastAttack){lastAttack=a;lastStage='';}
  if(a&&a.stage!==lastStage){
   if(a.stage==='active'&&a.key!=='dart'){const b=a.key==='breath'?s.breath:null;if(!b?.blocked)burst(b?b.x:e.x+Math.cos(a.a)*(e.r+10),b?b.y:e.y+Math.sin(a.a)*(e.r+10),a.key,a.key==='gland'?16:6,a.key==='gland'?90:40);}
   if(a.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'){carryFrame=lastSpriteKey||'infection_03';carryAt=t;}
   lastStage=a.stage;
  }
  if(a?.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'){
   const step=a.infectionShots>1?'second':a.time<s.infectionRhythm.move?'move':'recast';
   if(step!==lastInfectionStep){carryFrame=lastSpriteKey||'infection_03';carryAt=t;if(step==='second'&&s.infection)burst(s.infection.x,s.infection.y,'infection',10,60);lastInfectionStep=step;}
  }else lastInfectionStep='';
  if(s.shots.length>lastShots){const shot=s.shots[s.shots.length-1];burst(shot.originX,shot.originY,shot.kind,shot.kind==='gland'?10:3,shot.kind==='gland'?65:22);}lastShots=s.shots.length;
  if(a&&a.stage==='active'&&['breath','infection','storm'].includes(a.key)&&visualTime>=emitAt&&decorative()){
   emitAt=visualTime+.20;const r=e.r+18,aa=a.a+(Math.sin(visualTime*8)*.21),b=a.key==='breath'?s.breath:null;if(!b?.blocked)burst(b?b.x+Math.cos(a.a)*6:e.x+Math.cos(aa)*r,b?b.y+Math.sin(a.a)*6:e.y+Math.sin(aa)*r,a.key,2,32);
  }
  const p=sample(s);warm(p.clip);request(p.key,true);request(p.next,true);
 }
 // Pose artwork stays rigid. Only the upper sensory tendrils receive subpixel secondary motion.
 const motionSurface=(()=>{
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=512;const gl=c.getContext('webgl',{alpha:true,premultipliedAlpha:true,preserveDrawingBuffer:true,antialias:false});
  if(!gl)return {draw:()=>false,info:()=>({enabled:false,bodyWarp:false})};
  const shader=(type,src)=>{const s=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(s,src);gl.compileShader(s);if(!gl.getShaderParameter(s,gl.COMPILE_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getShaderInfoLog(s));return s;};
  const p=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(p,shader(gl.VERTEX_SHADER,'attribute vec2 a;varying vec2 uv;void main(){uv=vec2((a.x+1.)*.5,(1.-a.y)*.5);gl_Position=vec4(a,0.,1.);}'));
  gl.attachShader(p,shader(gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,'precision mediump float;varying vec2 uv;uniform sampler2D A;uniform sampler2D B;uniform float q;uniform float tm;void main(){float crown=1.-smoothstep(.36,.65,uv.y);vec2 d=vec2(sin(tm*1.4+uv.y*18.)*.0014,sin(tm*1.1+uv.x*13.)*.0006)*crown;vec4 a=texture2D(A,uv+d);vec4 b=texture2D(B,uv+d);float al=mix(a.a,b.a,q);vec3 rgb=mix(a.rgb*a.a,b.rgb*b.a,q);float g=step(rgb.r*1.2,rgb.g)*step(.15,rgb.g);rgb*=1.+g*.018*sin(tm*1.25);gl_FragColor=vec4(rgb,al);}'));
  gl.linkProgram(p);if(!gl.getProgramParameter(p,gl.LINK_STATUS))return {draw:()=>false,info:()=>({enabled:false,bodyWarp:false})};gl.useProgram(p);
  const buffer=gl.createBuffer();gl.bindBuffer(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,buffer);gl.bufferData(gl.ARRAY_BUFFER,new Float32Array([-1,-1,1,-1,-1,1,1,1]),gl.STATIC_DRAW);const a=gl.getAttribLocation(p,'a');gl.enableVertexAttribArray(a);gl.vertexAttribPointer(a,2,gl.FLOAT,false,0,0);
  const stage=document.createElement('canvas');stage.width=stage.height=512;const g=stage.getContext('2d'),textures=new Map();let renders=0;
  function tex(key,im){if(textures.has(key)){const t=textures.get(key);textures.delete(key);textures.set(key,t);return t;}const f=art.motion.frames[key],E=art.flow.extent;g.clearRect(0,0,512,512);g.drawImage(im,(-f.ax-art.flow.ox)*512/E,(-f.ay-art.flow.oy)*512/E,f.w*512/E,f.h*512/E);const t=gl.createTexture();gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,t);for(const [k,v] of [[gl.TEXTURE_MIN_FILTER,gl.LINEAR],[gl.TEXTURE_MAG_FILTER,gl.LINEAR],[gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_S,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE],[gl.TEXTURE_WRAP_T,gl.CLAMP_TO_EDGE]])gl.texParameteri(gl.TEXTURE_2D,k,v);gl.texImage2D(gl.TEXTURE_2D,0,gl.RGBA,gl.RGBA,gl.UNSIGNED_BYTE,stage);textures.set(key,t);while(textures.size>4){const k=textures.keys().next().value;gl.deleteTexture(textures.get(k));textures.delete(k);}return t;}
  return {draw(pick){if(quality()<2||gl.isContextLost())return false;const A=frame(pick.key),B=frame(pick.next);if(!A||!B)return false;const ts=[tex(pick.key,A),tex(pick.next,B)];gl.useProgram(p);for(let i=0;i<2;i++){gl.activeTexture(gl.TEXTURE0+i);gl.bindTexture(gl.TEXTURE_2D,ts[i]);gl.uniform1i(gl.getUniformLocation(p,['A','B'][i]),i);}gl.uniform1f(gl.getUniformLocation(p,'q'),pick.mix||0);gl.uniform1f(gl.getUniformLocation(p,'tm'),getState().clock);gl.viewport(0,0,512,512);gl.drawArrays(gl.TRIANGLE_STRIP,0,4);ctx.drawImage(c,art.flow.ox,art.flow.oy,art.flow.extent,art.flow.extent);renders++;return true;},info:()=>({enabled:!gl.isContextLost(),renders,gpuImageSlots:textures.size,gpuFlowSlots:0,bodyWarp:false,policy:'stable-pose-holds'})};
 })();
 const posePlan={
  dart:{windup:[[0,'idle_00'],[.18,'dart_00'],[.56,'dart_01'],[.91,'dart_02']],active:[[0,'dart_02']],recovery:[[0,'dart_02'],[.18,'dart_03'],[.76,'idle_00']]},
  tentacle:{windup:[[0,'melee_00'],[.3,'melee_01'],[.62,'melee_03'],[.92,'melee_04']],active:[[0,'melee_04'],[.22,'melee_05']],recovery:[[0,'melee_06'],[.40,'melee_07'],[.82,'idle_00']]},
  breath:{windup:[[0,'breath_00'],[.25,'breath_01'],[.52,'breath_02'],[.84,'breath_03']],active:[[0,'breath_04']],recovery:[[0,'breath_05'],[.30,'breath_06'],[.82,'idle_00']]},
  spore:{windup:[[0,'spore_00'],[.42,'spore_01'],[.92,'spore_02']],active:[[0,'spore_02']],recovery:[[0,'spore_03'],[.76,'idle_00']]},
  gland:{windup:[[0,'gland_00'],[.5,'gland_01'],[.93,'gland_02']],active:[[0,'gland_02'],[.32,'gland_01'],[.60,'gland_02']],recovery:[[0,'gland_03'],[.78,'idle_00']]},
  storm:{windup:[[0,'storm_00'],[.5,'storm_01'],[.92,'storm_02']],active:[[0,'storm_02']],recovery:[[0,'storm_03'],[.78,'idle_00']]},
  infection:{windup:[[0,'infection_00'],[.32,'infection_01'],[.63,'infection_02'],[.90,'infection_03']],active:[[0,'infection_04']],recovery:[[0,'infection_03'],[.60,'infection_00'],[.86,'idle_00']]}
 };
 function sample(s){
  const a=s.attack;let clip='idle',key='idle_00',next=key,mix=0;
  const walkingInfection=a?.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'&&Math.hypot(s.boss?.vx||0,s.boss?.vy||0)>5;
  if(walkingInfection){clip='move';const seq=['move_04','move_05','move_06','move_07'],q=(s.clock%1.8)/1.8*4,i=Math.floor(q);key=seq[i%4];next=seq[(i+1)%4];mix=ease(clamp((q-i-.68)/.32));}
  else if(a){const recast=a.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'&&a.infectionShots===1&&a.time>=s.infectionRhythm.move,stage=recast?'windup':a.stage,k=a.key==='acid'?'breath':a.key,sp=s.specs[a.key],duration=recast?s.infectionRhythm.recastWarn:stage==='windup'?sp.prepare:stage==='active'?sp.active:sp.recover,seq=a.key==='gland'&&stage==='active'?[[0,'gland_02'],[Math.max(.10,((a.glandGap??.38)*.45-.05)/sp.active),'gland_01'],[Math.max(.20,((a.glandGap??.38)-.10)/sp.active),'gland_02']]:posePlan[k][stage],u=clamp((recast?a.time-s.infectionRhythm.move:a.time)/duration);clip=k==='tentacle'?'melee':k;
   let i=0;while(i+1<seq.length&&u>=seq[i+1][0])i++;next=seq[i][1];key=i?seq[i-1][1]:next;mix=i?ease((u-seq[i][0])*duration/.10):0;if(mix>=1){key=next;mix=0;}
  }else if(s.shroud&&['cloudWindup','cloud'].includes(s.cycle.mode)){
   clip='gland';const c=s.shroud,u=clamp(c.age/c.warn),seq=posePlan.gland.windup;
   if(c.age<c.warn){let i=0;while(i+1<seq.length&&u>=seq[i+1][0])i++;next=seq[i][1];key=i?seq[i-1][1]:next;mix=i?ease((u-seq[i][0])*c.warn/.13):0;if(mix>=1){key=next;mix=0;}}
   else {clip='storm';key='storm_02';next=key;mix=0;}
  }else if(s.cycle.mode==='opening'){clip='gland';key='gland_03';next='idle_00';mix=ease(clamp((s.cycle.elapsed-.20)/Math.max(.1,s.cycle.duration-.20)));
  }else if(Math.hypot(s.boss?.vx||0,s.boss?.vy||0)>5){clip='move';const seq=['move_04','move_05','move_06','move_07'],q=(s.clock%(s.kiteMode?1.15:1.8))/(s.kiteMode?1.15:1.8)*4,i=Math.floor(q);key=seq[i%4];next=seq[(i+1)%4];mix=ease(clamp((q-i-.75)/.25));}
  if(carryFrame&&t-carryAt>=0&&t-carryAt<.18&&(!a||walkingInfection||(a.key==='infection'&&a.stage==='active'))){next=key;key=carryFrame;mix=ease((t-carryAt)/.18);}
  return {key,next,mix,clip,index:Math.max(0,(art.motion.clips[clip]||[]).indexOf(key))};
 }

 function drawBody(e,size,alpha=1,shadowOnly=false,death=null){
  e.venomDrawSizeV544=size;
  const raw=getState();if(!e?.venomV537&&!death)return false;
  if(!death&&(e.dead||!(e.hp>0)))return false;
  // Arrival already has an engine entity, before the attack bridge attaches.
  // Render the same HD motion throughout the entrance instead of the legacy 285px sprite.
  const s=raw.boss===e||death?raw:{...raw,boss:e,clock:t,attack:null,alive:true};
  let picked;
  if(death){const seq=art.motion.clips.death,u=clamp((t-death.at)/1.65);picked={index:Math.min(seq.length-1,Math.floor(u*seq.length)),key:seq[Math.min(seq.length-1,Math.floor(u*seq.length))],next:seq[Math.min(seq.length-1,Math.floor(u*seq.length)+1)],clip:'death'};}
  else picked=sample(s);
  selectedFrame=picked.key;request(picked.key,true);request(picked.next,true);
  let shown=picked.key,im=frame(shown);
  if(!im){const seq=art.motion.clips[picked.clip];shown=seq?.filter(k=>cache.has(k)).sort((a,b)=>Math.abs(seq.indexOf(a)-picked.index)-Math.abs(seq.indexOf(b)-picked.index))[0]||'idle_00';im=frame(shown);if(!shadowOnly)fallbackDraws++;}
  if(!im)return false;
  lastSpriteKey=shown;if(!shadowOnly)draws++;
  const f=art.motion.frames[shown];
  const sc=size/art.motion.reference,ground=e.y+size*.40,dir=death?.face||face;
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(e.x,ground);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;ctx.imageSmoothingQuality='high';ctx.shadowBlur=0;
  if(shadowOnly){
   // Projected silhouette is generated from the currently displayed motion frame.
   const key=shown;let sh=shadows.get(key);
   if(!sh){sh=document.createElement('canvas');sh.width=200;sh.height=Math.max(1,Math.round(200*f.h/f.w));const g=sh.getContext('2d');g.drawImage(im,0,0,sh.width,sh.height);g.globalCompositeOperation='source-in';g.fillStyle='#030609';g.fillRect(0,0,sh.width,sh.height);shadows.set(key,sh);if(shadows.size>22)shadows.delete(shadows.keys().next().value);}
   ctx.globalAlpha=.29*alpha;ctx.transform(1,0,-.24,-.14,0,0);ctx.scale(dir*sc,sc);ctx.drawImage(sh,-f.ax,-f.ay,f.w,f.h);
  }else{
   // Frame artwork itself provides all articulation. Fixed body scale and foot
   // anchor avoid the slide/tilt/whole-sprite pumping used by the old version.
   ctx.scale(dir*sc,sc);
   if(!death&&shown===picked.key&&motionSurface.draw(picked)){}else if(!death&&shown===picked.key&&picked.next!==picked.key&&picked.mix>0){const nextIm=frame(picked.next),nf=art.motion.frames[picked.next];if(nextIm){ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*(1-picked.mix);ctx.drawImage(im,-f.ax,-f.ay,f.w,f.h);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*picked.mix;ctx.drawImage(nextIm,-nf.ax,-nf.ay,nf.w,nf.h);ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;}else ctx.drawImage(im,-f.ax,-f.ay,f.w,f.h);}else ctx.drawImage(im,-f.ax,-f.ay,f.w,f.h);
  }
  ctx.restore();return true;
 }
 const sectorCache=new Map();
 function warningReach(x,y,a,len,r){if(!terrainHitV418(x,y,x+Math.cos(a)*len,y+Math.sin(a)*len,r))return len;let lo=0,hi=len;for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const m=(lo+hi)/2;if(terrainHitV418(x,y,x+Math.cos(a)*m,y+Math.sin(a)*m,r))hi=m;else lo=m;}return lo;}
 function sectorPointsV582(x,y,r,a,half){
  const key=[x.toFixed(1),y.toFixed(1),r.toFixed(1),a.toFixed(3),half.toFixed(3)].join('|');let pts=sectorCache.get(key);
  if(!pts){pts=[];for(let i=0;i<=28;i++){const aa=a-half+2*half*i/28,c=Math.cos(aa),s=Math.sin(aa);let rr=r;if(terrainHitV418(x,y,x+c*r,y+s*r,1)){let lo=0,hi=r;for(let j=0;j<8;j++){const m=(lo+hi)/2;if(terrainHitV418(x,y,x+c*m,y+s*m,1))hi=m;else lo=m;}rr=lo;}pts.push([x+c*rr,y+s*rr]);}sectorCache.set(key,pts);if(sectorCache.size>12)sectorCache.delete(sectorCache.keys().next().value);}
  return pts;
 }
 function warningSectorPathV582(x,y,r,a,half){
  const pts=sectorPointsV582(x,y,r,a,half),p=new Path2D();
  if(half<Math.PI-.001)p.moveTo(x,y);else p.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);
  for(const v of pts)p.lineTo(v[0],v[1]);p.closePath();return p;
 }
 function pathSector(x,y,r,a,half){
  const pts=sectorPointsV582(x,y,r,a,half);ctx.beginPath();
  if(half<Math.PI-.001)ctx.moveTo(x,y);else ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);
  for(const p of pts)ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]);ctx.closePath();
 }

 function drawWarningOverlay(){
  const s=getState();if(!s.alive)return;
  drawVenomAttackWarningV582(s,warningSectorPathV582,warningReach);
 }

 function softCloud(x,y,rx,ry,a,alpha,seed=0){
  if(!decorative())return;
  // Each cloud is assembled from transparent lobes. No hard geometric clipping plane.
  stamp('storm',x,y,rx*1.9,ry*1.8,a,alpha*.66);
  const n=quality()>1?7:4,c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const u=(i+.5)/n*2-1,px=u*rx*.70,py=Math.sin(seed+i*2.7+visualTime*.55)*ry*.22,r=(.52+.14*Math.sin(i*1.7+seed));stamp('wisp',x+px*c-py*s,y+px*s+py*c,rx*r,ry*(1.0+r*.3),a+Math.sin(i+visualTime*.4)*.16,alpha*.42);}
 }
 function puddle(x,y,rx,ry,a,alpha,seed=0){
  ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(a);const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,Math.min(rx,ry)*.12,0,0,Math.max(rx,ry));g.addColorStop(0,'#a9df45c9');g.addColorStop(.58,'#52982d66');g.addColorStop(1,'#294b1c00');ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle=g;
  ctx.beginPath();const n=26;for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){const aa=i/n*TAU,rr=.90+.06*Math.sin(i*1.7+seed)+.035*Math.sin(i*3.1+seed*.5),px=Math.cos(aa)*rx*rr,py=Math.sin(aa)*ry*rr;i?ctx.lineTo(px,py):ctx.moveTo(px,py);}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
  if(decorative()){stamp('acid',x,y,rx*1.98,ry*1.90,a,alpha*.67);const n=quality()>1?5:2;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const aa=i*2.4+seed,rr=(.25+(i%3)*.19),px=Math.cos(aa)*rx*rr,py=Math.sin(aa)*ry*rr,c=Math.cos(a),s=Math.sin(a);stamp('drop',x+px*c-py*s,y+px*s+py*c,6+2*Math.sin(visualTime*1.4+i),7+2*Math.sin(visualTime*1.4+i),aa,alpha*.70);}}
 }
 function zone(z,s){
  const warn=z.warn>0,fade=clamp(z.life/.5),q=1-clamp((z.warn||0)/(z.kind==='leak'?.22:1.3)),tm=s.clock,col=s.colors[z.kind];
  if(warn&&z.kind==='leak'){const u=.45+.55*q;puddle(z.x,z.y,(z.rx||z.r)*u,(z.ry||z.r*.83)*u,z.a||0,.25+.4*q,z.seed||0);return;}
  if(warn)return;
  if(z.kind==='spore'){
   if(decorative()){softCloud(z.x,z.y,z.r,z.r*.85,0,.33*fade,2);stamp('sporeRing',z.x,z.y,z.r*1.94,z.r*1.8,Math.sin(tm*.35)*.03,.48*fade);stamp('spore',z.x,z.y,30,30,0,.98*fade);}else{ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.globalAlpha=.28;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(z.x,z.y,z.r,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(z.x,z.y,6,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
  }else if(z.kind==='leak')puddle(z.x,z.y,z.rx||z.r,z.ry||z.r*.83,z.a||0,.74*fade,z.seed||0);
  else if(z.kind==='acid'){
   const n=Math.max(3,Math.ceil(z.len/(z.w*.72))),c=Math.cos(z.a),si=Math.sin(z.a);for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const u=(i+.5)/n-.5,bend=Math.sin(i*1.7)*z.w*.11,taper=.60+.40*Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,1-u*u*4)),x=z.x+c*u*Math.max(0,z.len-z.w*.7)-si*bend,y=z.y+si*u*Math.max(0,z.len-z.w*.7)+c*bend;puddle(x,y,z.w*(.63+.10*Math.sin(i*2.1))*taper,z.w*(.43+.12*Math.sin(i*1.4+1))*taper,z.a,.70*fade,i*.65);}
  }else if(z.kind==='storm'){
   if(decorative())softCloud(z.x,z.y,z.len/2,z.w/2,z.a,.85*fade,4);else{ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle=col;ctx.globalAlpha=.6*fade;ctx.setLineDash([3,6]);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(z.x,z.y,z.len/2,z.w/2,z.a,0,TAU);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();}
  }
 }
 function ribbon(s){
  const a=s.attack,e=s.boss;if(!a||a.stage!=='active')return;const aa=a.a,k=a.key,g=s.geometry;
  if(k==='breath'){
   const b=s.breath;if(!b||b.blocked)return;
   const len=warningReach(b.x,b.y,aa,b.len,4),n=quality()>1?13:7;
   if(len<2)return;
   for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const u=(i+.5)/n,rr=u*len,rx=7+u*len*.13,ry=4+rr*Math.tan(b.half)*.84,x=b.x+Math.cos(aa)*rr,y=b.y+Math.sin(aa)*rr;if(terrainHitV418(b.x,b.y,x,y,2))continue;
    const phase=(s.clock*.45+i*.171)%1,px=x-Math.sin(aa)*Math.sin(phase*TAU)*u*8,py=y+Math.cos(aa)*Math.sin(phase*TAU)*u*8;
    if(decorative())stamp('wisp',px,py,rx*2.2,ry*1.9,aa,.42*(1-u*.42));else{ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#92e761';ctx.globalAlpha=.10;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(px,py,rx,ry,aa,0,TAU);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();}
   }
   // Keep the narrow throat of the animated breath at the open jaws.
   if(decorative())animated('fx_breath',b.x+Math.cos(aa)*len*.53,b.y+Math.sin(aa)*len*.53,len*1.02,len*.91,aa,s.clock*.7,.68);
  }else if(k==='tentacle'){
   const half=42.5*Math.PI/180,r=e.r+g.tentacle,swing=aa-half+clamp(a.time/s.specs.tentacle.active)*half*2,root={x:e.x,y:e.y},end={x:e.x+Math.cos(swing)*r,y:e.y+Math.sin(swing)*r};
   ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(root.x,root.y);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(e.x+Math.cos(swing-.25)*r*.55,e.y+Math.sin(swing-.25)*r*.55,end.x,end.y);ctx.strokeStyle='#223e19';ctx.lineWidth=13;ctx.stroke();ctx.strokeStyle='#b7e866';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();if(decorative())animated('fx_slash',e.x+Math.cos(aa)*r*.55,e.y+Math.sin(aa)*r*.55,r*1.1,r*1.15,aa,clamp(a.time/.12)*.74,.9);
  }else if(k==='acid'){
   for(const ray of a.acidRays||[]){const c=Math.cos(ray.a),si=Math.sin(ray.a);for(let i=0;i<12;i++){const u=(i+.5)/12,r=u*ray.len,bend=Math.sin(i*1.7)*ray.w*.10,taper=.68+.32*Math.sin(u*Math.PI);puddle(ray.x+c*r-si*bend,ray.y+si*r+c*bend,ray.w*.70*taper,ray.w*(.40+.10*Math.sin(i*1.3))*taper,ray.a,.64,i*.7);}}
  }else if(k==='gland'){
   if(decorative()){const second=a.time>=.38,u=clamp((a.time-(second?.38:0))/.30);animated('fx_burst',e.x,e.y,90+u*170,90+u*170,second?Math.PI/22:0,u*.74,.9*(1-u*.5));}
  }
 }
 function infection(s){
  const list=s.infections||[],g=s.geometry,speed=g.infectionSpeed,n=quality()>1?(list.length>1?5:8):3;
  for(const z of list)for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const elapsed=z.time-i*s.infectionRhythm.frontGap;if(elapsed<0||elapsed>g.infection/speed+1.5)continue;const front=z.r+Math.min(g.infection,elapsed*speed),back=z.r+Math.max(0,(elapsed-1.5)*speed),mid=(front+back)/2,aa=z.a+i*TAU/3;
   for(let j=0;j<n;j++){const angle=aa-g.infectionHalf*.94+g.infectionHalf*1.88*j/(n-1),rr=mid+Math.sin(j*2.3+s.clock*.65)*6,x=z.x+Math.cos(angle)*rr,y=z.y+Math.sin(angle)*rr;if(terrainHitV418(z.x,z.y,x,y,2))continue;
    const w=30+mid*.17,h=Math.max(25,front-back+18),fade=clamp((g.infection/speed+1.5-elapsed)/.6);
    if(decorative()){stamp('wisp',x,y,w*1.5,h*1.18,angle-Math.PI/2,.55*fade);animated('fx_infection',x,y,w,h,angle-Math.PI/2,s.clock*.55+j*.16,.7*fade);}else puddle(x,y,w*.5,h*.45,angle-Math.PI/2,.5*fade,j);
   }
  }
 }

 // A continuous supply fills the space between outgoing fronts for 3 seconds.
 function ventCloud(s){
  const list=s.clouds||[],viewZ=typeof venomPointerZoomV540==='function'?venomPointerZoomV540():1,left=cameraLeft(),top=cameraTop(),right=left+W/viewZ,bottom=top+H/viewZ;let budget=48;
  for(const z of list){const windup=z.age<z.warn,col='#a4ed62';
   if(windup){softCloud(z.x,z.y,z.startR,z.startR*.7,0,.4,z.seed);continue;}
   const r=z.r,inner=z.innerR||0,fade=z.alpha;if(!(r>0)||fade<=0)continue;
   ctx.save();const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(z.x,z.y,0,z.x,z.y,r),lo=clamp(inner/r);
   g.addColorStop(0,inner>0?'#8fe55b00':'#7fc94f70');if(inner>0)g.addColorStop(Math.min(.96,lo),'#8fe55b00');g.addColorStop(lo+(1-lo)*.32,'#8ed85b4d');g.addColorStop(lo+(1-lo)*.78,'#a3e95d42');g.addColorStop(1,'#89d65b00');ctx.globalAlpha=fade;ctx.fillStyle=g;pathSector(z.x,z.y,r,0,Math.PI);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
   const count=Math.min(budget,z===s.shroud?32:16);budget-=count;
   for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
    const aa=i*2.399963+z.seed,wave=(s.clock*.27+i*.173)%1,radial=inner+(r-inner)*(i<12&&z.emitting?.10+wave*.62:.38+.58*((i*.618)%1)),reach=warningReach(z.x,z.y,aa,r,2);
    if(radial>reach+5)continue;const x=z.x+Math.cos(aa)*radial,y=z.y+Math.sin(aa)*radial;
    if(x<left-160||x>right+160||y<top-160||y>bottom+160)continue;
    const size=Math.max(28,Math.min(108,(r-inner)*.24+radial*.11)),alpha=fade*(z.emitting?.44:.34);
    stamp('wisp',x,y,size*2.1,size*1.6,aa+Math.sin(s.clock*.4+i)*.12,alpha);if(i%3===0)stamp('storm',x,y,size*1.8,size*1.4,aa,.20*fade);
   }
   if(z.emitting){
    // New vapor remains visibly attached to the body throughout the channel.
    for(let i=0;i<6;i++){const a=i*TAU/6+z.seed,q=(s.clock*1.1+i*.19)%1,rr=z.startR*(.55+q*1.1);stamp('wisp',z.x+Math.cos(a)*rr,z.y+Math.sin(a)*rr,42+q*42,32+q*36,a,.48*(1-q*.65));}
   }
  }
 }
 function retreatSpill(s){
  const e=s.boss,v=Math.hypot(e.vx||0,e.vy||0);if(!s.kiteMode||v<12)return;
  const ux=e.vx/v,uy=e.vy/v;
  // Ground mist seeps from the lower body; it uses the existing storm artwork.
  for(let i=0;i<2;i++){const x=e.x-ux*(e.r*.36+i*20),y=e.y-uy*(e.r*.36+i*20)+9;softCloud(x,y,30+i*12,19+i*7,Math.atan2(uy,ux),.37-i*.08,i);}
 }
 function spitFrame(i,x,y,scale,alpha){
  const c=spitAsset.cells[i];ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.drawImage(spitAtlas,0,c.y,spitAsset.width,c.h,x-(spitAsset.tipX-70)*scale,y-c.tipY*scale,spitAsset.width*scale,c.h*scale);
 }

 function fogTrail(s){
  for(const z of s.zones){if(z.kind!=='leak')continue;
   const rx=z.rx||z.r,ry=z.ry||z.r*.72,fade=clamp(z.life/.45),grow=z.warn>0?.72+.28*clamp(1-z.warn/.22):1;
   // Only appearance changed: the old ellipse, .22s warning, 2.9s life and damage are retained.
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(z.x,z.y);ctx.rotate(z.a||0);ctx.scale(rx,ry);const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,0,.1,0,0,1);g.addColorStop(0,'#a3eb6570');g.addColorStop(.65,'#7fcb553b');g.addColorStop(1,'#75be5000');ctx.globalAlpha=fade*(z.warn>0?.35:.65);ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(-1,-1,2,2);ctx.restore();
   softCloud(z.x,z.y,rx*grow,ry*grow,z.a||0,.76*fade*(z.warn>0?.55:1),z.seed||0);
  }
 }
 function drawGround(){const s=getState();if(!s.alive)return;visualTime=t;fogTrail(s);for(const z of s.zones)if(z.kind!=='leak')zone(z,s);infection(s);ventCloud(s);}
 function drawOver(e){
  const s=getState();if(!s.alive||e!==s.boss)return;ribbon(s);retreatSpill(s);const a=s.attack;
  if(decorative()&&a?.stage==='active'&&(a.key==='breath'||a.key==='acid')){
   const jets=a.key==='acid'?(a.acidRays||[]):s.breath&&!s.breath.blocked?[s.breath]:[];
    for(const jet of jets)for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const q=(s.clock*1.4+i/4)%1,reach=q*Math.min(90,jet.len??90),x=jet.x+Math.cos(jet.a)*reach,y=jet.y+Math.sin(jet.a)*reach;if(a.key==='breath'&&terrainHitV418(jet.x,jet.y,x,y,2))continue;stamp(a.key==='acid'?'drop':'wisp',x,y,16+q*27,12+q*22,jet.a,.42*(1-q*.55));}
  }
   if(decorative()&&a?.key==='dart'&&a.stage==='active'&&a.shots){const since=a.time-(a.shots-1)*.34;if(since<.16)for(const p of a.bodyOrigins||[]){if(p.blocked)continue;const len=12+since*170;stamp('drop',p.x+Math.cos(a.a)*len*.5,p.y+Math.sin(a.a)*len*.5,len+12,9+since*10,a.a,.78*(1-since/.16));}}
  for(const z of s.zones){if(z.kind!=='spore'||!(z.warn>0)||!decorative())continue;const elapsed=s.clock-(z.launchAt??s.clock);if(elapsed<0)continue;const q=clamp(elapsed/.70),x=e.x-face*18+(z.x-e.x+face*18)*q,y=e.y-80+(z.y-e.y+80)*q-Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*42;stamp('spore',x,y,15+q*8,15+q*8,0,.85);}
  for(const b of s.shots){
   const aa=Math.atan2(b.vy,b.vx),age=Math.max(0,s.clock-b.born),alpha=b.fading!==undefined?clamp(b.fading/.30):1,scale=(b.kind==='dart'?144:124)/spitAsset.width;
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(b.x,b.y);ctx.rotate(aa);
   if(spitAtlas){const p=(age*8+(b.seed||0)*.137)%4,i=Math.floor(p),m=ease(p-i);spitFrame(i,0,0,scale,alpha*(1-m));spitFrame((i+1)%4,0,0,scale,alpha*m);}
   else {ctx.globalAlpha=alpha;ctx.fillStyle='#b8ec55';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(10,0);ctx.lineTo(-24,-3);ctx.lineTo(-18,0);ctx.lineTo(-57,2);ctx.lineTo(-21,4);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
   ctx.restore();
  }

  if(decorative()){for(const p of motes){const q=1-p.age/p.life;stamp(p.id,p.x,p.y,p.r*3*q+2,p.r*4*q+2,p.a,q*.65);}for(const p of bursts){const q=clamp((t-p.at)/p.life),r=15+q*38;stamp(p.key==='infection'?'roots':p.key==='spore'?'sporeRing':'impact',p.x,p.y,r,r,p.at*4,(1-q)*.48);}}
 }

 function died(e){if(!e?.venomV537)return;deaths.push({e:{...e},at:t,face});if(deaths.length>2)deaths.shift();warm('death');burst(e.x,e.y,'spore',20,86);}
 function drawDeath(){
  for(let i=deaths.length-1;i>=0;i--){const d=deaths[i],elapsed=t-d.at;if(elapsed<0||elapsed>1.85){deaths.splice(i,1);continue;}drawBody(d.e,208,clamp((1.85-elapsed)/.55),false,d);}
 }

// V554: stage-fixed VENOM encounters; health loss increases full reinforcement waves.
function createVenomReinforcementsV554(host){
 const profiles={2:{interval:3,melee:96,ranged:0,baseMelee:6,baseRanged:0},3:{interval:3,melee:48,ranged:48,baseMelee:3,baseRanged:3}};
 let owner=null,phase=0,tier=0,clock=0,nextWave=0,pending=[],waveCount=0,spawnCount=0,cancelled=0,deferred=0,history=[],sprites=null;
 function clear(){if(owner||pending.length)host.clear();owner=null;phase=0;tier=0;clock=0;nextWave=0;pending=[];waveCount=spawnCount=cancelled=deferred=0;history=[];}
 function usable(p,minimum,sites=pending,living=host.living()){
  const a=host.arena(),b=host.boss(),r=p.kind==='spitter'?8:6.8;if(!a||!b)return false;
  const pad=(a.half||12)+r+16;
  if(p.x<a.l+pad||p.x>a.r-pad||p.y<a.t+pad||p.y>a.b-pad)return false;
  if(Math.hypot(p.x-host.player.x,p.y-host.player.y)<minimum||Math.hypot(p.x-b.x,p.y-b.y)<b.r+r+28)return false;
  if(living.some(e=>!e.dead&&Math.hypot(p.x-e.x,p.y-e.y)<26))return false;
  if(sites.some(q=>q!==p&&Math.hypot(p.x-q.x,p.y-q.y)<28))return false;
  return host.valid(p.x,p.y,r);
 }
 function sites(){
  const a=host.arena(),points=[];if(!a)return points;const side=Math.min(a.r-a.l,a.b-a.t),offset=Math.random(),insets=[24];
  for(const inset of insets){const pad=(a.half||12)+inset,sx=a.r-a.l-pad*2,sy=a.b-a.t-pad*2;
   for(let edge=0;edge<4;edge++)for(let n=0;n<20;n++){const u=.025+.95*((n+.35+offset*.3)/20);points.push({x:edge===1?a.r-pad:edge===3?a.l+pad:a.l+pad+u*sx,y:edge===0?a.t+pad:edge===2?a.b-pad:a.t+pad+u*sy});}
  }
  for(let i=points.length-1;i>0;i--){const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1));[points[i],points[j]]=[points[j],points[i]];}return points;
 }
 function wave(profile){
  const multiplier=2**tier,living=host.living().filter(e=>!e.dead),melee=profile.baseMelee*multiplier,ranged=profile.baseRanged*multiplier;
  const count=kind=>living.filter(e=>e.type===kind).length+pending.filter(e=>e.kind===kind).length;
  if(count('crawler')+melee>profile.melee||count('spitter')+ranged>profile.ranged){deferred++;return;}
  const a=host.arena(),minimum=Math.min(210,Math.min(a.r-a.l,a.b-a.t)*.25),points=sites(),selected=[],kinds=[];
  for(let n=0;n<Math.max(melee,ranged);n++){if(n<melee)kinds.push('crawler');if(n<ranged)kinds.push('spitter');}
  for(const kind of kinds){let chosen=null;for(let i=0;i<points.length;i++){const p={...points[i],kind,at:clock+.9,waveId:waveCount+1};if(usable(p,minimum,selected,living)){chosen=p;points.splice(i,1);break;}}if(!chosen){deferred++;return;}selected.push(chosen);}
  waveCount++;history.push({id:waveCount,at:clock,releasesAt:clock+.9,tier,multiplier,melee,ranged,planned:selected.length,born:0,cancelled:0});if(history.length>8)history.shift();pending.push(...selected);
 }
 function tick(dt){
  const b=host.boss();if(!b||b.dead||b.hp<=0||!b.venomV537||!host.enabled()){if(owner||pending.length)clear();return;}
  if(b!==owner){clear();owner=b;}
  if(!host.ready())return;
  clock+=Math.max(0,dt);const p=host.phase(),profile=profiles[p];
  if(p!==phase){phase=p;tier=0;pending=[];nextWave=clock+2.1;}
  const ratio=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,b.hp/Math.max(1,b.maxHp)));tier=Math.max(tier,Math.min(3,Math.floor((1-ratio+1e-9)/.3)));
  if(!profile)return;
  let born=0;const living=host.living();
  for(let i=pending.length-1;i>=0;i--){const q=pending[i];if(clock+1e-8<q.at)continue;const record=history.find(w=>w.id===q.waveId);
   if(usable(q,110,pending,living)){const e=host.spawn(q.kind,q.x,q.y,owner);if(e&&!e.dead){spawnCount++;born++;if(record)record.born++;}else{cancelled++;if(record)record.cancelled++;}}
   else{cancelled++;if(record)record.cancelled++;}pending.splice(i,1);
  }
  if(born&&host.afterSpawn)host.afterSpawn();
  if(clock+1e-8<nextWave)return;
  nextWave=clock+profile.interval;wave(profile);
 }
 function warningSprite(kind){
  if(!sprites)sprites=new Map();if(sprites.has(kind))return sprites.get(kind);
  const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=64;const g=c.getContext('2d'),glow=g.createRadialGradient(32,32,2,32,32,30);
  glow.addColorStop(0,'rgba(167,240,89,.34)');glow.addColorStop(1,'rgba(72,132,45,0)');g.fillStyle=glow;g.fillRect(0,0,64,64);g.strokeStyle=kind==='spitter'?'#c9e77f':'#91c975';g.lineWidth=1.8;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(32,32,20,13,0,0,Math.PI*2);g.stroke();sprites.set(kind,c);return c;
 }
 function draw(ctx){if(!host.ready())return;for(const q of pending){const age=1-Math.max(0,q.at-clock)/.9,r=13+age*6;ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.35+age*.55;ctx.drawImage(warningSprite(q.kind),q.x-r,q.y-r,r*2,r*2);ctx.restore();}}
 function info(){const living=host.living().filter(e=>!e.dead),profile=profiles[phase],multiplier=2**tier;return {version:554,phase,tier,multiplier,hpPercent:owner?owner.hp/owner.maxHp*100:100,clock,nextWave,waves:waveCount,spawned:spawnCount,cancelled,deferred,alive:living.length,melee:living.filter(e=>e.type==='crawler').length,ranged:living.filter(e=>e.type==='spitter').length,wave:{melee:(profile?.baseMelee||0)*multiplier,ranged:(profile?.baseRanged||0)*multiplier},pending:pending.map(p=>({...p})),history:history.map(w=>({...w})),profiles};}
 return {tick,clear,draw,info};
}

 }
});
(()=>{
 const baseUpdate=update;update=function(dt){const before=t,result=baseUpdate(dt);if(t>before)VENOM_REINFORCEMENTS_V554.tick(t-before);else if(ended||!running)VENOM_REINFORCEMENTS_V554.clear();return result;};

// Dedicated VENOM resolution preserves BIO's existing stage resolver and motion.
function finishVenomStageEncounterV554(e){
 if(ended||stageFlowV526.result||!e||e.dead)return;
 const phase=e.venomPhaseV554||e.encounterPhaseV526||1,retreat=phase<3;

function mapNeighborsV562(i,fn){

 const all=mapRoadsV562.map(z=>{const points=[];if(z.enabled===false)return points;
  for(let y=Math.max(3,z.y-z.ry);y<=Math.min(1533,z.y+z.ry);y+=5)for(let x=Math.max(3,z.x-z.rx);x<=Math.min(861,z.x+z.rx);x+=5){
   const wx=x*MAP_SCALE_V562,wy=y*MAP_SCALE_V562;if(!mapInRoadV562(wx,wy,z)||!mapInBirthBandV562(wx,wy,z,radius)||!mapCircleClearV562(wx,wy,key))continue;

    if(!safe)continue;const k=mapNearestCellV562(previous.x,previous.y,mapNavV562.main),end=k>=0?mapPointV562(k):null;

}
function mapChooseDoorV562(o,r=o.r||8){

   for(const e of enemies){if(e===o||e.dead)continue;const dx=e.x-p.x,dy=e.y-p.y;if(Math.abs(dx)>110||Math.abs(dy)>110)continue;gap=Math.min(gap,Math.hypot(dx,dy)-r-e.r);}

}

{

  const route=e?.mapEntranceRouteV562;if(!route?.length||e.dead)return steer(e);
  while(route.length&&Math.hypot(route[0].x-e.x,route[0].y-e.y)<7)route.shift();
  if(!route.length){e.mapEntranceRouteV562=null;return steer(e);}
  const speed=Math.hypot(e.vx||0,e.vy||0);if(speed<1||e.burst>0||(e.stunned||0)>0)return steer(e);
  let target=route[0];for(let i=route.length-1;i>0;i--)if(!terrainHitV418(e.x,e.y,route[i].x,route[i].y,e.r+.3)){route.splice(0,i);target=route[0];break;}
  const dx=target.x-e.x,dy=target.y-e.y,len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;e.vx=dx/len*speed;e.vy=dy/len*speed;
 };
}

// Pure geometry and portable document format for the mobile map editor.
function createMapEditorCoreV562(metadata=null,gates=null){
 const META=Object.freeze(metadata||{format:'EVOLVE_COLLISION_EDIT',version:1,mapId:'EVOLVE_HUB_FACILITY_NE_V566',baseFieldSha256:'97186f5802079fc4adf85d445327ca5f43bda29894e8b8a1289a5429a36f9e71',reference:[864,1536],field:[1728,3072]});
 const DEFAULT_GATES=Object.freeze(gates||{"northwest":true,"north":true,"northeast":true,"west":true,"east":true,"southwest":true,"south":true,"southeast":true});
 function empty(){return {...META,reference:[...META.reference],field:[...META.field],operations:[],entrances:{...DEFAULT_GATES}};}
 function validate(input){
  if(!input||input.format!==META.format||input.version!==1||input.mapId!==META.mapId||input.baseFieldSha256!==META.baseFieldSha256)throw Error('이 맵의 수정 파일이 아니에요.');
  if(!Array.isArray(input.reference)||input.reference[0]!==864||input.reference[1]!==1536||!Array.isArray(input.field)||input.field[0]!==1728||input.field[1]!==3072)throw Error('맵 크기가 맞지 않아요.');
  if(!Array.isArray(input.operations)||input.operations.length>2048)throw Error('수정 횟수가 너무 많아요.');
  const result=empty();let total=0,length=0;
  for(const item of input.operations){
   if(!item||!['block','open','under'].includes(item.mode)||!Number.isFinite(item.radius)||item.radius<1||item.radius>96||!Array.isArray(item.points)||!item.points.length||item.points.length>4096)throw Error('칠하기 정보가 올바르지 않아요.');
   const points=[];
   for(const p of item.points){
    if(!Array.isArray(p)||p.length!==2||!Number.isFinite(p[0])||!Number.isFinite(p[1])||p[0]<0||p[0]>864||p[1]<0||p[1]>1536)throw Error('맵 밖의 좌표가 포함되어 있어요.');
    if(points.length)length+=Math.hypot(p[0]-points.at(-1)[0],p[1]-points.at(-1)[1]);
    points.push([p[0],p[1]]);
   }
   total+=points.length;if(total>120000||length>500000)throw Error('수정 파일이 너무 복잡해요.');
   result.operations.push({mode:item.mode,radius:item.radius,points});
  }
  if(input.entrances!=null){for(const id of Object.keys(DEFAULT_GATES)){if(typeof input.entrances[id]!=='boolean')throw Error('진입로 정보가 올바르지 않아요.');result.entrances[id]=input.entrances[id];}}
  return result;
 }
 function parse(text){if(typeof text!=='string'||text.length>5*1024*1024)throw Error('수정 파일 크기를 확인해 주세요.');let doc;try{doc=JSON.parse(text);}catch(_){throw Error('수정 파일을 읽지 못했어요.');}return validate(doc);}
 function key(doc){return JSON.stringify([doc.operations,doc.entrances]);}
 function baseMask(field){const m=new Uint8Array(field.length);for(let i=0;i<m.length;i++)m[i]=field[i]<0?1:0;return m;}
 function paintStroke(mask,w,h,stroke,scale=2){
  const radius=stroke.radius*scale,value=stroke.mode==='block'?1:0;
  let minX=w,minY=h,maxX=-1,maxY=-1;
  function stamp(rx,ry){
   const cx=rx*scale,cy=ry*scale,y0=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(cy-radius)),y1=Math.min(h-1,Math.floor(cy+radius));
   for(let y=y0;y<=y1;y++){
    const span=Math.sqrt(Math.max(0,radius*radius-(y-cy)**2)),l=Math.max(0,Math.ceil(cx-span)),r=Math.min(w-1,Math.floor(cx+span));
    if(l>r)continue;mask.fill(value,y*w+l,y*w+r+1);minX=Math.min(minX,l);maxX=Math.max(maxX,r);minY=Math.min(minY,y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,y);
   }
  }
  const points=stroke.points;stamp(points[0][0],points[0][1]);
  for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++){
   const a=points[i-1],b=points[i],n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1])/Math.max(.5,stroke.radius*.30)));
   for(let j=1;j<=n;j++)stamp(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*j/n,a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*j/n);
  }
  return maxX>=0?{x:Math.max(0,minX-2),y:Math.max(0,minY-2),r:Math.min(w,maxX+3),b:Math.min(h,maxY+3)}:null;
 }
 async function compose(base,operations,w=1728,h=3072,yieldTask=async()=>{}){const mask=base.slice();for(let i=0;i<operations.length;i++){paintStroke(mask,w,h,operations[i]);if(i%8===7)await yieldTask();}return mask;}

 // Exact separable Euclidean distance transform. The function is self-contained
 // so the same implementation can run in a Blob worker or a time-sliced fallback.
 async function signedField(mask,w,h,progress=()=>{},yieldTask=async()=>{}){
  if(!(mask instanceof Uint8Array)||mask.length!==w*h)throw Error('Invalid collision mask');
  const n=w*h,tmp=new Float32Array(n),output=new Int16Array(n),v=new Int32Array(h),z=new Float64Array(h+1),f=new Float64Array(h),INF=1e12;
  for(let target=1;target>=0;target--){
   for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
    const off=y*w;let last=-1e6;
    for(let x=0;x<w;x++){if(mask[off+x]===target)last=x;const d=x-last;tmp[off+x]=Math.min(INF,d*d);}
    last=1e6;
    for(let x=w-1;x>=0;x--){if(mask[off+x]===target)last=x;const d=last-x;if(d*d<tmp[off+x])tmp[off+x]=d*d;}
    if(y%128===127){progress((1-target)*.5+.12*y/h);await yieldTask();}
   }
   for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    for(let y=0;y<h;y++)f[y]=tmp[y*w+x];
    let k=0;v[0]=0;z[0]=-Infinity;z[1]=Infinity;
    for(let q=1;q<h;q++){
     let s=(f[q]+q*q-f[v[k]]-v[k]*v[k])/(2*(q-v[k]));
     while(s<=z[k]&&k>0){k--;s=(f[q]+q*q-f[v[k]]-v[k]*v[k])/(2*(q-v[k]));}
     k++;v[k]=q;z[k]=s;z[k+1]=Infinity;
    }
    k=0;
    for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
     while(z[k+1]<y)k++;
     const i=y*w+x;if(mask[i]===target)continue;
     const distance=Math.sqrt((y-v[k])**2+f[v[k]]),raw=Math.round((distance-.5)*8)*(target===1?1:-1);
     output[i]=Math.max(-32768,Math.min(32767,raw));
    }
    if(x%24===23){progress((1-target)*.5+.12+.38*x/w);await yieldTask();}
   }
  }
  progress(1);return output;
 }
 function extraGeometry(baseField,mask,w=1728,h=3072){
  const size=96,cols=Math.ceil(w/size),boxes=new Map();
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++)for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
   const i=y*w+x;if(!mask[i]||baseField[i]<0)continue;
   const key=(y/size|0)*cols+(x/size|0);let box=boxes.get(key);
   if(!box){box={l:x,r:x,t:y,b:y};boxes.set(key,box);}else{box.l=Math.min(box.l,x);box.r=Math.max(box.r,x);box.t=Math.min(box.t,y);box.b=Math.max(box.b,y);}
  }
  return [...boxes].map(([id,b])=>({id:'paint_'+id,label:'직접 칠한 이동불가 구역',broadPhaseOnly:true,points:[[b.l/2,b.t/2],[(b.r+1)/2,b.t/2],[(b.r+1)/2,(b.b+1)/2],[b.l/2,(b.b+1)/2]]}));

}

function mapUnderMaskV575(doc,x,y,w,h,density=1){
 const first=doc.operations.findIndex(o=>o.mode==='under');if(first<0)return null;
 const c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(w*density));c.height=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(h*density));

  const q=doc.operations[i];g.globalCompositeOperation=q.mode==='under'?'source-over':'destination-out';g.lineWidth=q.radius*2;
  g.beginPath();g.moveTo(...q.points[0]);for(let j=1;j<q.points.length;j++)g.lineTo(...q.points[j]);g.stroke();
  g.beginPath();g.arc(...q.points[0],q.radius,0,Math.PI*2);g.fill();
 }

}
function mapUnderBuildV575(doc){

}

function campaignBossPlanV567(radius=76,preferred=null){

  while(dist>0&&terrainHitV418(x,y,x+dx/len*dist,y+dy/len*dist,radius+2))dist-=6;

}

function clarityKernelV574(radius){

 const offsets=[],limit=Math.ceil(r+.65);

  if(!x&&!y)continue;const weight=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,r+.65-Math.hypot(x,y)));if(weight)offsets.push({x,y,weight});
 }

}
function clarityEdgePixelsV574(pixels,w,h,regions,color){
 const p=pixels.data,alpha=new Uint8Array(w*h);for(let i=0;i<alpha.length;i++)alpha[i]=p[i*4+3];let changed=0;
 for(const region of regions){
  const x0=Math.max(0,region.x|0),y0=Math.max(0,region.y|0),x1=Math.min(w,Math.ceil(region.x+region.w)),y1=Math.min(h,Math.ceil(region.y+region.h)),offsets=clarityKernelV574(region.radius);
  for(let y=y0;y<y1;y++)for(let x=x0;x<x1;x++){
   const index=y*w+x,a=alpha[index]/255;if(a===1)continue;let cover=0;
   for(const o of offsets){const xx=x+o.x,yy=y+o.y;if(xx<x0||xx>=x1||yy<y0||yy>=y1)continue;cover=Math.max(cover,alpha[yy*w+xx]*o.weight);if(cover>=255)break;}
   const edge=cover/255*CLARITY_V574.edgeOpacity*(1-a);if(edge<.001)continue;
   const total=a+edge,i=index*4;for(let k=0;k<3;k++)p[i+k]=Math.round((p[i+k]*a+color[k]*edge)/total);p[i+3]=Math.round(total*255);changed++;
  }
 }
 return changed;
}

// V575: quiet walking-floor detail, baked into the existing native-size map cache.
const FLOOR_V575={enabled:true,detailRetention:.42,broadContrast:.84,exposure:.94,pivot:62,blend:.92,blurRadius:4,edgeStart:3,edgeEnd:11,chromaStart:.22,chromaEnd:.58};
const floorStatsV575={builds:0,pixels:0,buildMs:0,maxBuildMs:0,failures:[]};
let clarityMapFieldV575=null;
function floorSmoothV575(v,lo,hi){const q=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(v-lo)/(hi-lo)));return q*q*(3-2*q);}
function floorRectV575(source){
 if(source===mapImageV562)return {x:0,y:0,w:864,h:1536};
 if(source===mapNePatchLayerV566)return MAP_NE_PATCH_V566;
 return mapTilesV562.find(q=>q.image===source)||null;
}
function clarityFloorApplyV575(g,c,source){
 if(!FLOOR_V575.enabled||mapFieldV562.length!==mapFieldWidthV562*mapFieldHeightV562)return;
 const rect=floorRectV575(source);if(!rect)return;const started=performance.now(),w=c.width,h=c.height;
 try{
  const image=g.getImageData(0,0,w,h),p=image.data,horizontal=new Float32Array(w*h),columns=new Float64Array(w),fieldX=new Int32Array(w);
  const rx=Math.max(1,Math.round(FLOOR_V575.blurRadius*w/rect.w)),ry=Math.max(1,Math.round(FLOOR_V575.blurRadius*h/rect.h)),wx=rx*2+1,wy=ry*2+1;
  // Separable local luminance average. No image is resized or re-encoded.
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
   const row=y*w;let sum=0;
   const luma=x=>{const i=(row+Math.max(0,Math.min(w-1,x)))*4;return p[i]*.2126+p[i+1]*.7152+p[i+2]*.0722;};
   for(let x=-rx;x<=rx;x++)sum+=luma(x);
   for(let x=0;x<w;x++){horizontal[row+x]=sum/wx;sum+=luma(x+rx+1)-luma(x-rx);}
  }
  for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
   fieldX[x]=Math.max(0,Math.min(mapFieldWidthV562-1,Math.floor((rect.x+(x+.5)/w*rect.w)*2)));
   let sum=0;for(let y=-ry;y<=ry;y++)sum+=horizontal[Math.max(0,Math.min(h-1,y))*w+x];columns[x]=sum;
  }
  let changed=0;
  for(let y=0;y<h;y++){
   const row=y*w,fieldRow=Math.max(0,Math.min(mapFieldHeightV562-1,Math.floor((rect.y+(y+.5)/h*rect.h)*2)))*mapFieldWidthV562;
   const add=Math.min(h-1,y+ry+1)*w,remove=Math.max(0,y-ry)*w;
   for(let x=0;x<w;x++){
    const mean=columns[x]/wy;columns[x]+=horizontal[add+x]-horizontal[remove+x];
    const i=(row+x)*4,originalAlpha=p[i+3];p[i+3]=0;if(originalAlpha<8)continue;
    const distance=mapFieldV562[fieldRow+fieldX[x]]/16;if(distance<=FLOOR_V575.edgeStart)continue;
    const r=p[i],green=p[i+1],b=p[i+2],hi=Math.max(r,green,b),lo=Math.min(r,green,b),chroma=(hi-lo)/Math.max(32,hi);
    const mix=FLOOR_V575.blend*floorSmoothV575(distance,FLOOR_V575.edgeStart,FLOOR_V575.edgeEnd)*(1-floorSmoothV575(chroma,FLOOR_V575.chromaStart,FLOOR_V575.chromaEnd));if(mix<.005)continue;
    const luminance=r*.2126+green*.7152+b*.0722;
    const target=(FLOOR_V575.pivot+(mean-FLOOR_V575.pivot)*FLOOR_V575.broadContrast+(luminance-mean)*FLOOR_V575.detailRetention)*FLOOR_V575.exposure;
    p[i]=r+(target+(r-luminance)*.88-r)*mix;
    p[i+1]=green+(target+(green-luminance)*.88-green)*mix;
    p[i+2]=b+(target+(b-luminance)*.88-b)*mix;
    // Opaque paint under source-atop retains the original destination alpha.
    p[i+3]=255;
    changed++;
   }
  }
  // Paint changed floor pixels only. A full read/put round trip can slightly
  // recolor unrelated semitransparent HD tile edges through alpha rounding.
  // A 64-row scratch canvas bounds the extra surface to under 0.5 MiB.
  const strip=document.createElement('canvas');strip.width=w;strip.height=64;const sg=strip.getContext('2d');
  g.save();g.globalCompositeOperation='source-atop';
  try{for(let y=0;y<h;y+=64){const rows=Math.min(64,h-y),part=new ImageData(p.subarray(y*w*4,(y+rows)*w*4),w,rows);sg.putImageData(part,0,0);g.drawImage(strip,0,0,w,rows,0,y,w,rows);}}finally{g.restore();strip.width=strip.height=1;}
  const elapsed=performance.now()-started;
  floorStatsV575.builds++;floorStatsV575.pixels+=changed;floorStatsV575.buildMs+=elapsed;floorStatsV575.maxBuildMs=Math.max(floorStatsV575.maxBuildMs,elapsed);
 }catch(error){floorStatsV575.failures.push(String(error?.message||error));}
}
window.__EVOLVE_TEST_MODULE_V575__=window.__EVOLVE_TEST_MODULE_V574__;
window.__EVOLVE_CLARITY_V575__={read:()=>({...clarityReadV574(),version:575,floor:{...FLOOR_V575},floorStats:{...floorStatsV575}})};
if(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('evolveQA')==='1')window.__EVOLVE_CLARITY_V575__.test={
 ...window.__EVOLVE_CLARITY_V574__.test,
 setFloorEnabled(value){FLOOR_V575.enabled=!!value;clarityClearMapV574();clarityWarmMapV574();frozenFrameDrawnV438=false;},
 rect:floorRectV575
};

// Accepted LIGHTNING06 behavior, adapted to the current V575 campaign engine.
// The host retains boss damage, phases, terrain, rewards, saves and stage flow.
function createCampaignLightningV576(host){
 const clamp=(v,a,b)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,v)),tau=Math.PI*2;
 let queue=[],arcs=[],blasts=[],fields=[],cast=null,auto=.22,clock=0,serial=0;
 let metrics={casts:0,contacts:0,strongContacts:0,blockedDamage:0};
 const enemies=()=>host.enemies(),player=()=>host.player();
 function reset(){for(const e of enemies())delete e.arcShockV576;queue=[];arcs=[];blasts=[];fields=[];cast=null;auto=.22;clock=0;serial=0;metrics={casts:0,contacts:0,strongContacts:0,blockedDamage:0}}
 function valid(e){return e&&!e.dead&&!e.arrivalInvuln&&e.hp>0}
 function castChain(strong=false){
  if(!host.active()||(!strong&&cast&&cast.age<.24))return false;
  const p=player(),range=host.range(),pool=enemies().filter(e=>valid(e)&&Math.hypot(e.x-p.x,e.y-p.y)<=range);
  const reachable=pool.filter(e=>host.canLink(p,e));
  if(!reachable.length)return false;
  const priority=host.focus(),first=reachable.includes(priority)?priority:reachable.reduce((best,e)=>{const score=q=>Math.hypot(q.x-p.x,q.y-p.y)*(q.finalBoss?.65:q.elite?.8:1);return !best||score(e)<score(best)?e:best},null);
  const a=Math.atan2(first.y-p.y,first.x-p.x),id=++serial,lead=strong?.125:.085,visited=new Set(),hops=host.hops()+(strong?3:0),damage=host.damage(strong);
  cast={age:0,life:strong?.44:.34,strong,a,id};host.face(a);let e=first,from=null;
  for(let i=0;i<hops&&e;i++){
   visited.add(e);queue.push({delay:lead+i*(strong?.032:.044),target:e,from,strong,primary:i===0,id,damage:damage*Math.pow(.96,i)});from=e;
   e=pool.filter(x=>!visited.has(x)&&Math.hypot(x.x-from.x,x.y-from.y)<(strong?225:190)&&host.canLink(from,x)).sort((x,y)=>Math.hypot(x.x-from.x,x.y-from.y)-Math.hypot(y.x-from.x,y.y-from.y))[0];
  }
  queue=queue.slice(-64);metrics.casts++;return true;
 }
 function step(dt){
  if(!host.active()||!(dt>0))return;
  clock+=dt;if(cast){cast.age+=dt;if(cast.age>=cast.life)cast=null}
  for(const e of enemies()){
   const s=e.arcShockV576;if(!s)continue;s.age+=dt;s.wait=Math.max(0,s.wait-dt);
   if(s.wait<=0){s.hp+=(e.hp/Math.max(1,e.maxHp)-s.hp)*(1-Math.exp(-dt*11));if(Math.abs(s.hp-e.hp/e.maxHp)<.003)s.hp=e.hp/e.maxHp}
   if(s.age>=s.life&&Math.abs(s.hp-e.hp/e.maxHp)<.004)delete e.arcShockV576;
  }
  for(const a of arcs)a.age+=dt;arcs=arcs.filter(a=>a.age<a.life);
  for(const b of blasts)b.age+=dt;blasts=blasts.filter(b=>b.age<b.life);
  for(const f of fields)f.age+=dt;fields=fields.filter(f=>f.age<f.life);
  auto-=dt;if(auto<=0)auto=castChain(false)?host.interval():.12;
  for(const q of queue){
   q.delay-=dt;if(q.delay>0)continue;q.done=true;const e=q.target;if(!valid(e))continue;
   const p=player(),a=cast?.a??p.look??0,source=q.from||p;if(!host.canLink(source,e))continue;const from=q.from?{x:q.from.x,y:q.from.y-5}:{x:p.x+Math.cos(a)*20,y:p.y-12+Math.sin(a)*8},to={x:e.x,y:e.y-5},angle=Math.atan2(to.y-from.y,to.x-from.x);
   const mass=e.finalBoss?.12:e.elite?.36:e.type==='brute'?.58:1;
   const old=e.arcShockV576;e.arcShockV576={age:0,hold:q.strong?.058:.034,life:q.strong?.32:.23,a:angle,strong:q.strong,mass,hp:Math.max(old?.hp||0,e.hp/Math.max(1,e.maxHp)),wait:.13,vx:e.vx||0,vy:e.vy||0};
   e.kx=(e.kx||0)+Math.cos(angle)*(q.strong?285:142)*mass;e.ky=(e.ky||0)+Math.sin(angle)*(q.strong?285:142)*mass;
   host.damageEnemy(e,q.damage,q.strong,angle);metrics.contacts++;if(q.strong)metrics.strongContacts++;
   arcs.push({from,to,strong:q.strong,age:0,life:q.strong?.25:.18,seed:metrics.contacts});
   blasts.push({x:to.x,y:to.y,strong:q.strong,age:0,life:.32,column:q.primary&&q.strong});
   if(q.primary&&q.strong){fields.push({x:p.x,y:p.y,age:0,life:.9,size:host.fieldSize()});host.kick(angle)}
  }
  queue=queue.filter(q=>!q.done);arcs=arcs.slice(-32);blasts=blasts.slice(-24);fields=fields.slice(-3);
 }
 function pose(e){const s=e.arcShockV576;if(!s||s.age>=s.life)return null;const p=clamp((s.age-s.hold)/(s.life-s.hold),0,1),envelope=s.age<s.hold?1:Math.cos(p*Math.PI*.5)**2,force=envelope*s.mass*(s.strong?1:.6);return {bend:Math.cos(s.a)*.24*force,sx:1+.045*force,sy:1-.12*force,fade:1-s.age/s.life,flash:Math.max(0,1-s.age/(s.strong?.065:.045)),angle:s.a,strong:s.strong}}
 function holdEnemy(e){const s=e.arcShockV576;return !!s&&s.age<s.hold}
 function sprite(ctx,cell,x,y,w,h,rotation=0,alpha=1){const im=host.texture();if(!im||alpha<=0)return;const sw=im.naturalWidth||im.width,sh=im.naturalHeight||im.height;if(!sw||!sh)return;ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.rotate(rotation);ctx.globalAlpha=clamp(alpha,0,1);ctx.drawImage(im,(cell%2)*sw/2+2,Math.floor(cell/2)*sh/2+2,sw/2-4,sh/2-4,-w/2,-h/2,w,h);ctx.restore()}
 function drawGround(ctx){for(const f of fields){const p=f.age/f.life,envelope=Math.min(1,p*7)*Math.pow(1-p,.7),s=f.size*(.66+p*.58);sprite(ctx,1,f.x,f.y+5,s,s*.72,p*.18,envelope*.78)}}
 function draw(ctx){
  ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
  for(const a of arcs){const p=1-a.age/a.life,dx=a.to.x-a.from.x,dy=a.to.y-a.from.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,angle=Math.atan2(dy,dx);sprite(ctx,0,(a.from.x+a.to.x)/2,(a.from.y+a.to.y)/2,d+28,clamp(d*.58,a.strong?100:73,a.strong?170:120),angle,Math.pow(p,.55)*(a.strong?.92:.76));
   ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';const steps=Math.max(5,Math.ceil(d/12)),points=[];for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){const q=i/steps,j=i===0||i===steps?0:Math.sin(i*17.71+a.seed*8.13)*Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*(a.strong?13:8);points.push([a.from.x+dx*q-dy/d*j,a.from.y+dy*q+dx/d*j])}
   for(const [width,color,alpha] of [[a.strong?12:8,'#287cff',.36],[a.strong?6.5:4.2,'#5cddff',.92],[a.strong?2.8:1.9,'#f4ffff',1]]){ctx.globalAlpha=p*alpha;ctx.strokeStyle=color;ctx.lineWidth=width;ctx.beginPath();points.forEach(([x,y],i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(x,y):ctx.moveTo(x,y));ctx.stroke()}ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
  }
  for(const b of blasts){const p=b.age/b.life,envelope=Math.min(1,p*12)*Math.pow(1-p,.75),size=(b.strong?125:70)*(.58+p*.72);if(b.column&&b.age<.22)sprite(ctx,0,b.x,b.y-110,220,100,Math.PI/2,Math.sin(Math.PI*b.age/.22)*.62);sprite(ctx,1,b.x,b.y,size,size*.78,0,envelope*.78);ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=(1-p)**2*.8;ctx.strokeStyle='#b8ecff';ctx.lineWidth=1.4;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(b.x,b.y+8,size*.5,size*.18,0,0,tau);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
  if(cast){const p=player(),lead=cast.strong?.125:.085,charge=clamp(cast.age/lead,0,1),alpha=cast.age<lead?charge:Math.max(0,1-(cast.age-lead)/.13),x=p.x+Math.cos(cast.a)*20,y=p.y-12+Math.sin(cast.a)*8;ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';ctx.globalAlpha=alpha*.9;ctx.strokeStyle='#d4faff';ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,5+(1-charge)*16,clock*4,clock*4+tau*.85);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
  ctx.restore();
 }
 function overlay(ctx,e,size){const p=pose(e),s=e.arcShockV576;if(!s||e.dead)return;const w=e.elite?42:Math.max(22,Math.min(36,size*.6)),y=e.y-size*.63;if(e.hp<e.maxHp){ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle='#05101cdd';ctx.fillRect(e.x-w/2-1,y-1,w+2,5);ctx.fillStyle='#eaffff';ctx.fillRect(e.x-w/2,y,w*clamp(s.hp,0,1),3);ctx.fillStyle='#48d9ff';ctx.fillRect(e.x-w/2,y,w*clamp(e.hp/e.maxHp,0,1),3);ctx.restore()}if(!p)return;ctx.save();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='lighter';ctx.globalAlpha=p.fade*.86;ctx.strokeStyle=p.flash>.2?'#ffffff':'#8fe7ff';ctx.lineWidth=p.strong?1.8:1.1;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(e.x-size*.28,e.y-size*.22);ctx.lineTo(e.x+size*.07,e.y-size*.10);ctx.lineTo(e.x-size*.11,e.y+size*.02);ctx.lineTo(e.x+size*.25,e.y+size*.13);ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
 return {reset,step,castChain,pose,holdEnemy,drawGround,draw,overlay,block:()=>{metrics.blockedDamage++},cast:()=>cast,read:()=>({version:'V575-WEB-9-LIGHTNING06',weapon:'lightning',...metrics,queued:queue.length,arcs:arcs.length,fields:fields.length,cast:cast?{...cast}:null,reacting:enemies().filter(e=>!!pose(e)).length}),context:()=>({queue,arcs,blasts,fields})};
}
